- Project-wide find and replace (F-10.2): `src/shared/replace.ts` owns the options (`ReplaceOptions { query, replacement, matchCase, wholeWord }`), the limits (`REPLACE_QUERY_MAX` 200, `REPLACEMENT_MAX` 1000, `REPLACE_MAX_DOCUMENTS` 500, `REPLACE_SAMPLES` 5), the zod shapes (`ReplaceRequest` = options + `scopeId`, `ReplacePreview { items { id, title, location, count, samples { before, after: { text, range } } }, total (matching documents before the cap), truncated }`, `ReplaceCommitRequest` = request + `ids`, `ReplaceCommitResult { changed { id, count, wordCount }, total (occurrences) }`, `ReplaceUndoResult { restored { id, wordCount }, skipped }`), and the pure functions over stored Tiptap JSON: `findInRun`, `countInDoc`, `samplesInDoc` (reuses `buildSnippet`), `replaceInDoc` (never mutates; a document with no match comes back as the same object). Matching rules, different from search on purpose: the query is NFC but otherwise as typed (not trimmed, no whitespace collapsing; empty finds nothing), `foldCase` unless Match case, quotes always straightened on both sides, Whole word = no `\p{L}\p{M}\p{N}_` on either side, non-overlapping left to right (a refused whole-word candidate resumes one character later), the replacement is literal and never rescanned. A match lives in one *run* — the adjacent text nodes of one parent — so a block boundary, a hard break, and an inline tag token are all boundaries and replacing never merges paragraphs or eats a token. Marks: the replacement takes everything (marks, so provenance too) of the node holding the match's first character; remainders keep theirs; emptied nodes are dropped, pieces from the same source node are joined, an emptied block loses its `content` key. Main `src/main/search/replaceStore.ts`: `previewReplace` (documents only — every `kind === 'document'` row, never notes, entities, or titles — in tree order via `nodesInTreeOrder`/`nodeLocation`, both exported from `searchStore.ts`; `scopeId` = that node and its descendants, unknown = NOT_FOUND; a corrupt or never-written document is skipped), `commitReplace(db, request, ids)` (one transaction; each document recomputed from its content as stored now, so a stale preview cannot write stale text; ids outside the scope skipped; writes through `saveDocument`, so word count and `modified` move as for a save), `undoReplace` (module-level undo entries `{ id, before, after }` of the last commit that changed anything; a document is restored only while its stored content is still exactly `after`, else it is named in `skipped`; spent once the transaction held), `clearReplaceUndo()` in `manager.onChange`. `handlers.ts`: channels `replace:preview`, `replace:commit`, `replace:undo`; `document:save`'s follow-up was extracted into `documentsWritten(db, ids)` (voice version bump once, then per id the gated summary touch and the mention touch, whose scan republishes proposed tags) and the commit and the undo call it once for the batch, after `clearSearchCache()`. Renderer `src/renderer/features/search/`: `SearchFrame.tsx` is the modal frame both dialogs share (title row, close button, Escape, backdrop; `SEARCH_INPUT_CLASS`); `replaceStore.ts` (`open`, the fields and options, `scope all | selection` with `scopeNode` captured from the tree selection on open, `preview`, `answered` — the request the preview answers, which is exactly what a commit sends — `excluded`, `status`, `busy`, `undoable`; typing debounced `REPLACE_DEBOUNCE_MS` 200, options ask at once, a generation counter drops overtaken answers; a preview, a commit, and an undo each `flushPendingSaves()` first; after a commit or an undo `refresh` merges the word counts into the tree and calls `documentStore.reload(ids)`; `reset` on project close; `resetReplaceStore()` in `afterEach` too); `ReplaceDialog.tsx` (`role="dialog"` `Replace in project`, `data-testid="replace-dialog"`, textboxes `Find` / `Replace with`, checkboxes `Match case` / `Whole word`, radiogroup `Replace in` only when something is selected, one `data-testid="replace-document"` group per document with a checkbox `Replace in <title>`, up to five `<del>`/`<ins>` sample lines and `+k more`, footer `Replace n occurrences in m documents` behind a `dialogs.confirm` titled `Replace across documents`, and `Undo last replace` while `undoable` — the toast API has no actions, so the undo lives in the footer). `autosaveStore.reload(ids)` is the one path that makes a mounted editor show a main-side rewrite: it waits for a write on the wire, drops the timers and pending drafts of the loaded ids (a draft of text main just rewrote must never be saved), and `load`s them again, which takes the record through loading and so rebuilds the `RegionEditor` (keyed on load state) — every region of a stack included; the rebuilt editor starts a fresh undo history. Openers: `APP_SHORTCUTS.replaceProject` (Ctrl+Shift+H, handled by `SearchShortcut` in `App.tsx`, which closes the other dialog), Edit › `replaceProject` `Replace in project…` (`when: 'project'`), and `Replace…` in the search dialog's header (hands its query over). E2E: after the F-10.1 block, storm → squall in Scene 1 (scoped to the selection), editor and `documentText` agree, Undo last replace restores both. Known gaps: no regex and no case-preserving replacement; a text stored in NFD is matched as stored against an NFC query; every changed document queues a summary job when summaries are on (the same cost a save has, times the documents changed); the undo is one level, in memory, and gone with the project or the next commit; the search dialog's last answer is not refreshed by a replace; with more than 500 matching documents the commit is done in rounds.
- Goals (F-10.3): `src/shared/goals.ts` owns `GOALS_KEY` (`goals`, a project `settings` row read and written by `getGoals`/`setGoals` in `settingsStore.ts`), the caps (`GOAL_TARGET_MAX` 10,000,000, `NODE_TARGETS_MAX` 5,000), `ACTIVE_GAP_MS` (5 min) and `PACE_MIN_ACTIVE_MS` (1 min), the zod `Goals` `{ projectTarget, deadline (YYYY-MM-DD), dailyTarget, nodeTargets: Record<nodeId, target> }` (every field defaulted), `GoalsPatch` (partial; `nodeTargets` as `{ nodeId, target|null }` changes), `GoalsStatus`, and the pure arithmetic both sides share (`applyGoalsPatch`, `localDay`/`localHour` on the local clock, `addDays`/`daysBetween` at local midday so DST never skips a day, `computeStreak` — consecutive days at the daily target ending today, or yesterday while today is under target, plus the best run —, `daysLeft` (today and the deadline counted), `perDayNeeded`, `wordsPerHour`, `addActiveTime`, `progressRatio`, `parseGoalTarget`/`goalTargetError` for typed targets with thousands separators). Words written live in `writing_log` (migration `0013_writing_log`: `(day, hour)` primary key, `words` signed net, `active_ms`), hourly so F-10.5 reads the same table. Main `src/main/goals/goalsStore.ts`: `manuscriptWordCount(db, id)` (the stored count of a document under the manuscript root, else null; content-free selects), `recordWriting(db, delta, now)` (upserts the bucket and the module-level session `{ words, activeMs, lastWriteAt }`; each counted save adds the gap since the previous one capped at 5 min; a zero save with no time writes no row), `resetGoalsSession()` in `manager.onChange`, `readGoals` (prunes node targets whose node is gone or outside the manuscript and rewrites the row), `updateGoals` (VALIDATION for a target on a non-manuscript node; clearing always works), `goalsStatus(db, now)` (manuscript words from the light node rows, today clamped at 0, streaks, pace, session). Only `document:save` counts: the handler reads `manuscriptWordCount` before `saveDocument` and records the delta after it, so import, replace (`documentsWritten` does not count), delete, move, duplicate, and matter documents never count. Channels `goals:get`, `goals:set` (answers the new status). Renderer `src/renderer/features/goals/`: `goalsStore.ts` (`status`, `open`, generation-guarded `load` from `App.tsx` with the other project stores, `clear` on close, `set(patch)` takes main's answer and toasts a refusal, `refreshSoon()` — a 400 ms debounced `goals:get` called from the document store's `onSaved` —, `show`/`close`; tests call `resetGoalsStore()` in `beforeEach` and `afterEach`), `goalsFixture.ts`, `GoalBar.tsx` (the one progressbar: dialog, strip, and tree rows; success color once met), `GoalsDialog.tsx` (`role="dialog"` `Goals`, `data-testid="goals-dialog"`; project target + date deadline + progress + pace line, daily target + today's bar + streak, session time/words/words per hour; Save sends only the changed fields, an empty field clears; reloads the status on open; mounted in `ProjectScreen`), `GoalsStrip.tsx` (in `StatusBar` before `CreditNotice`: `Today n / target`, `n-day streak`, a thin project bar; a click opens the dialog), `goalFormat.ts`. Tree: manuscript rows with a target show `NodeTarget` (`1,234 / 2,000` above an `@[18rem]` tree width, `formatCompactWords` `1.2k / 2k` below, a bar, a check once met; `data-testid="node-target"`); `treeContextMenuItems(index, id, format, hasTarget)` appends `Set word target…` (a `dialogs.prompt`) and `Clear word target` on manuscript documents and folders. Openers: Tools › `openGoals` `Goals…` (`when: 'project'`, leaves focus mode first) and the strip. E2E: after the Ctrl+S save of Scene 1 the strip reads the sentence's words, a daily target of 5 gives `Today 9 / 5` and a one-day streak, and a 2,000-word target on Scene 1 shows on its row, then is cleared. Known gaps: the session is per project open, not per app run; the strip shows only where an editor's status bar is; writing time counts only the gaps between saves, so a session of one save has none.
- Word count (F-10.4): `src/shared/wordCount.ts` owns `textStats(doc)` (words, characters, characters without whitespace; one walk, and `countWords` delegates to it so every count agrees; an inline tag is one word with its stored `name`'s characters; block boundaries add no characters), `addTextStats`, `WORDS_PER_PAGE` (250), `estimatedPages` (rounded up), and the zod `TextStats`/`WordCountReport`. Channel `stats:wordCount { nodeId | null }` → `{ chapter: { id, title, stats } | null, manuscript }`, answered by `src/main/document/wordCountReport.ts` from the stored documents (`manuscriptDocuments(db, rows)` now takes the rows it already read); the chapter is the nearest chapter-level node among `nodeId` and its ancestors, only under the manuscript. Renderer `src/renderer/features/stats/WordCountDialog.tsx` is a shell dialog (`shellDialogStore` id `wordCount`, rendered by `ShellDialogs` only with a project, so it opens over focus mode): it counts the selection (`doc.cut(from, to)`) and the document from the active editor when it opens, flushes the document store, then asks main with the active editor's id or the tree selection. Menu Tools › Word count… (`openWordCount`).
- Statistics (F-10.5): no migration, no AI. `src/shared/statsDashboard.ts` owns the zod `StatsDashboard` (`today`, `days` — only logged days in the last `STATS_LOG_DAYS` 371 —, `hours` [24], `scenes` `SceneLengthStats`, `pov` `PovRow[]`, `characters`/`settings` `AppearanceRow { tagId|null, name, scenes, mentions, povScenes? }`, `truncated`), the constants (`HEATMAP_WEEKS` 53, `WORDS_PER_DAY_DAYS` 30, `STATS_ROWS_MAX` 50, `SCENE_LENGTH_BUCKETS` 0 / 1–499 / 500–999 / 1k–2k / 2k–3k / 3k–5k / 5k+), and the pure helpers (`nameKey` folds case and treats spaces and hyphens alike so the tag `mara-voss` matches the POV `Mara Voss`; `sceneLengthStats`, `tallyPov`, `heatmapGrid` Monday-first with future cells null, `heatThresholds`/`heatLevel` quartiles at the lower rank so the busiest day is darkest, `dailySeries`, `bestHour`, the formatters); day math reuses `addDays`/`daysBetween` from `goals.ts`. Main `src/main/stats/dashboardStore.ts` `statsDashboard(db, now)`: `listNodes` once + `manuscriptDocuments`, stored `word_count` and `scene_meta` only (never parses `content`); hours sum the whole log's positive buckets; a character/setting row counts a manuscript document once when the tag is linked or mentioned, or (characters) when the scene names it as POV (mentions summed from `tag_mention` in manuscript documents only), a setting also when the scene's `location` names it, an untagged location gets its own row; names come from the entity holding the tag, else the tag; sorted scenes, mentions, name. Channel `stats:dashboard`. Renderer: `StatsFrame.tsx` is the modal frame the word count and statistics dialogs share; `StatsDialog.tsx` is shell dialog `statistics` (Tools › `openStatistics` `Statistics…`, opens over focus mode), flushes the document store, then asks; panels `stats-heatmap` (`heat-cell` with `data-day`/`data-level`), `stats-daily`, `stats-hours`, `stats-scene-lengths`, `stats-pov`, `stats-characters`, `stats-settings`, each with its own empty state; bars are divs with a percent inline style, shades are the accent at opacities. Known gaps: the log only knows words saved from the editor since F-10.3 shipped, so older writing is not in the calendar; the e2e computes "today" in the test process, so a run across local midnight can fail.
- Tag filter (F-4.10): `documentTag:listAll` (`listAllDocumentTagLinks` in the main document-tag store: every link as `{ nodeId, tagId }`, inner join so a dangling link never shows, ordered by node id then tag name; a project-wide read that checks no target) feeds `useDocumentTagStore.loadAll()`, which rebuilds `tagIdsByNode` from the reply and blanks every previously loaded node that has no link left, so the chips, the tree filter, and the Tag Manager read one map; it is called on every filter pick and every detail open, never on project open. `tagFilter` (a tag id or null) lives on `useTreeStore` beside `collapsed` and resets in `load()`/`clear()`; readers treat an id missing from the bank (a deleted tag) as no filter. `src/renderer/features/manuscript/tagFilter.ts` `tagFilterView(index, tagIdsByNode, tagId)` is the pure view: `matches` in depth-first display order and `visible` = matches plus their ancestors (a matching folder's untagged children stay hidden). `TagFilterBar.tsx` (mounted by `ManuscriptTab` above the tree; renders nothing while the bank is empty) is a `Filter by tag` select with an `<optgroup>` per non-empty category, a count line (`N documents carry #name`), and a `Clear filter` button. Under a filter `ManuscriptTree` lists only `visible` rows, ignores `collapsed` (every visible folder is open, the chevron is a static icon, section clicks and the keyboard toggles are no-ops so no dead state is written), mutes the non-matching ancestor rows, and draws a tag-colored dot on each match; drag and drop stays on, since placement reads the full index. `TagDetail.tsx` calls `loadAll()` on open (toast on failure), lists the matches as button rows (title plus the parent folder's title, none for a section root) that `select` the node, and `Show in tree` sets the filter and `setSidebarTab('manuscript')`. Gotcha: the sidebar remounts `TagsTab` on every tab switch, so its detail view does not survive leaving the tab, and the smoke test's tag rows match `getByRole('button', { name: 'Back' })` by substring (`flashback`), so use `exact: true` for that button.
- Scene metadata (F-4.5): `src/shared/sceneMeta.ts` (zod `SceneMeta` `{ location, pov, timeline }`; since F-11.2 the Timeline field is a picker over the timeline events, see Timeline, `EMPTY_SCENE_META`, lenient `parseStoredSceneMeta`) stored as JSON in the existing `node.scene_meta` column, no migration. `src/main/tree/contentTarget.ts` `requireContentTarget(db, id, what)` is the one "any node but a section root" rule shared by notes, scene metadata, and document tags (so chapters and parts carry tags and metadata too). `src/main/document/sceneMetaStore.ts` behind `sceneMeta:get`/`sceneMeta:set`. Renderer: `sceneMetaStore.ts` is the third `createAutosaveStore` instance (debounce, flush, pending-save registration for free); `MetadataPane.tsx` (`role="group"` "Scene metadata") loads per node on mount and unloads on unmount, fields disabled until the load lands; `SuggestInput.tsx` is the combobox (label + input `role="combobox"`, listbox of names starting with the text, ArrowUp/Down, Enter fills, Escape/blur close; free text allowed) fed by the bank's setting tags (Location) and character tags (POV). Since 2026-10-06 the pane is the "Scene details" disclosure at the foot of the notes column (collapsed by default, mounted only while open, scrolling within half the column) and no longer carries the synopsis: `SynopsisBox` (same file) is the 4-line textarea at the top of the notes column for a node with a hierarchy level, holding the same `sceneMeta` record (holder-counted, so both share one read and one write). Tests that enumerate stacked document regions filter `getAllByRole('region')` to `<section>` elements, and the e2e uses `section[aria-label]`, because the tags panel is a region too.
- Scene brief (F-14.3), main side: `src/shared/sceneMeta.ts` adds `SceneBrief` `{ goal, conflict, turn, beat, after }` (each ≤ `SCENE_BRIEF_FIELD_MAX` 200) as `SceneMeta.brief`, zod-defaulted so a row stored before F-14.3 still parses (no migration), plus `SCENE_BRIEF_FIELDS`, `EMPTY_SCENE_BRIEF`, `emptySceneMeta()`, `isBriefEmpty`, `renderSceneBriefBlock({ current, previous, next })` — the one prompt block every feature carries: the scene's non-empty lines, then the previous scene's `after` (else its `turn`) and the next scene's `goal` — and `BRIEF_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 20,000 / `BRIEF_TEXT_MIN` 200. `src/main/document/sceneNeighbours.ts` `sceneBriefBlock(db, nodeId)` and `sceneNeighbours` read it over `manuscriptDocuments` (F-14.3 made it reading order: sorted by each row's chain of positions from the root, because `listNodes` groups by parent id, which is not reading order across chapters; the provenance and consistency reports gained the same order); a node that is not a manuscript document gets its own brief and no neighbours. Feature id `brief` (tier `fast`, JSON, `minDial` 1, budgets 200 out / 6,000 in); `FEATURE_INPUT_BUDGETS.ghostText` rose 1,500 → 2,000 to carry the block. Contract `ai:draftBrief { nodeId, requestId }` → `AiDraftBriefResult`. Main: `src/main/ai/prompts/brief.v1.ts` (`BRIEF_RULES` opens with `You are the scene-brief feature inside a novel-writing app.`, which the e2e fake server keys on; system = rules + the `Scene:` line, user = the scene text + `Draft the brief.`; no voice block), `src/main/ai/draftBrief.ts` `draftBrief` (dial gate first, the `BRIEF_TEXT_MIN` text gate, head cut to `BRIEF_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` → `truncated`, `fast` + `json`, hash over the scene text as sent and the metadata; `parseBriefAnswer` is lenient field by field — a missing or non-string line reads as '' — and PROVIDER when the answer is not a JSON object), handler `ai:draftBrief` (one pending `ai_proposal` row holding the brief as JSON; nothing is written to the node, the pane's Use draft writes the fields through `sceneMeta:set`). The block rides in `ghostText.v2`/`ghostTextRegen.v2`, `chat.v2`/`chatRegen.v2` (Agent mode), and `critique.v2`/`critiqueRegen.v2`; `rewrite.v1` is deliberately not wired, since a rewrite must keep the passage's meaning.
- Scene brief, renderer (F-14.3): `MetadataPane.tsx` grows a **Brief** disclosure (collapsed by default, `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) over the five `SCENE_BRIEF_FIELDS` inputs, each editing `meta.brief` through the same `sceneMetaStore.edit`, so the brief autosaves and flushes with the other three fields. The pane scrolls (`h-full overflow-y-auto`) because five more lines outgrow the bar; opening the brief grows the tag bar to `TAG_BAR_BRIEF_HEIGHT` (300 px) when it is shorter and never shrinks it, and the default tag-bar height rose 120 → 180 px so the three rows plus the Brief row fit collapsed with no scrollbar (`SuggestInput`'s listbox is fixed-positioned and re-measured on typing, scroll, and resize, because an `overflow-y: auto` ancestor clips an absolutely positioned list). `briefDraft.ts` (`useBriefDraft`) owns the Draft-with-AI flow for a document: the gate is the tag bar's (dial + toggle via `AI_DATA_SHARING.brief`, `BRIEF_TEXT_MIN` characters of live text from `useDocumentStore`, nothing in flight; the reason shows in the button's `title`), the request flushes the document store first and goes through `useAiActivityStore().track('brief', …)` so Cancel (F-5.10) and the header indicator find it, and the answer is a proposal (F-14.5) settled accepted on **Use draft** (which fills all five fields) or rejected on **Discard**; a `CANCELLED` reply returns to idle in silence. `BriefDraftPanel.tsx` renders the three states (pending + Cancel, failure + next step, the `role="group" "Brief draft"` list with `model · formatRequestCost · cached`). The e2e drafts a brief on Scene 1 before the VibeWrite step (fake server keys on `BRIEF_SENTINEL`) and then asserts the ghost-text and critique bodies carry the brief block. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): the brief draft is `features/editor/briefDraftStore.ts` (one draft app-wide, started by the assistant's Actions › Draft scene brief, accepted through `patchSceneMeta`), shown by `BriefDraftPanel` in the assistant's `AiResults`; the metadata pane has no Draft with AI button and the draft no longer opens the Brief disclosure.
- Outline (F-11.1): `SceneMeta` gains `synopsis` (≤ `SCENE_SYNOPSIS_MAX` 1000) and `status` (`SCENE_STATUSES` none/idea/draft/revised/final), both zod-defaulted (no migration, no new channel); main `setSceneMeta` takes the schema's input shape and parses. Prompt inputs and `summarize.ts`/`chatContext.ts` take `PromptSceneMeta` (location, pov, timeline, brief) and `draftBrief`'s hash picks those four keys, so neither field reaches the AI or a cache key. Status colours are tokens `--ms-status-*` in every theme block (`bg-status-*`); `features/outline/statusColors.ts` `STATUS_BG`, `status.tsx` `StatusDot`, `StatusSelect`, `AiSummaryLine` (the greyed F-5.6 summary behind an AI mark, shown only while the synopsis is empty, never copied). `MetadataPane` has Status and Synopsis. `CorkBoard.tsx`: a folder's direct children as cards (`ol` "Cork board", `li` per card named by title) (the folder's tags are in the tags column); each card holds its node in `useSceneMetaStore` (holder-counted, so the metadata pane and outline rows can hold the same id) and, for manuscript documents, `useSummaryStore`; native drag from the card header (before/after by horizontal half) and Alt+ArrowLeft/Right, both through `treeStore.move` via the pure `cardMove.ts` (`cardDropTarget` reuses the tree's `resolveDropTarget`, same parent only); an empty folder reuses `StackedEditor`'s exported `EmptyFolder`; `LevelIcon.tsx` now holds the level icon and colour for the tree and the cards. `outlineViewStore.ts` `folderView` `stacked | cork` is one in-memory choice for all folders (not persisted); `App.tsx` `MainPane` shows `FolderViewToggle` (`role="group"` "Folder view", two `aria-pressed` buttons) for folders outside focus mode, and focus mode always shows the stack. `OutlineTab.tsx` (sidebar tab `outline`, `ListTree`): the Manuscript section depth-first, always expanded, read-only, each row a status dot, a title button (`aria-current` on the active node), and the synopsis or AI fallback clamped to two lines, with a status-count header; one `sceneMeta:get` per node and one `summary:get` per document on mount, no batching. E2E: after the Chapter 1 stack check, the cork board step sets a synopsis and Draft on Opening, drags Scene 1 before it, restores the order with Alt+ArrowLeft, and ends on Stacked. Structure templates (F-11.1b): `src/shared/structure.ts` holds the three templates (`threeAct` 8 beats, `saveTheCat` 15, `herosJourney` 12; kebab-case beat ids, acts in order), `ProjectStructure { template | null }` under settings key `structure` (`structure:get`/`structure:set`, `settingsStore` `getProjectStructure`/`setProjectStructure`, missing or bad row reads as none; no migration), and the pure `groupByBeat`. The beat lives in `SceneMeta.beats` (template id → beat id, zod-defaulted `{}`, ids capped at 64, one beat per template so switching templates keeps every template's assignments; an id the template lacks reads as unassigned); `promptSceneMeta` leaves it out, so it never reaches a prompt or hash. Renderer: `features/outline/structureStore.ts` (loaded/cleared in `App.tsx`, writes at once, reverts and toasts on failure, generation-guarded); `OutlineTab` has a header with the `Structure` select and, while a template is set, an `Outline view` group (Outline/Beats, `outlineViewStore.outlineMode`, in memory); `BeatsView.tsx` `BeatBoard` holds every manuscript node's scene meta (holder-counted) and lists beats per act (`li[data-testid=beat][data-beat][data-empty]`, nodes as select buttons, "Empty beat"), "N of M beats filled", and "Not on a beat: K" (documents only); `listOutline` moved to `outlineRows.ts`. `MetadataPane` shows a `Beat` select (optgroups per act) after Status for manuscript nodes only while a template is set. E2E: after the cork step, the Outline tab picks Save the Cat, puts Opening on Catalyst from the metadata pane, checks the Beats view, then resets to None and the Manuscript tab. Plot threads (F-11.1c): `outlineViewStore.outlineMode` gains `threads`; the `Outline view` group is always shown (Outline, Threads, and Beats only while a template is set; a stored Beats choice without a template reads as Outline). `threadGrid.ts` `threadGrid(rows, isDocument, tagIdsByNode, threadIds)` is the pure grid (threads ordered by first appearance, unused split out, cells `on | gap | off`, gap = inside the first..last document span; per-thread `scenes`/`gaps`). `ThreadsView.tsx` `ThreadBoard` calls `useDocumentTagStore.loadAll()` on mount (toast on failure; `documentTag:changed` keeps it current), filters the bank to `plotThread`, and renders `table` "Plot threads" (sticky `bg-surface` title column, vertical thread headers, `tr[data-testid=thread-row][data-node]`, `td[data-state]` with an sr-only Yes/Gap/No, the tag colour as the one inline style), `thread-counts`, `thread-summary` rows, `thread-unused`; empty states for no plot-thread tag and no linked scene. E2E: after the Beats step, Opening gets main-plot from its tag bar, the Threads view shows it (the smoke project has several "Scene 1" documents, so `.first()`), and the chip is removed.
- Outline progress and plan links (F-11.1d; no migration; feature id `planLinks`, fast, JSON, Ask, 400 out / 7,000 in, prompt `planLinks.v1`): `OutlineTab` `OutlineList` computes each row's progress (`sceneProgress` from the tree's live `wordCount` and the held meta's status; folders `folderProgress` over the documents below them) → `li[data-progress]`, `outline-progress-badge`, and the `outline-progress` count line; the outline mirrors the binder because it reads `useTreeStore` (unit-tested with index swaps). `src/shared/planLinks.ts`: `PlanRef` (`scene` nodeId | `beat` template + beatId), `planKey`, `planLinkKey` (`scene:<id>>`+scene / `beat:<t>:<b>>`+scene), `PlanLinkSuggestion`, `PlanLinkState { suggestions, dismissed, aiApplied }` under settings key `planLinks` (`settingsStore` `getPlanLinkState`/`setPlanLinkState`, lenient), `PlanLinkView`, `PlanLinksRunResult`, caps (30 plans, 40 scenes, 240-char texts, 20 s debounce). A planned scene's link is `SceneMeta.fulfilledBy` (optional, ≤ 64, outside `promptSceneMeta`); a beat's link is the scene's `beats[template]`. Main `src/main/ai/planLinks.ts`: `runPlanLinks` (gate; open plans = empty manuscript documents not fulfilled + empty template beats; candidates = written scenes with a stored summary, unlinked first; no request when either is empty; `parsePlanLinksAnswer` drops unknown labels, dismissed pairs, a plan twice, a scene on two beats; re-validates after the request; chat mode Auto applies through `setSceneMeta` and records `aiApplied`, else stores suggestions replacing older ones for the same plans), `getPlanLinks` (only suggestions still valid), `confirmPlanLink` (NOT_FOUND / VALIDATION), `dismissPlanLink`, `unlinkPlan` (both remembered in `dismissed`). Handlers: the channels above; `planQueue` (`JobKind` `planLinks`, one job per project on the manuscript root, silent, failures dropped except cancel) is touched after a summary run that made a request and on `structure:set`, gated by `isFeatureAllowed`; it emits `planLinks:changed { changedNodeIds }`. Renderer: `features/outline/planLinksStore.ts` (loaded by `OutlineList`, cleared in `App.tsx`; writes flush scene meta first and reload the changed nodes, the timeline pattern; `run` tracked as `planLinks` with a toast of what it found), `FindLinksButton` (`outline-find-links`, shown while the feature is allowed), `PlanLinkLines` (`outline-fulfilled` with Unlink and an AI mark for `aiApplied`, `outline-fulfils`, `plan-suggestion` groups "Suggested link" with Confirm/Dismiss). E2E: the Plan links toggle is unchecked with the summaries toggle (background runs would break exact counts); after the Auto/Ask agent step it is turned on, Find links asks once (the fake links P1 to S1), Confirm shows "Fulfilled by", Unlink removes it, the toggle goes off. Known gap: a background link landing while the author edits that scene's metadata can be overwritten by the pending autosave (proposed again next run).
- Timeline (F-11.2): `src/shared/timeline.ts` owns the shape: `ProjectTimeline { events }` under settings key `timeline` (no migration; array order is story order, set by the author), each `TimelineEvent { id, label (trimmed, non-empty), when (free text), year (integer or null, ±1e6), note }`, caps `TIMELINE_EVENTS_MAX` 500 / label 200 / when 100 / note 1000 / id 64; `timelineProblem` refuses duplicate ids and duplicate labels (folded with the entities' `toEntityNameKey`) inside the zod `superRefine`; `eventText` (`When: Label` or the label; `TIMELINE_EVENT_TEXT_MAX` stays under `SCENE_META_TIMELINE_MAX`), `eventForText` (exact match), and `addEvent`/`updateEvent`/`removeEvent`/`moveEvent` that answer the same array when nothing changes (`moveEvent` and the reference panel's `movePin` share `src/shared/listMove.ts` `moveItem`). A scene links through `SceneMeta.eventId` (optional, not defaulted; absent = unlinked, an id the timeline lacks reads as unlinked); `promptSceneMeta` leaves it out, so only the `timeline` text reaches a prompt or hash (a rename therefore marks linked summaries stale, as it should). Channels `timeline:get` (lenient `settingsStore` `getProjectTimeline`, missing or bad row = no events) and `timeline:set` → `{ timeline, changedNodeIds }` (`src/main/project/timelineStore.ts` `setProjectTimeline`: VALIDATION on a bad value; one transaction upserts the row and walks every node with stored scene meta: event gone → drop `eventId`, keep the text; `eventText` differs → rewrite `timeline`; rewritten rows get a new `modified`; unlinked nodes untouched). Renderer: `features/timeline/timelineStore.ts` (loaded/cleared in `App.tsx`, generation-guarded; `save` checks `timelineProblem` locally, then `useSceneMetaStore.flush()` → `timeline:set` → `useSceneMetaStore.reload(changedNodeIds)`, so open panes show the synced text and no queued autosave writes the old text back; reverts and toasts on failure). `MetadataPane`'s Timeline field is a `SuggestInput` over the events' `eventText` in story order: picking links (`eventId` set), any other text unlinks. `TimelineTab.tsx` (sidebar tab `timeline`, `CalendarRange`): `Timeline view` group (Events / Reading order, local state, not persisted); Events has the `Add event` form, `ol` "Timeline events" of `li[data-testid=timeline-event][data-event]` (aria-label = label, focusable; edit in place via the `Edit event` form, delete after `dialogs.confirm`, native drag and Alt+ArrowUp/Down to reorder), linked nodes per event in reading order, `data-warning` + "Year is before an earlier event", "No events yet.", `timeline-unplaced` "Not on the timeline: K" (documents only); Reading order lists the manuscript documents (`li[data-testid=timeline-row][data-node]`) with the event label or "—", a track dot positioned by event index (the one inline style), and `data-flashback` + "Earlier" when the event precedes the latest one read so far. Pure helpers in `timelineView.ts` (`linkedEventId`, `nodesByEvent`, `unplacedCount`, `yearWarnings`, `readingOrderRows`; `parseYear` moved to `src/shared/timeline.ts` in F-11.2b). Holding every manuscript node's scene meta is the shared `features/editor/useHeldSceneMeta.ts` (also `BeatBoard`), and node title buttons are `BeatsView.tsx`'s exported `NodeTitleButton`. Known gap: a metadata keystroke landing in the same instant as a timeline write can race the flush (the normal path is covered by flush-before-set). E2E: after the Threads step, the Timeline tab adds two events, Opening picks "Spring: The siege begins", a rename follows into the pane and the stored meta, Reading order lists Opening, deleting both keeps the text and drops the link, the field is cleared, back to Manuscript. Character ages (F-11.2b, no migration, no contract change): the birth year is the character sheet field `born` ("Born (story year)", one line; not an observed attribute; the CSV/JSON exchange gains the column through `ENTITY_FIELD_IDS`); `src/shared/timeline.ts` adds `parseYear` (whole numbers only, shared with the year input, so "Year 1170" is no year), `ageAt(born, year)` (negative = not born yet), and `characterBirthYear(entity)`. `timelineView.ts` adds `eventAges(events, linked, entities, tagIdsOf, povOf)` (per event with a year: characters with a birth year whose entity tag is on one of the event's nodes or whose name key is a node's POV; inline mentions are not consulted; sorted by name), `agesOnTimeline(events, born)`, and `ageText`. The Events view loads `documentTag:listAll` on mount and shows `data-testid=event-ages` "Ages: Mara 30, Tobin not born yet" on a card; `EntityEditor` shows, for a structured character with a whole-number Born (the draft, so it follows typing), `section` "Age on the timeline" (one `li` per event with a year, story order: label, when, "Year N", "age A" / "not born yet"; "No timeline event has a year yet." without any), and a hint while Born is filled but not a whole number. E2E: the siege gets year 1200, a new character Rowan born 1170 shows "age 30" on her page, Opening's POV "Rowan" lists "Ages: Rowan 30" on the siege's card, then the POV is cleared and Rowan deleted (her `rowan` tag stays in the bank). Appearance and location logs (F-11.2c, renderer only, no migration, no contract change): pure `features/timeline/usageLog.ts` — `namesEntity` (name key, never blank), `appearancesOf(entity, documentIds, tagIdsOf, mentionsIn, povOf)` (documents in the order given where the entity tag is linked, its name was scanned (F-4.12), or — characters only — the POV names it; `how { linked, mentions, pov }`), `scenesSetAt(name, documentIds, locationOf)` (Location by name key), `inStoryOrder(rows, events, eventOf)` (groups per event in story order, events without rows left out, the rest last under `event: null`), `appearsBy(tagIdsOf, mentionedIn, povOf)`, and `locationConflicts(events, linked, characters, appearsIn, locationOf)` (a character in two or more of an event's linked documents with different non-empty Locations; display only, nothing stored, not a continuity finding). `features/entities/UsageLog.tsx` on character and setting pages (after Age on the timeline) replaces the F-9.4 Scenes section there (decided 2026-10-05, two lists that disagree were a parallel implementation; world items keep Scenes): `Log order` group (Reading order / Story order, `aria-pressed`, component state), `section` "Appearances" ("In n scenes" / "Not in any scene yet"; `role="list"` "Appearances" over every document in tree order (`documentsInTreeOrder`, so links and mentions outside the manuscript still count, and the manuscript reads in reading order), POV checked on manuscript documents only; in story order one list "Appearances: <event>" per group, "Not on the timeline" last; rows are the F-9.4 `SceneRowButton` (folder, Tagged, `×n`, jump to the first mention) plus a "POV" badge), for settings `section` "Scenes set here" ("No scene is set here yet."), for characters `role="note"` `data-testid=location-conflict` "At <event>: Keep, Harbor". It loads through `useEntityUsage` and holds the manuscript documents' scene meta (`useHeldSceneMeta`). The Events view of `TimelineTab` loads `mention:listForNode` for every document on an event (refreshed by the store's `mention:changed` subscription) and shows `data-testid=event-conflict` "Rowan: Keep, Harbor" per character on the card. E2E: the F-9.4 Mara jump now runs from her Appearances list; with Rowan as Opening's POV, her page's Appearances lists Opening (POV) and, in story order, under The siege; the conflict is unit-tested only.
- Inline tags (F-4.6): `@tiptap/suggestion` (pinned to the core version) is the only addition. `src/renderer/features/editor/InlineTag.ts` owns the `inlineTag` inline atom (attrs `{ id, name }`, `marks: ''`, not draggable; `renderHTML` emits `<span data-inline-tag data-id data-name class="inline-tag">#name</span>` with `--tag-color` from the bank; `parseHTML` keeps copy/paste), `suggestItems(query)` (substring match plus a "Create #kebab" row unless an exact name exists), the `#` Suggestion plugin (inserts the node plus exactly one plain space, swallowing a following space, then `documentTagStore.add`), and `resyncInlineTags(view, byId)` (a DOM-only repaint on bank changes: no transaction, no dirty flag). `InlineTagSuggestList.tsx` is the `role="listbox"` popup mounted by the plugin's own floating-ui `mount` helper (follows scrolling, closes on outside click; Escape is the plugin's): ArrowUp/Down wrap, Tab and Enter pick, the highlight returns to the first row whenever the rows change (a stale index from a long list once clamped to "Create"), and a row is highlighted by pointer movement, not by appearing under a resting pointer. `buildExtensions({ inlineTagNodeId })`: only manuscript documents pass it, so notes never get the node. `src/shared/inlineTags.ts` `countInlineTags(doc)` feeds the tag bar's "Inline tags" list (documents only) and `wordCount` counts a token as one word. Right-click on a token opens the manuscript `ContextMenu` with Remove (node only; the link stays) and Open in Tag Manager (opens the sidebar, `setSidebarTab('tags')`, `useTagStore.requestSelection(id)`, which `TagsTab` consumes once and clears). Known gaps: Ctrl+Z after an insertion leaves the link; a token pasted into another document is counted there but not linked; Edit on the token menu is deferred.
- Granular tags (F-4.8): a tag on a stretch of text is the `tagRange` mark (`TAG_RANGE_MARK` in `src/shared/tagRanges.ts`, attrs `{ tagId }`, `excludes: ''` so different tags overlap, same-tag ranges merge as equal marks, `inclusive: false`, `spanning`), stored in the document JSON like `aiOrigin`; no table, no migration, no channel. `src/renderer/features/editor/TagRange.ts` owns the mark, the commands `setTagRange(tagId)` / `clearTagRanges()` (selection) / `clearTagRangesAt(pos)` (every range covering a point, whole), the Mod+Alt+T keymap (calls the `onTagSelection` option, threaded through `buildExtensions`; listed in `EDITOR_SHORTCUTS`), the pure `tagRunsOf` / `blockTagsOf` / `tagRangeDecorations`, and the decoration plugin: one 2px line per live tag under each run (inline `background-image` stack, `title` lists `#names`) and a gutter bar on each top-level block (`--range-colors`, `.tag-range-block::before` in `app.css`). Colours resolve through `tokenTag` (merge aliases; deleted tags paint nothing, the mark stays). The plugin view subscribes to `useTagStore` and dispatches a `refresh` meta (no doc change, `addToHistory: false`) when `byId`/`aliases` change and the document holds a range (never otherwise: with an unconditional refresh the e2e typewriter step typed at the document start 3 runs in 3, a stale ProseMirror selection written back over Chromium's Control+End). Registered with the manuscript extensions only (never notes), so `manuscriptSchema` parses it and the compiled preview prints a plain span. Entry points live in `DocumentEditor.tsx`: its `contextmenu` listener (after the inline-token check) opens Tag selection… / Clear tags in selection over a non-empty selection, or Clear tags here over a `[data-tag-range]` with none; the selection is snapshotted at the right button's `mousedown` (capture), so a word Chromium selects on the right-click itself (a misspelling) still reaches main's spelling menu. Tag selection… and the shortcut open `TagPicker` (now with optional `label` / `listLabel`) in a fixed wrapper, capture `from`/`to` on open, and link the tag through `documentTag:add`; clearing leaves the link. Export prints only known marks and `docToText` ignores marks, so prompts, hashes, counts, drafts, and snapshots never see ranges. Known gap: a right-click on a tagged word with no selection opens the range menu, so a misspelled tagged word loses the spelling menu.
- AI provider and keys (F-5.1): `src/shared/ai.ts` is the one owner of `AiProviderId` (`AI_PROVIDER_IDS = ['openai', 'cloud']` since F-15.4), `Tier`, `DEFAULT_MODELS` (`fast: gpt-5.4-mini`, `strong: gpt-5.4`; F-5.11 turns it into a setting), `maskKey`, the `AiErrorCode` taxonomy (`NO_KEY | INVALID_KEY | RATE_LIMIT | QUOTA | NETWORK | PROVIDER | BUDGET | DISABLED | CANCELLED | SIGNED_OUT | NO_CREDIT | CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE`), `AI_NEXT_STEP` copy, and the `AiStatus`/`AiTestConnectionResult` shapes. `src/main/ai/providers/types.ts` is the generic `Provider` interface (`complete`, `stream`, `testConnection`, results carry `usage`) plus the `AiProviderError` class per code (SDK error on `cause`, fixed copy in messages, never the key); `openai.ts` is the only file importing `openai` (`buildOpenAiProvider(key, { fetch? })`, Chat Completions with `max_completion_tokens` and JSON mode, `maxRetries: 0`, `testConnection` = `models.retrieve` on the fast model, `mapOpenAiError`: 401 → INVALID_KEY, 429 + `insufficient_quota` → QUOTA, other 429 → RATE_LIMIT, connection → NETWORK, else PROVIDER with the status). `src/main/ai/keyStore.ts` (`AiKeyStore`) keeps `<userData>/ai-keys.json` `{ version, keys: { openai: <base64 ciphertext> } }` via `safeStorage` (atomic write, lenient read; a corrupt file reads as no key); `encryption()` is `os | plain | none`. `src/main/index.ts` calls `safeStorage.setUsePlainTextEncryption(true)` on Linux so a keyring-less box (this WSL machine) stores the key obfuscated and the AI tab warns; Windows/macOS use the OS store. `src/main/ai/registry.ts` (`AiProviderRegistry.get()`) rebuilds the client only when the decrypted key changes. Channels `ai:getStatus` (`hasKey`, masked `hint`, `encryption`), `ai:setKey`, `ai:clearKey`, `ai:testConnection` (expected failures come back as data with `code` and `nextStep`; only unexpected errors throw). The plaintext key exists only in main. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/ai/aiStore.ts` (`load/setKey/clearKey/test`, `resetAiStore()`) and `AiSettingsTab.tsx`, registered as the AI tab in `SETTINGS_DIALOG_TABS`. The e2e runs a fake OpenAI on `127.0.0.1` through the SDK's `OPENAI_BASE_URL` (it echoes the requested model id); no test may reach api.openai.com. Tiers (F-5.11): the mapping is the app-state field `models` (`AiModels`, one `AiModelMap` per provider id as an explicit zod object whose `cloud` map is defaulted, so an app-state file written before F-15.4 still loads, defaulted as a whole from `defaultAiModels()` for older files; `TIER_USE` holds the per-tier hint copy, all in `src/shared/ai.ts`); the registry hands the cached provider a live `resolveModel(tier)` closure over `appState.get().models`, so `ai:setModels` applies on the next request without a rebuild; `AiStatus.models` carries the whole per-provider map (since F-15.4; the AI tab edits the active source's entry); the AI tab's Models group commits on blur/Enter (blank or unchanged writes nothing, a refused save reverts and toasts) and its Reset is disabled at the defaults. Feature code names a tier, never a model; `grep gpt-` should hit only `src/shared/ai.ts` and tests.
- AI billing slice B1 (2026-10-07; `docs/research/AI-BILLING-SPEC.md`, plan `plan-billing-spec.md`; no migration, additive contract): **OpenRouter** joins `AI_PROVIDER_IDS` (`openrouter`, appended) as the default own-key provider; `OwnKeyProvider` (`openrouter | openai`) is app-wide in `app-state.json` `ownKeyProvider` (absent until picked; `effectiveOwnKeyProvider` keeps an install that holds an OpenAI key on OpenAI), `AiProviderRegistry.ownKeyProvider()` is the one owner (handlers store/clear the key of the provider it names), `buildOwnKeyProvider` points the OpenAI adapter at `OPENROUTER_BASE_URL` with `openRouter: true` (attribution headers, `GET /key` for Test connection, 402 → QUOTA, its own 404 copy), `OPENROUTER_DEFAULT_MODELS` (`openai/gpt-5.4-mini`, `openai/gpt-5.4` until the author approves the proposal in `QUESTIONS.md`), `OPENROUTER_PRICING` beside `MODEL_PRICING` (kept apart so `CLOUD_RATES` does not list a model twice). Keys only in the OS keychain (S2): `AiKeyStore.canStoreProviderKey()` refuses the Linux plain-text fallback for provider keys (`NO_KEYCHAIN_MESSAGE`; the Cloud session still uses it until B3); `MYTHSCRIBE_E2E_PLAINTEXT_KEYS=1` is the e2e's escape hatch (xvfb has no keyring); `AiStatus.canStoreKey` carries the rule to the AI tab. Cached input (A4, R6): `CompletionUsage.cachedInputTokens` from `prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens` (`usageOf` in `openai.ts`), written to the ledger's existing `cached_tokens` column and priced at `ModelPrice.cachedInUsdPerM` (`priceFor`/`costOf`, 4th argument; `Provider.price` gained it too). Model choice (M8, R4): `src/shared/aiRouting.ts` owns `DEFAULT_ROUTING_TABLE` (the bundled Auto table: each feature's existing tier; continuity and edit passes absent, their callers pick per request; guarded by `prompts/routingTable.test.ts` against the catalogue), `AiRouting` (`all` tier or null, per-feature tiers; app state `routing`, read leniently), `resolveTier` (per task → all → Auto table → caller), and the hosted `CloudPricing` contract for `GET /pricing` (price table with cached price and multiplier, markup, packs, thresholds, routing, nullable per-word constants; the Worker side is slice B2). `runAiRequest` routes through `deps.routing` (bound in `buildAiRequestDeps`: the Cloud table merged over the bundled one only for `cloud`) and records the routed tier. `src/main/account/cloudPricing.ts` (`CloudPricingService`) fetches `/pricing` after a sign-in and at launch when signed in (hourly cache in app state `cloudPricing`, a failure keeps the cache); the Cloud adapter prices a listed model with `hostedPriceFor` (cost × (1 + markup), cached input at its price, ceil to a micro-dollar), else the bundled `cloudPriceFor`. Channels: `ai:setOwnKeyProvider`, `ai:getModelChoice`, `ai:setRouting`, `ai:usageHistory` (E7, `usageHistory` in `usageStore.ts`, paged by `USAGE_HISTORY_PAGE`). Renderer: `aiStore` `choice`/`history`, `ownKeyOf`/`providerOf`/`routedTier`; the AI tab has the key-provider picker, the keychain refusal, "Which model each task uses" (All tasks + per task), the usage history, and hides tokens on Cloud (C4: usage block, Cloud rate line, edit-pass estimate and report). Not done here: the per-answer cost line (`describeRequest`) and the Account tab still show tokens on Cloud (B3); no per-request model picker (none existed).
- AI request path (F-5.14): every AI feature calls `runAiRequest(deps, input)` in `src/main/ai/request.ts` (pure over injected deps; `buildAiRequestDeps` binds the open project's db, the registry, and app state). Order: clamp `maxTokens` to `FEATURE_BUDGETS[feature]`, refuse an input estimate over `FEATURE_INPUT_BUDGETS` with `BUDGET`, refuse when the day's tally would exceed the cap (a cap of 0 pauses AI, cached answers included), cache lookup by `cacheKey` (feature, prompt version, model, json mode, context hash, messages), provider call, `priceFor(model)` (unknown → cost 0, `priced: false`; `MODEL_PRICING` in `src/shared/ai.ts` is the one owner), ledger row, cache write with the 500-row eviction, tally update. A provider error is neither logged nor cached; a cache hit logs a zero-cost row with `cached = true`. Tables `ai_usage` and `ai_cache` (migration `0003_ai_usage`) in the project db via `usageStore.ts` and `cacheStore.ts`; the daily cap and today's tally (`AiUsageState`, local-day rollover in `dailyCap.ts`) live in app state so the cap holds across projects. `AiFeature` (`ghostText | tags | summary`; extend it when a feature lands), `estimateTokens` (chars/4), `DAILY_CAP_*`, and `AiUsageSummary` are in `src/shared/ai.ts`; `src/shared/aiThrottle.ts` `shouldTrigger` is the ghost-text trigger discipline for F-5.3. Channels `ai:usageSummary` (`today` and `session` are app-wide, `total`/`byFeature`/`recent` are this project's; the AI tab's Usage block says so) and `ai:setDailyCap`. Cost visibility (F-5.9): `src/main/ai/sessionUsage.ts` (`createSessionUsage`) is the in-memory tally for one run of the app, created once in `registerHandlers` and handed to every `buildAiRequestDeps` call site through the local `requestDeps(db)` helper (one object, or the tally splits); `runAiRequest` spends against it beside `dailyCap.spend` on both the live and the cache-hit path. `recentUsage(db, limit)` is the newest ledger rows (`AiUsageRecent`, `USAGE_RECENT_LIMIT` = 10 in `src/shared/ai.ts`), shown as the Usage block's Recent requests table. The per-request line under every AI answer is `describeRequest` in `src/renderer/features/ai/usageFormat.ts` (model · cost · tokens in/out · cached); each feature store keeps `usage` beside `costUsd`, and `ChatMessage.usage` (`src/shared/chat.ts`, nullable, defaulted) persists a turn's tokens — a turn written before F-5.9 reads null and shows no token part. The F-14.4 dial gate belongs to each caller, not to `runAiRequest`. 2026-10-07: `cacheable` never caches an empty answer or a JSON answer with `finishReason: 'length'`; results carry `finishReason` when the provider said; `prepare` resolves the reasoning mode (`resolveReasoning(routing, tier, model)`: `AiRouting.reasoning[tier]`, else `bundledReasoning` from the price tables; `default` for Cloud and without routing) and `CompletionRequest.reasoning` carries it when not `default`; the OpenRouter adapter sends `reasoningParam(mode)` as `reasoning`, others ignore it; the trace's `prepared` reports it (`DevAiRequest.reasoning`); `DevToolsService.annotate(requestId, note)` (also `devtools:aiNote` from the window) appends notes to the newest row of an id or a suffixed step. The Worker: `HostedModelPrice.reasoning` (optional) → `UpstreamParams.reasoning`, sent by `openRouterUpstream` only.
- Prompt versions and the eval harness (F-5.12): `src/main/ai/prompts/catalogue.ts` lists every shipped `<feature>.v<N>.ts` (`PROMPT_VERSIONS`, `PromptVersion`, `PROMPT_CATALOGUE` → feature, tier, `json | text`, the shipping feature); `runAiRequest` takes a `PromptVersion`, not a string, so a feature cannot send an uncatalogued prompt. A new prompt is a new file exporting `<NAME>_PROMPT_VERSION` equal to its basename, a golden test, a catalogue line, and at least one case in `src/main/ai/eval/fixtures.ts`; `catalogue.test.ts` and `prompts.eval.ts` refuse anything missing. Shipped versions are never edited (their goldens and their rows in `src/main/ai/eval/token-report.md` pin them). The eval project (`vitest.config.ts`, `*.eval.ts` under `src/main/ai/eval/`, `npm run eval:ai`) is offline by default and pins the token report with `toMatchFileSnapshot` (`-u` to accept; the diff is the token delta); the live block (`MYTHSCRIBE_EVAL_LIVE=1`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, optional `OPENAI_BASE_URL`) calls `buildOpenAiProvider` directly, scores prose with `postProcessGhostText` + `checkGhostTextFidelity` and JSON against the bank, and writes `test-results/eval-ai/live-report.md`. The fixture's maxed ghost-text case sits at 1 432 of the 1 500-token budget (68 headroom; the regenerate at 1 459): a voice block at its 600-token budget plus the author block at its 130 (F-14.2), a full caret window, notes, and long metadata; anything added to `ghostText.v2` must come out of that.
- AI dial (F-14.4): `src/shared/aiSettings.ts` owns `AiDial` (0 Off, 1 Ask, 2 Suggest, 3 Draft; `AI_DIAL_LABEL`/`AI_DIAL_MEANING`), `AiSettings` `{ dial, features }` with `defaultAiSettings()` (dial 0, all toggles on), `AI_DATA_SHARING` (the one registry per `AiFeatureId`: label, what is sent, minimum dial; the panel renders it and `isFeatureAllowed` reads it; every feature must have an entry, a test enforces it), and `isFeatureAllowed(settings, feature)` (monotonic: level ≥ the feature's minimum and the toggle on). `AiFeatureId` in `src/shared/ai.ts` is the superset owner (`ghostText`, `tags`, `summary`, `chat`, `authorMode`, `query`, `critique`, `embeddings`); `FEATURE_BUDGETS`/`FEATURE_INPUT_BUDGETS` are partial with `outputBudget()`/`inputBudget()` defaults. Per project under key `ai` in the `settings` table (`getAiSettings`/`setAiSettings` beside the editor ones; fallback to defaults, no seeding) behind `aiSettings:get`/`aiSettings:set`. `src/main/ai/dial.ts` `assertFeatureAllowed` throws `AiDisabledError` (code `DISABLED`, next step names Settings); every caller checks it before `runAiRequest`, which stays gate-free. Renderer: `aiSettingsStore.ts` (editor-settings pattern: optimistic, one debounced write, revert and toast, pending-save registration, loaded with the tree, cleared on close, `resetAiSettingsStore()` in before and after each) and `AiDialSection.tsx` ("This project" at the top of the AI tab: radiogroup with roving tabindex, toggles disabled below level with "(needs X)" in the label, the data-sharing table, and the closing note). One switch (F-5.21, 2026-10-06): `AiDial` now parses 0..3 and outputs 0 | 1 (a stored 2 or 3 reads as 1, Ask, never Auto); `AiSettings.auto` (defaulted false) is the Auto bit; `AI_SWITCH_POSITIONS`/`AiSwitch`/`AI_SWITCH_LABEL`/`AI_SWITCH_MEANING`, `aiSwitchOf(settings)`, `aiSwitchPatch(position)`, and `needsSwitchText(label)` (the one gate wording, "X needs the AI switch at Ask or Auto") live beside it; every `minDial` is 1, so `isFeatureAllowed` is "on and toggled" and a new feature at `minDial: 1` runs at Ask and Auto. Since 2026-10-07 (decided by the author) the switch is split: `dial` is **Use AI** (`USE_AI_LABEL`, `USE_AI_MEANING`), a `role="switch"` checkbox at the top of `AiDialSection` and nowhere else; `AiSettings.chatMode` (`AssistantMode` `auto | ask | plan`, `ASSISTANT_MODES`, `ASSISTANT_MODE_LABEL`/`_MEANING`, `DEFAULT_ASSISTANT_MODE` ask) is the chat's mode, picked by `AssistantModeControl.tsx` under the message box (radiogroup `Mode`, `data-testid="assistant-mode"`, disabled while AI is off). The schema reads the legacy `auto` bit only to migrate (`storedAssistantMode`: on with `auto` → Auto, anything else Ask) and never writes it. `AiSwitch`/`aiSwitchPatch` survive only for `project:create.aiSwitch` (the wizard sends `off` or `ask`); `needsSwitchText` reads "X needs Use AI turned on" and `AI_NEXT_STEP.DISABLED` points at Settings › AI. The Settings table has no level column. The website's docs page (`site/public/docs/index.html`, table `dial-levels`) lists Use AI off/on and the three modes and `site.test.ts` checks it.
- Tag recommendations (F-4.7): the template for every AI feature. `src/main/ai/prompts/tags.v1.ts` is the first prompt (system rules first, then the bank names, then the passage last; JSON `{ "tags": [...] }`; `TAGS_TEXT_CHAR_BUDGET` 6 000 chars head-truncated; `maxTokens` from `outputBudget('tags')`) with a golden snapshot test and a budget assertion; new prompt versions are new files. `src/main/ai/recommendTags.ts` is the use case: `getDocumentContent` (documents only) → `docToText` (`src/shared/docText.ts`, the one plain-text extractor; inline tags render as `#name`) → the 50-char gate (`TAGS_MIN_CHARS`) → `assertFeatureAllowed(settings, 'tags')` → `runAiRequest` (`tags`, `fast`, `json`, `promptVersion`, `contextHash` over text plus sorted bank names) → strict zod parse → `toTagName` on both sides, unknown, linked, and duplicate names dropped, cap 8. `ai:recommendTags { nodeId }` returns `AiRecommendTagsResult`: success with the tags, usage, cost, model, cached; expected failures as data via `aiFailure` (code, message, next step) like `ai:testConnection`. Known limitation: a malformed answer is cached inside `runAiRequest` before the parse; a validation hook on the request path is a later change. The tag bar's Recommend button flushes the document store first (main reads the saved row), is disabled under 50 characters, keeps its state per node id in component state, links accepted chips through `documentTagStore.add` (nothing enters the manuscript), and shows `model · cost · cached` from `formatRequestCost`. The e2e fake server answers `POST /v1/chat/completions` with canned tag names.
- Writing presets (F-5.2): `src/shared/presets.ts` owns `PRESET_IDS` (general, action, suspense, dialogue, romance, worldBuilding, custom), `PresetParams` (temperature 0–1.5, `maxSuggestionTokens` 20–60 so the ghost-text budget is never exceeded, `styleInstruction` ≤ 300 chars, `allowNewElements`), the six built-in `PRESETS` with one-sentence instructions, `WritingPresets` `{ active, custom }`, `defaultWritingPresets()`, and `resolvePreset(settings)` (the params the prompt builders consume; they append the instruction after the voice profile, never in place of it). Per project under key `presets` in the settings table (`getWritingPresets`/`setWritingPresets`, fallback to defaults) behind `presets:get`/`presets:set`. Renderer: `presetsStore.ts` (the `aiSettingsStore` pattern retargeted) and `WritingPresetsSection.tsx` under the dial in the AI tab (radiogroup with roving tabindex; arrow keys live on the radios so the Custom fields keep theirs; built-ins show a read-only `<dl>`, Custom shows the four fields with clamp-on-commit and a blank-instruction restore).
- Voice profile (F-14.1): no AI call anywhere in it. `src/shared/voice.ts` owns the exemplar kinds, the limits (`VOICE_EXEMPLAR_TEXT_MIN/MAX` 80/2 000 chars, `VOICE_EXEMPLAR_MAX` 12, `VOICE_BLOCK_TOKEN_BUDGET` 600) and `voiceConfidence`; `src/shared/stylometry.ts` is the pure heuristics module (`computeStylometrics`, `renderVoiceRules` with evidence gates and a cap of 8 lines, `classifyKind`; thresholds are exported constants, tests pin the boundaries). Table `voice_exemplar` (migration `0004_voice`; `node_id` set null on delete so an exemplar outlives its node) via `src/main/voice/exemplarStore.ts` (`listExemplars`, `addExemplar` snapshots plain text plus the document's POV and classifies the kind, refuses a 13th with `VALIDATION`, `removeExemplar`). `src/main/voice/profile.ts` `buildVoiceProfile(db, { pov? })` (manuscript documents only; the POV group counts only when it holds ≥ `POV_MIN_WORDS` 2 000, else the whole manuscript; the corpus actually used sets `wordCount` and confidence) caches per POV against `versionCache.ts`, whose counter `document:save` and every exemplar write bump and `manager.onChange` resets; `voiceBlock.ts` renders the rules plus up to 3 exemplars (kind match +2, POV match +1, greedy within the budget, only the first one may be truncated) or `null` when there is nothing, and `generateGhostText` passes it into the prompt's `voice` slot with `voiceVersion` in the context hash. Channels `voice:listExemplars`/`addExemplar`/`removeExemplar`/`profile`. Renderer: `voiceStore.ts` (exemplars loaded with the tree, `loadProfile` on demand, `add`/`remove` merge, `resetVoiceStore()` in before and after each), `selectedText.ts` (`editor.state.doc.textBetween` with newlines), `MarkVoiceExemplarButton.tsx` in the single-document toolbar (enabled for 80–2 000 selected characters, disabled at the cap, toasts `Added to your voice profile (n of 12)`), and `VoiceSection.tsx` under the presets in the AI tab (`data-testid` `voice-section`/`voice-words`, confidence `<progress>`, rules, `Voice exemplars` list with Remove). Per-POV attribution reads each document's own scene metadata (no ancestor walk); the e2e asserts the fake server's captured system message carries the voice block. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): `MarkVoiceExemplarButton` is deleted (no UI marks an exemplar by hand; `voice:addExemplar` and `useVoiceStore.add` stay, and the e2e marks one through the bridge).
- Learned voice (F-14.14; migration `0016_voice_exemplar_source`, job kind `voice`, feature id `voiceNotes` at Ask, `fast`, JSON, 400 out / 2,500 in, prompt `voiceNotes.v1`): `src/shared/voice.ts` owns `VoiceExemplarSource` (`author` | `auto`), the thresholds (`VOICE_AUTO_EXEMPLAR_MAX` 6, `VOICE_AUTO_REFRESH_WORDS` 1,500, `VOICE_JOB_DEBOUNCE_MS` 30 s, passages 200–900 chars; notes: `VOICE_NOTES_MAX` 8 × 160 chars, first at 2,000 words, then every 5,000, a 6,000-char sample, `VOICE_NOTES_TOKEN_BUDGET` 200 inside the voice block) and the settings rows `voiceAuto` (`VoiceAutoState { basedOnWords, dismissed: sha256[] }`) and `voiceNotes` (`VoiceNotes { notes, basedOnWords, model, updated }`), read and written by `get/setVoiceAutoState` and `get/setVoiceNotes` in `settingsStore.ts`. `VoiceExemplar.source` and `VoiceProfile.notes` are in the contract. Main: `voice/autoExemplars.ts` (`authorPassages` per document: top-level paragraphs without an `aiOrigin` mark, short ones joined to ~400 chars, long ones cut to whole sentences, other blocks close a run; `selectAutoExemplars` pure: kinds in rotation, closest `styleDistance` to the profile's stylometrics, +1 for a used scene, +0.25 for a used POV, skips dismissed hashes and overlaps with hand-marked texts; `refreshAutoExemplars` when due); `exemplarStore.ts` (`replaceAutoExemplars` touches only `auto` rows and bumps the version only on a change; `removeExemplar` of an `auto` row records its `passageHash`; the 12 cap counts `author` rows); `ai/voiceNotes.ts` (`voiceNotesDue`, `sampleVoicePassages` newest-first stride, `parseVoiceNotesAnswer` lenient, `refreshVoiceNotes` gate → sample → shrink to the input budget → request → store → bump, `clearVoiceNotes` keeps the word count so notes return after 5,000 more words); `voice/voiceJob.ts` (`manuscriptRootId`, `runVoiceJob`: the local step always, the notes step when due, allowed, and a provider is set up; failures swallowed except a cancel, remembered in a per-project memo until 1,500 more words). `voiceBlock` renders `Style notes learned from the manuscript:` after the stylometric rules and before the author rules, and ranks a hand-marked exemplar ahead of an automatic one on a tie; with no notes the block is byte-identical to before, so no prompt version moved. `handlers.ts`: `voiceQueue` (silent, `minIntervalMs` 0), touched by `documentsWritten`, queued by `backfillSummaries` (open, AI settings change, saved key, import), cleared on project change; channels `voice:notes`, `voice:clearNotes`. Renderer: `voiceStore` (`notes`, `loadNotes`, `clearNotes` → reloads the profile), `VoiceSection` (re-lists exemplars and loads notes on mount; `voice-notes` block "What MythScribe has learned about your style" with Clear and `voice-notes-updated`; "Picked automatically" on auto rows; `voice-words` counts marked and picked apart), `MarkVoiceExemplarButton` (deleted 2026-10-06) counted only hand-marked rows. E2E: the Voice section's notes block and the hand-marked row filtered from automatic ones. Known gaps: no event when the job changes exemplars or notes (the section re-fetches on mount); the word thresholds use the manuscript's total word count, not author-only words.
- Fidelity check (F-14.7): `src/shared/voiceFidelity.ts` is the pure scorer over `Stylometrics` (`checkGhostTextFidelity` for a fragment: tense, person, dialogue tag, adverbs, sentence length, semicolon, each with an evidence gate on the profile side; `scoreDocumentDrift` for a whole document; thresholds are exported constants). `generateGhostText` runs it after post-processing unless `voiceBlock` returned `null` (no rules, no exemplars, no author rules; since F-14.2 the seeded banned phrases make the block non-null on a fresh project, so the banned-phrase signal can fire from day one while the stylometric ones stay evidence-gated), regenerates once through `src/main/ai/prompts/ghostTextRegen.v1.ts` (`buildGhostTextRegenPrompt` wraps the base prompt and appends `Your last attempt <violation>. …` to the system turn; `GHOST_REGEN_PROMPT_VERSION`; the violation's message joins the context hash so the two calls never share a cache key, and since F-14.2 two banned phrases, which share one code, never share a cached regenerate either), re-scores, and returns `flagged`/`violation` with `usage` and `costUsd` summed over both calls; the second `runAiRequest` alone is wrapped in try/catch and any failure returns the first answer flagged. The data-sharing line for ghost text names the regenerate. Renderer: `GhostState` carries `flagged`/`violation` through `set`/`advance`/consume, `setGhost(text, flagged?, violation?)`, `renderGhost` sets `data-flagged` and appends `.ghost-text-flag` (title and aria-label; note the widget is `aria-hidden`, like the ghost text itself); styles use `--ms-warning`. Report: `buildConsistencyReport(db, { pov? })` in `src/main/voice/consistency.ts` (reuses `manuscriptDocuments`/`documentText` exported from `profile.ts`; `short` under `RULE_MIN_WORDS`, `drift`, `ok`; tree order; on demand, never cached, never on a timer) behind `voice:consistencyReport`; `voiceStore.loadReport()` and the `Voice consistency` list in `VoiceSection.tsx` (Check voice consistency button, drifting rows first with a nested `Drift in <title>` list, skipped scenes summarised in one line, titles call `useTreeStore.select`). The e2e's fake server answers a sentinel passage off-voice on every call so the badge is provably shown; the clean-regenerate path is unit-tested.
- Author rules (F-14.2): `src/shared/authorRules.ts` owns `AUTHOR_RULES_KEY` (`authorRules`, JSON in the project `settings` table like `ai` and `focus`), the limits (`AUTHOR_RULES_TEXT_MAX` 400 chars, `BANNED_PHRASE_MAX` 60, `BANNED_PHRASES_MAX` 100, `AUTHOR_RULES_TOKEN_BUDGET` 130), `DEFAULT_BANNED_PHRASES` (the seeded AI-isms), the `AuthorRules` schema (`rules` kept as typed so a trailing space survives a keystroke; `bannedPhrases` normalised in a transform: `normalizeBannedPhrase` trims, collapses whitespace, folds curly quotes; `normalizeBannedPhrases` drops empty and over-length entries, dedupes case-insensitively, cuts at the cap; both default so an older row parses), `defaultAuthorRules()` (no text, the seeded list: a missing row is the seed, nothing is written at creation), `findBannedPhrases(phrases, text)` (case-insensitive, `\p{L}\p{N}` boundaries at both ends, whitespace-flexible, quote-folded on both sides; in order of first appearance, each once), `AUTHOR_RULES_HEADER`, and `renderAuthorRulesBlock` (header, trimmed rules text, then `Never use these phrases: a; b.` with as many phrases in order as keep the estimate under the budget; a phrase left out of the prompt is still enforced by the post-filter). Main: `getAuthorRules`/`setAuthorRules` in `settingsStore.ts` (the focus-settings pattern) behind `authorRules:get`/`authorRules:set`; the set handler calls `bumpVoiceVersion()` because `buildVoiceProfile` fills `VoiceProfile.authorRules` inside its cached compute. `voiceBlock` renders the block after the stylometric rules and before the exemplars, under its own budget (the exemplar greedy fill still measures against `VOICE_BLOCK_TOKEN_BUDGET` alone), and returns non-null whenever the block is. `src/shared/voiceFidelity.ts`: `FIDELITY_CODES` gained `bannedPhrase`, `checkBannedPhrases(banned, text)`, and `checkGhostTextFidelity(profile, fragment, banned = [])` prepends them; `checkChatFidelity(profile, text, banned = [])` in `chat.ts` does the same for both branches, and ghost text, chat Agent mode, and rewrite pass `profile.authorRules.bannedPhrases`, so the F-14.7 regenerate clause names the phrase. The ghost-text context hash carries the rendered block. Renderer: `authorRulesStore.ts` (the `aiSettingsStore` pattern verbatim; loaded with the tree and cleared on close in `App.tsx`; after a successful write it calls `useVoiceStore.loadProfile()` when a profile is held), `AuthorRulesSection.tsx` in the AI tab right after the Voice section (`data-testid` `author-rules-section`; textarea `Style rules` with a counter; `Banned phrases` list of chips with `Remove phrase <x>` buttons; `New banned phrase` input, Enter or Add; `Restore default phrases` merges only the missing seeded phrases and is disabled when none are; the field and Add are disabled at the cap). The ghost-text `sends` copy and the website's docs table (pinned by `site.test.ts`) name the rules. Gap: the consistency report does not yet scan the author's own text for banned phrases.
- Proposals (F-14.5): every AI output is a row in `ai_proposal` (migration `0005_proposals`; `node_id` and the self-reference `regenerated_from` are `ON DELETE SET NULL`, the lazy `(): AnySQLiteColumn =>` form; indexes on `created_at` and `node_id`). `src/shared/proposal.ts` owns `PROPOSAL_STATUSES`, `SETTLED_STATUSES`/`SettledStatus`, `PROPOSAL_NOTE_MAX` (300), `PROPOSAL_RETENTION_MAX` (500), `normalizeProposalNote` (blank → null). `src/main/ai/proposalStore.ts`: `createProposal` (mints the id, inserts `pending`, stores an evicted predecessor as null, then `evictProposals` keeps the newest 500 like `evictCache`), `settleProposal` (`UPDATE … WHERE status = 'pending'`, returns whether a row changed, so a second settle is a no-op), `getProposal`, `proposalCount`. Handlers: `ai:ghostText` creates a proposal when `text !== ''` and returns `proposalId` (else null); `ai:recommendTags` always creates one with `content = JSON.stringify(names)` (a historical snapshot, never a bank join) and takes optional `note`/`regeneratedFrom`, which switch `recommendTags` to `prompts/tagsRegen.v1.ts` (wraps `buildTagsPrompt`, one system clause `The writer asked for a different set…`) and join the context hash so a note-less regenerate never hits the cache; `proposal:settle { id, status, note? }`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/ai/proposalStore.ts` `proposalStore.settle(id, status, note?)` (never throws, toasts on failure, a session-wide `Set` sends each id once; `resetProposalStore()` in before and after each). Ghost text: `GhostState.full` is the original text; plugin state is `{ ghost, exit }` and a plugin view reports each exit exactly once through `editor.storage.ghostText.onSettle(status, consumed)` (`accepted` all of `full` consumed, `acceptedPart` some, `rejected` none; `destroy` is the backstop; a `registerPlugin` reconfigure does not fire); the controller registers the hook per session, stashes `proposalId` per request, and settles with no note. Tags: `TagsPanel`'s `done` state carries `proposalId` and `acceptedCount`; Dismiss settles `acceptedPart`/`rejected`, linking the last chip settles `accepted` (issued inside the `setRecommend` updater, safe under StrictMode because of the per-id guard), Regenerate… uses `dialogs.prompt` (validate refuses over 300 chars) then `askForTags(nodeId, { note, regeneratedFrom })`. The e2e's `TAGS_REGEN_SENTINEL` repeats the clause prefix because the e2e tsconfig cannot import `src/main`. Statuses have no `edited`: editing accepted text is F-14.6's. The target range columns are filled by `ai:rewrite` (F-14.10) as a snapshot of the ProseMirror range at request time; other features leave them null.
- Provenance (F-14.6): `src/shared/provenance.ts` owns `AI_ORIGIN_MARK`, `AI_ORIGIN_KEEP_RATIO` (0.5), `AiOriginAttrs { proposalId, accepted }`, `aiOriginAttrsOf`, `aiOriginStats(doc)` (`aiChars`, `totalChars`, `byProposal`; text-node characters only), and `aiOriginPercent`. `src/renderer/features/editor/aiOrigin.ts`: the `AiOrigin` mark (`inclusive: false`, `span.ai-origin[data-ai-origin][data-proposal-id][data-accepted]`, `parseHTML` for in-app paste), `markAiOrigin(tr, from, to, attrs)` (adds the mark, re-marks an adjacent run of the same proposal so word-by-word acceptance is one span, and sets the `AI_ORIGIN_KEY` meta that vouches for the insertion), and the plugin: state `{ marked }` so unmarked documents pay nothing; `appendTransaction` on a doc change without the vouching meta strips the mark from `insertedRanges(tr)` (each step map's new ranges mapped forward through the later maps; skipped for `history$` undo/redo and `uiEvent` paste/drop) and then clears every span of a proposal whose surviving characters fall under `accepted × AI_ORIGIN_KEEP_RATIO` (max `accepted` across its spans), in one appended transaction so undo restores it with the edit. `ghostText.ts`: `GhostState.proposalId`/`acceptedChars`, `setGhost(text, flagged?, violation?, proposalId?)`, `insertAccepted` marks what Tab/Shift+Tab insert with the running total; a mark-only transaction (Add/RemoveMarkStep only) is neither typing nor a caret move, so the appended strip cannot dismiss a suggestion. `extensions.ts` registers `AiOrigin` with `InlineTag` and `GhostText` (manuscript documents only). `DocumentEditor.tsx` `useLiveDocStats` (one `update` subscription, one `getJSON` per document change, cached by document identity) feeds `StatusBar`'s optional `aiPercent` (`data-testid="status-ai"`, `N% AI`, only when > 0). Main: `src/main/provenance/report.ts` `buildProvenanceReport(db)` (manuscript documents via `manuscriptDocuments`; `documentJson(row)` in `voice/profile.ts` is the one defensive parse, `documentText` delegates to it; `proposals` is the distinct id count) behind `provenance:report`; `disclosure.ts` `renderDisclosure(report, { projectName, date })` is pure Markdown with an inline-snapshot test; `provenance:export` asks `dialogs.chooseExportPath(defaultName, filters, directory?)` (default `<project>-ai-disclosure.md` beside the project folder), writes tmp + rename, returns `{ path }` or null on cancel. Renderer: `provenanceStore.ts` (`load`/`exportReport` flush pending saves first because main reads saved rows; cleared on project close in `App.tsx`; `resetProvenanceStore()`), `ProvenanceSection.tsx` under `VoiceSection` (`provenance-section`, `provenance-percent`, `provenance-empty`, list `AI origin by scene` with `provenance-scene` items whose title button selects the scene, button `Export disclosure report`, toast `Saved to <path>`). The e2e sets a DOM selection inside the span (it can wrap across lines, so a positioned click is unreliable) and deletes 25 of the 41 accepted characters (the join at the caret adds a leading and a trailing space to the continuation) to see the mark go.
- Assistant panel (F-5.4): `src/shared/chat.ts` owns the conversation model and limits (`CONVERSATIONS_KEY`, `ChatMode`, `CHAT_*` caps, `ChatMessage`, `Conversation`, `Conversations`, `parseStoredConversations`, `titleFor`, `parseTagRefs`); conversations are JSON in the project `settings` table (`getConversations`/`setConversations`, `conversations:get`/`set`), main is stateless about them and the renderer sends the last `CHAT_HISTORY_TURNS` as `history`. `src/main/ai/request.ts` has `runAiStream(deps, input, onDelta)` beside `runAiRequest`, sharing `prepare`/`serveCached`/`record` (a cache hit is one delta; a stream without usage falls back to `estimateTokens`; a mid-stream error logs and caches nothing); `StreamChunk.usage?` arrives on the OpenAI adapter's final chunk (`stream_options.include_usage`). `src/main/ai/context/chatContext.ts` `buildChatContext(db, { nodeId, message })` (scene text head-truncated to `CHAT_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET`, `#name` refs resolved against the tag bank to the notes of the documents linked to that tag within `CHAT_REF_NOTES_CHAR_BUDGET`, unknown names dropped). `src/main/ai/chat.ts` `runChat` (Plan needs `chat` allowed, Agent also `dial >= AI_DATA_SHARING.ghostText.minDial`; the task context is appended to the system turn so history turns are real user/assistant messages; the oldest history is dropped first when over the input budget; Agent answers go through `postProcessChatText` and `checkChatFidelity` with one `chatRegen.v1` regenerate and fall back flagged; every answer is an `ai_proposal` row, Plan ones stay pending). Prompts `chat.v1.ts`/`chatRegen.v1.ts` and, since F-14.3, `chat.v2.ts`/`chatRegen.v2.ts` (Agent mode folds the scene brief in right after the metadata line; Plan mode never carries it) are catalogued with fixture cases (the maxed Agent case sits at 7 003 of 8 000 on v1, 7 406 on v2). Handler `ai:chat` emits `ai:chatDelta { requestId, delta }` per non-empty delta before resolving. Renderer: `activeEditorStore.ts` (`{ id, editor } | null`, set by `DocumentEditor` on ready and focus, released on unmount) is the one way the panel reaches the editor; `assistantStore.ts` (load with the tree, clear on close, the presets write pattern; `send` appends the user turn and an empty assistant turn, subscribes to deltas for its `requestId`, drops stale ones, removes the empty turn and toasts on failure; Agent mode focuses the editor, `setGhost(text, flagged, violation, proposalId)`, records the notice turn, wraps `storage.ghostText.onSettle` for exactly one settlement and restores it; an empty answer or no editor settles `rejected` and toasts); `AssistantPanel.tsx` (`aside[aria-label="Assistant"]`, `data-testid="assistant-panel"`, tablist `Conversations`, log `Messages` with `chat-turn`/`chat-pending`/`chat-turn-cost`, radiogroup `Mode`, combobox `Paragraphs`, textbox `Message` (no Clear conversation since 2026-10-06), `assistant-disabled` below Ask) rendered in `ProjectScreen` after the main section with `Layout.assistant` (`LAYOUT_LIMITS.assistant` 0.2–0.5; `toggle` runs `normalizeLayout`, giving way assistant → notes → sidebar); `AssistantToggleButton` in the header owns Ctrl/Cmd+K. Ghost text: `insertAccepted` splits on blank lines into paragraphs (single newlines are hard breaks) with the mark on each and returns the end position; `.ghost-text` is `white-space: pre-wrap`. The e2e fake server streams SSE for `stream: true` and tells Agent requests by the opening of `AGENT_RULES`. Modes since 2026-10-07 (decided by the author): the panel's modes are Auto / Ask / Plan, one project setting (`AiSettings.chatMode`, previous bullet) instead of a per-conversation mode; `Conversation.mode` is optional, parsed for old rows and never written. `CHAT_MODES` (`query`, `agent` = Author, `plan`) stays only as the label a stored turn carries (`ChatMessage.mode`; a stored Author turn still renders `AGENT_NOTICE`). The panel no longer calls `ai:chat` (no streaming, no ghost placement, no paragraph count; `runChat`/`chat.v5` stay in main), nor subscribes to `ai:chatDelta`. While AI or the `chat`/`agent` toggles are off, `assistant-disabled` says so (`AI_OFF_MESSAGE` names Settings › AI).
