Known gaps: live fidelity for agent.v6 not run; the savings are fixture numbers (answer step −44 %, three-request run −25 %), not the ledger's.
- Index job queue (F-5.13): `src/shared/jobs.ts` owns `JOB_MIN_INTERVAL_MS` 500, `JOB_BACKOFF_MS` [5,15,45] s, `JOB_MAX_ATTEMPTS` 3, the zod `JobKind` (`summary` | `mentions`; a new kind is a new member and either a branch in a `run` or, when its failure policy differs, its own `createIndexQueue` instance with `kind` set — F-4.12's mention scan is the second instance, silent and never paused), `JobStatus` (`queued` | `failed`), `JobFailure { code, message, nextStep }`, `IndexQueueStatus { queued, running, failed, done, paused }` and `IDLE_INDEX_QUEUE`. Table `index_job` (migration `0007_index_jobs`): id `<kind>:<node_id>`, `node_id` cascaded, `attempts`, `last_error` JSON; only `queued` and `failed` rows are persisted — a running job is memory-only, so a crash leaves it `queued` and reopening resumes it, and a finished job leaves no row (the `scene_summary` and the ledger row are its record). `src/main/jobs/jobStore.ts` (`listJobs` oldest first, ties by rowid so one "Summarize all" keeps reading order; `getJob`, `enqueueJob` (a queued row is left in place, a failed one returns with attempts 0), `bumpAttempts`, `markFailed`, `deleteJob`, `deleteAllJobs`, `requeueFailed`; an unknown kind/status row is skipped and a corrupt `last_error` reads as a generic PROVIDER failure). `src/main/jobs/indexQueue.ts` `createIndexQueue<T>({ kind?, db, run, cancelRequest, now, onChange, onNodeStatus, debounceMs, minIntervalMs, backoffMs, maxAttempts, requestIdFor })` (`kind` narrows every `listJobs`/`deleteAllJobs`/`requeueFailed` call to that kind, so two instances over one table never pick, cancel, or requeue each other's rows; `jobStore` takes the same optional last parameter): one serial lane for foreground and background, `touch` (debounce → enqueue, node pending at once, a save during a run keeps the row for exactly one more run), `runNow` (joins the run in flight or waiting — handing it the author's request id — else jumps the queue and deletes the row), `load` (resume), `indexAll`, `cancelAll`, `resume`, `clear` (generation guard; rows stay), `status`, `nodeStatus`/`nodeError`. Failure policy: transient (RATE_LIMIT/NETWORK/PROVIDER/unknown) retries with backoff then `failed`; hard (NO_KEY/INVALID_KEY/QUOTA/BUDGET/DISABLED) pauses the whole queue with the job untouched; VALIDATION/NOT_FOUND are quiet (row deleted, node idle); CANCELLED records nothing; the one exception is a Summarize now the dial refuses, which leaves the node quiet and the queue unpaused because the channel answers it as data. Timers are unref'd and nothing throws into one. Contract: `jobs:status`, `jobs:cancel`, `jobs:resume`, `jobs:indexAll` (→ `JobsIndexAllResult`, gated by `assertFeatureAllowed(settings, 'summary')`) and the `jobs:changed` event carrying the whole status. Handlers build the queue where the scheduler was, with `db: () => manager.current() === null ? null : manager.require().connection.orm`, `run` = `summarizeScene` reporting `requested: !result.cached`, `cancelRequest` = `cancelInflight`, and `manager.onChange` → `clear()` then `load()`. Gotcha: a background job's request id is `job-<n>` from a module counter, so `ai:cancel` can reach it and main's duplicate-id guard never fires. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/ai/indexingStore.ts` (`status`, `load` opens the one `jobs:changed` subscription, `cancel`, `resume`, `indexAll` toasting "Queued N scenes for a summary." / "Every scene is up to date." / the refusal with its next step, `clear` on project close, `resetIndexingStore()` in before and after each) and `IndexingIndicator.tsx` in the header beside `AiActivityIndicator` (`indexing`, `role="status"` named Indexing; "Indexing {done + running} of {done + queued + running + failed} scenes", "Indexing paused · {message}" with the next step as its `title`, "{n} scenes could not be summarised", `indexing-resume` Retry when stalled, `indexing-cancel` always). "Summarize all scenes" (`summarize-all`) sits under the dial section in `AiSettingsTab`, disabled unless `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'summary')`. No provider batch API at launch (see `FEATURES.md` F-5.13): do not add a `batch?` to `Provider`. The e2e step follows the F-5.6 one: it edits Scene 1 with the toggle off so exactly one scene is stale, clicks the button, and checks the toast, the single request, and the ledger; the paused, failed, and Cancel paths are covered by the unit tests and are a manual check in the running app (turn the key off and click Summarize all scenes: the indicator pauses with "No API key is saved." and Retry). Backfill (2026-10-06): `backfillSummaries(resume)` in `handlers.ts` waits `SUMMARY_BACKFILL_DELAY_MS` (5 s, one timer, coalesced, dropped on a project change by `cancelBackfill`) and then, when `summary` is allowed and `ai.get(sourceOf(db))` has a provider, `queue.indexAll('summary', staleSummaryNodeIds(db))`; `resume` (from `aiSettings:set` and `ai:setKey`) also lifts a pause. Called from the project-open block, `aiSettings:set`, `ai:setKey`, and `import:commit` (which also queues the mention scan). `staleSummaryNodeIds` reads the tree once (`sourceOfRow`). Cloud sign-in does not trigger it; the next open or settings change does.
- Beta reader (F-14.11): `src/shared/betaReader.ts` owns the five categories (`BETA_READER_CATEGORIES` knows / believes / expects / confusion / droppedThread, + labels), the caps (`BETA_READER_TEXT_MIN` 200, `BETA_READER_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 20,000, `BETA_READER_SHRINK_CHARS` 2,000, `BETA_READER_SCENE_CHAR_FLOOR` 4,000, `BETA_READER_MAX_ITEMS` 12, quote 240 / note 300), the zod `BetaReaderItem { category, scene (1-based into the list sent), quote, note }` and `BetaReaderScene { nodeId, title, current }`. Feature id `betaReader` (tier `strong`, `minDial` 1, budgets 1,200 out / 12,000 in); the honesty setting is `AiSettings.critique.honesty`, shared with F-14.8. Contract `ai:betaReader { nodeId, requestId, note?, regeneratedFrom? }` → `AiBetaReaderResult` (`items`, `scenes` in the order sent with the current one last, `truncated`, `skipped`, `missing`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`, `requestId`, or the AI failure shape). Main: `src/main/ai/prompts/betaReader.v1.ts` (`BETA_READER_RULES` opens with `You are the beta-reader feature inside a novel-writing app.`, the e2e sentinel pinned by the golden test; system = rules + `BETA_READER_HONESTY[honesty]` (reader-flavoured lines, not the critique ones), user = `Scenes read so far, in order (summaries):` with `[n] Chapter › Title\nsummary\n- key point` per earlier scene or `No earlier scenes.`, then `[N] Title (this scene, full text):` and `Report as the reader.`; no voice block, no story bible, no brief by design) and `betaReaderRegen.v1.ts` (the note clause appended to the user turn, so the cached system prefix is byte-identical); `src/main/ai/betaReader.ts` `runBetaReader(db, deps, input)` (`assertFeatureAllowed` first, the text gate, VALIDATION for a node outside `manuscriptDocuments`, `summariesFor` over every earlier manuscript document (no summary → `missing`, staleness not checked), titles as `Chapter › Scene` unless the parent is a section root, `fitReadThrough` (shrink the scene to the floor, then drop the farthest scenes as `skipped`, measured as `runAiRequest` measures), `runAiRequest` JSON on the strong tier with the hash over the scene text, the ordered `(nodeId, contentHash)` of the summaries, missing/skipped, honesty, note, predecessor; `parseBetaReaderAnswer(text, texts)` drops and counts a bad shape, a scene number out of range, a blank quote or note, and a quote `findQuote` cannot find in that scene's text as sent (`summary + key points` for an earlier scene), dedupes by scene + normalized quote, caps at 12), handler `ai:betaReader` (one proposal per report with the items as JSON, never flagged), catalogue entries for both versions with eval cases fresh/full/maxed (20 earlier scenes at every cap sit at 11,599 of 12,000; 25 would not fit) plus the two regen cases under scoring kind `betaReader { texts }`. Renderer: `betaReaderStore.ts` (one session app-wide like `critiqueStore` minus Apply: `start` flushes the autosave then `track('betaReader', …)`; `show(index, editor)` only for an item whose scene is the current one, `locateText` or mark stale; `regenerate` settles `regenerated`; `close` settles `rejected` (a pending one cancels); `dismissFor`; `resetBetaReaderStore()`), `BetaReaderPanel.tsx` (`section[aria-label="Beta reader"]`, `beta-reader-panel`, `-pending`, `-stop`, `-honesty` (the `HonestySelect` shared with the critique panel, `testId` prop), `-group` per non-empty category in order with an `h3`, `-item` (`data-category`), `-quote` (a button for this scene, a `p` for an earlier one), `-scene` chip with the earlier scene's title, `-stale`, `-empty`, `-truncated`, `-skipped`, `-missing`, `-cost`, `-dropped`, `-regenerate` "Read again…" via `dialogs.prompt`, `-close`, `-error` `role="alert"`), and `BetaReaderButton.tsx` ("Beta reader", lucide `Glasses`, gated like `CritiqueButton`). `DocumentEditor` mounts the button after `CritiqueButton` and the panel under `CritiquePanel` (single-document view only) and calls `dismissFor(id)` on unmount or rebuild. The e2e step follows the F-5.6 summary step (Scene 1 has a summary; Opening before it is under the floor, so `missing` is 1 and the user turn says `No earlier scenes.`) and checks the fabricated quote and the item naming an unsent scene never reach the panel. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): `BetaReaderButton` is deleted (Actions › Beta reader, or a routed chat turn); `BetaReaderPanel` and `ProofreadPanel` render in `AiResults`.
- Story Intelligence query mode (F-5.7): `src/shared/query.ts` owns the limits (`QUERY_FULL_SCENES` 3, `QUERY_SUMMARY_SCENES` 10, `QUERY_SCENE_CHAR_BUDGET` 12,000, `QUERY_SHRINK_CHARS` 2,000, `QUERY_SCENE_CHAR_FLOOR` 3,000, `QUERY_MAX_CITATIONS` 6, `QUERY_QUOTE_MAX` 240, `QUERY_ANSWER_MAX` 2,000, `QUERY_ALSO_MAX` 8), `QUERY_NOT_FOUND`, the zod `QuerySceneRef { nodeId, title }`, `QueryCitation` (+ `scene` as sent, `quote`), `QueryTurn { found, uncited, citations, also }`, `CITATION_MARKER` (`[n]`), `citedSceneNumbers`, `stripDanglingMarkers`. `CHAT_MODES` gained `query` (label Query) and `ChatMessage.query: QueryTurn | null` (zod-defaulted to null so stored rows parse). Feature id `query` (tier `strong`, JSON, `minDial` 1, budgets 600 out / 12,000 in). Contract `ai:query { nodeId | null, message, history, requestId }` → `AiQueryResult` (`answer`, `found`, `uncited`, `citations`, `also`, `dropped`, usage/cost/cached/model, `proposalId`, `requestId`, or the failure shape). Main: `src/main/ai/context/queryContext.ts` (`queryTerms`: lowercase unicode tokens ≥ 3 chars minus a stopword list, plus `parseTagRefs`; pure `scoreCandidate` with `QUERY_WEIGHTS` title 4 / tag 4 / metadata 3 / summary block 2 / body 1 per distinct term + 0.1 × min(occurrences, 10), a `#name` ref matching a document tag + 6, word-prefix match through a unicode lookbehind, not `\b`; `rankCandidates(db, { question, nodeId })` over `manuscriptDocuments` with `listAllDocumentTags` (one query, new in `documentTagStore.ts`), `parseStoredSceneMeta`, `summariesFor`, and `documentText`, ties by reading order with the active scene first, score 0 excluded, fallback to the active scene then reading order when nothing scores; `sceneTitles` (`Chapter › Scene`) moved here from `betaReader.ts`), prompt `src/main/ai/prompts/query.v1.ts` (`QUERY_RULES` opens with `You are the Story Intelligence feature inside a novel-writing app.`, the e2e sentinel pinned by the golden test; system = rules + `Scenes (full text):` `[n] title` blocks + `Other scenes (summaries only):`, numbers continuing; history as real turns; the question last; no voice block, bible, or temperature), `src/main/ai/query.ts` `runQuery` (`assertFeatureAllowed` first, VALIDATION when no manuscript document has text, `fitQueryPrompt` in the order shrink the longest full scene to the floor → drop summaries lowest-ranked first → drop the oldest history down to `QUERY_HISTORY_KEEP` 2 → drop the lowest-ranked full scenes keeping one → the rest of the history, measured as `runAiRequest` measures; the hash covers the question, history, `(nodeId, text)` of the full scenes and `(nodeId, contentHash)` of the summaries; `parseQueryAnswer(text, scenes)` drops and counts a citation naming a summary-only or unsent scene, a blank quote, or one `findQuote` cannot find in the text sent, dedupes, caps at 6, strips dangling markers; `found: false` discards citations uncounted, `found` with none left is `uncited`; `also` = ranked minus cited, first 8), handler `ai:query` (proposal `query` with `{ answer, citations }` as JSON, pending, never flagged), catalogue `query.v1` with eval cases fresh/full/maxed (the maxed case is built by the real fit and sits at 11,552 of 12,000) under scoring kind `query { texts }`. Renderer: `assistantStore.ts` `send` routes a `query` conversation to `ai:query` (tracked as `query`, not streamed; the turn gets the answer, cost, proposal, and `query`), `openScene(ref, quote)` (`useTreeStore.select`, then the registered editor or a one-shot `useActiveEditorStore` subscription with `OPEN_SCENE_TIMEOUT_MS` 3 s, then `locateText` + `setTextSelection().scrollIntoView()`; a gone quote toasts `PASSAGE_GONE_MESSAGE`); `AssistantPanel.tsx` `Query` radio (gated on `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'query')` on top of the chat gate; Paragraphs stays Agent-only), `QueryAnswer` (`query-not-found`, `query-uncited` with `QUERY_UNCITED_WARNING`, live `[n]` markers as `query-cite` buttons, Sources as `query-citation` buttons, Also mentioned in as `query-also` chips). The e2e step follows the beta reader step: it asks about the storm on the ridge so Scene 1 ranks `[1]`, checks the fabricated quote and the `[3]` marker never reach the panel, and clicks the citation to select the passage. No split view: the app has none. query.v3 (2026-10-06): `runQuery` builds `query.v3` (`QUERY_RULES_V3` keeps v1's first sentence for the e2e sentinel; heading `QUERY_BIBLE_HEADING_V3`); the bible renders through `renderStoryBibleEntities(entities, QUERY_V3_BIBLE_TOKEN_BUDGET 1500, QUERY_V3_BIBLE_VALUE_MAX 600)` (the value cap is now a parameter, default `STORY_BIBLE_VALUE_MAX`); `sentSheets` lists the entities whose own sheet went out (`StoryBibleEntity.entityId`, optional, set by `storyBibleEntities`), `parseQueryAnswer(text, scenes, sheetsSent)` keeps the model's `sheets` names that match them (`toEntityNameKey`), and `uncited` needs no citation and no sheet. `rankCandidates` falls back to `sceneEntities` (entities the top full scenes name, a character by first name of 3+ letters too, most-named first, `QUERY_SCENE_ENTITIES` 3) when the question names none. `QueryTurn.sheets` (`QuerySheetRef`, defaulted `[]` for stored turns) rides `AiQueryResult`; `AssistantPanel` lists them under "From your notes" (`data-testid="query-sheet"`, opens the entity through `useEntityStore.select`). `send` and `sendQuery` flush the document store before the request.
  - F-5.17: `ai:query` takes an optional `pinActive`; `runQuery` passes it to `rankCandidates`, which then puts the active scene (when it has text) first whatever it scores, the rest in their usual order, so the What happened here? recap always sends the open scene as `[1]`.
- Ghost text (F-5.3): the renderer owns the trigger and sends only the caret window. `src/renderer/features/editor/ghostText.ts` is the `GhostText` Tiptap extension (always in the manuscript schema; the suggestion lives in plugin state and renders as a widget decoration `.ghost-text` at the caret, never in the document; the anchor maps with `assoc = -1` so a keystroke at the caret consumes rather than clears; Tab accepts all, Shift+Tab one word plus its space, Escape clears; a matching typed character (space included) consumes, anything else, caret movement, blur, or `compositionstart` clears; accepted text is inserted as plain text with the caret's marks through the normal editor transaction so it autosaves and undoes as one step). `ghostTextController.ts` (`useGhostTextController` in `DocumentEditor`) applies `shouldTrigger` (idle delay from `AiSettings.ghostText.idleMs`, `GHOST_MIN_NEW_CHARS` 12, no request while pending or a ghost is visible, `GHOST_MAX_PER_DAY` 200 in memory), sends `ai:ghostText { nodeId, before (≤ 500), after (≤ 100), requestId }`, drops stale answers (newer id, document switched, text changed), turns the mode off with one toast on DISABLED/NO_KEY/INVALID_KEY, and backs off `GHOST_BACKOFF_MS` (30 s) after other failures; `resetGhostTextController()` in tests. `VibeWriteToggle.tsx` sits in the single-document toolbar (`aria-pressed`, disabled below Suggest with the reason). `AiSettings.ghostText = { enabled, idleMs }` (defaulted so old rows load; the idle-delay row, 0.5–5 s, lives under the dial section). Main: `src/main/ai/ghostText.ts` `generateGhostText` (dial gate first, then notes ≤ 500 chars, scene metadata, `resolvePreset` for temperature, max tokens ≤ 60, the style instruction and the new-elements rule; `fast` tier; context hash over window + notes + meta + preset) with `postProcessGhostText` (trim, strip wrapping quotes, cut to two sentences, drop a leading repeat of the text before the caret, join spacing); `src/main/ai/prompts/ghostText.v2.ts` (F-14.3; `ghostText.v1.ts` stays as shipped: rules → voice block (F-14.1) → preset → user turn: meta, the scene brief, notes, the window; the golden tests pin 707 of the old 1 500 budget for v1, 1 093 of the old 2 000 for v2 with a brief at every line cap, and 1 243 of 2 200 for v3 with the story bible at its 150-token ghost budget on top, F-14.9, and 1 362 for v4 with the scene steer at its budget too, F-14.13; the features send v4). `Provider.complete` takes `temperature`. The e2e fake chat endpoint branches on `response_format`: JSON → tags, text → the canned continuation. Ghost text does not stream (one ≤ 60-token `complete`, idle-triggered, so nobody waits on it); `PLAN.md` §3's "streaming everywhere users wait" applies to chat and rewrites (F-5.4, F-14.10). 2026-10-07: an answer that is empty with `finishReason: 'length'` (all reasoning) throws `AiCutOffError` (`cutOff`, code PROVIDER) and the handler annotates the inspector row; `GhostState.pin` marks a chat insertion (F-5.22): placed with `setGhost(…, { at, pin })`, grown with `streamGhost`, kept through caret moves, edits elsewhere (mapped; ends when its anchor is deleted), and blur; reported to `storage.listeners` (every exit, with its pin) rather than `onSettle`; the controller never sets or clears over a pinned ghost (`ownGhostShowing`). The widget draws `\n` as `<br>` (`white-space: normal`).
- Website (F-15.10): `site/public/` is the static site (plain HTML + one `styles.css`, no build step, no scripts; landing, `privacy.html`, `terms.html`, `docs/index.html` for the AI settings, `404.html`, `_headers` with a strict CSP; no `_redirects`: Workers assets refuse absolute sources, so `www` → apex is a zone Single Redirect rule created through the API, see below). It deploys as a Cloudflare Worker serving static assets (Pages is folded into Workers now; `wrangler pages` commands delegate and fail without a config): `site/wrangler.toml` pins the account, `workers_dev = false`, and the two `custom_domain` routes, so `npm run site:deploy` (`wrangler deploy --config site/wrangler.toml`, wrangler pinned as a devDependency) creates the DNS records and certificates itself. The `www` → apex 301 is a zone-level redirect rule (`http_request_dynamic_redirect` phase, expression `http.host eq "www.mythscribe.app"`), created once through the API with a token that has Zone → Single Redirect → Edit; the Worker's `www` route still exists so the record and certificate are managed with the rest. The token lives in the gitignored `.env` (`CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN`, `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`); `wrangler whoami` fails under it, real commands work. `api.mythscribe.app` is reserved for F-15.4: nothing is created for it. `src/shared/site.test.ts` (`npm run site:check`) pins the docs page to `AI_DIAL_LABEL`/`AI_DIAL_MEANING`/`AI_DATA_SHARING` cell by cell and checks every page's links, assets, and structure, so a copy change in `aiSettings.ts` fails until the docs page is edited by hand. Since the 2026-10-07 revamp: fonts are self-hosted woff2 in `site/public/fonts/` (Fraunces, Inter, OFL texts beside them) so the CSP stays `'self'`; download buttons are disabled `<button>`s reading "Coming soon" (no script, no link); the pricing section is one block between `PRICING BLOCK` comments in `index.html`. The product shots (`img/shot-*.webp` at 1600 and 800 wide, PNG fallback at 1600) come from `scripts/site-screenshots.mjs`: after `npm run build`, run it under `xvfb-run -a -s "-screen 0 1600x1000x24"` with `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` unset; it builds the demo novel "The Lantern Ferry" over IPC, answers AI from a loopback fake OpenAI (no key, no network), captures at 1440×900 ×2, and resizes in the app's own Chromium (no image dependency). Its fake server matches the system-prompt openings, so a prompt whose opening changes needs the script updated. The edit-pass report and the estimate are deliberately not captured (they compare with a human editor's rates). Recapture when the UI changes.
- Demo video (Lemon Squeezy store verification and the website; author decisions 2026-10-08): `npm run demo:video -- [--out <dir>] [--skip-build | --edit-only]` runs `scripts/demo-video.mjs`, which builds, re-runs itself with `--record` under `xvfb-run` on a 1920×1080 screen, then edits with ffmpeg from the pinned `ffmpeg-static` devDependency (electron-builder ships only `out/**` and production dependencies, so it never reaches the app bundle); each Node-heavy step goes through `flock /tmp/mythscribe-heavy.lock` where `flock` exists. The demo novel, the fake OpenAI (now with `agent`/`prose`/`delayMs`/`proseChunkMs` options for scripted chat steps and streamed drafts), `seedLanternFerry`, and `showSection` (the F-9.11 section picker) live in `scripts/lantern-ferry.mjs`, shared with `site-screenshots.mjs`. The recording is Playwright's `recordVideo` of a 1280×720 window at `--force-device-scale-factor=1.5` (a sharp 1080p); a drawn cursor, a hover outline, and a shortcut badge are injected into the page because recordings show no cursor. Sections are cut by wall-clock marks against the webm's `creation_time` (Playwright stamps it at recorder start); title cards are HTML in a second BrowserWindow (`capturePage`) with the site's fonts and palette. Outputs go outside the repo (default `<tmp>/mythscribe-demo/`): `mythscribe-demo.mp4` (H.264 yuv420p, ~90 s), `mythscribe-hero.webm` (VP9 yuv420p 720p loop, ~20 s, no cards), `stills/`, `raw/`. Its fake answers match system-prompt openings like the screenshot script's, so a prompt whose opening changes needs `lantern-ferry.mjs` updated.
- MythScribe account (F-15.2): `src/shared/cloudApi.ts` is the wire contract shared by the app and the Worker (`CLOUD_API_URL`, `EMAIL_MAX`, `LOGIN_ATTEMPT_TTL_MS` 15 min, `SESSION_TTL_MS` 90 days, `POLL_INTERVAL_MS` 3 s, `START_RATE_LIMIT` 3, the zod bodies of `/auth/start|poll|me|signout`, `CloudSession`, `CloudApiError` + `CLOUD_ERROR_STATUS`); `src/shared/account.ts` owns `AccountStatus` (`signedOut | pending | signedIn`, the token never crosses IPC) and `cloudApiUrl(env)` (`MYTHSCRIBE_CLOUD_API_URL` overrides the default for dev and e2e). Main: `src/main/account/cloudAuthClient.ts` is the typed fetch (15 s timeout, `AccountError { code, message, nextStep }`); `accountService.ts` is the one owner of the state machine and the 3 s poll timer (injected `Schedule`), persists the session as JSON in `AiKeyStore` under `SecretId` `'cloudSession'` (same `ai-keys.json` and safeStorage as the OpenAI key; no safe storage → IO before any request), maps errors to `AppError` (`INVALID_EMAIL` → VALIDATION, else IO with the next step in the message), and emits `account:changed` via `onChange` (constructed in `index.ts`, disposed on `will-quit`). Channels `account:getStatus|requestLink|cancelLink|signOut|refresh` in `handlers.ts`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/account/accountStore.ts` (status, busy, inline `error`; `subscribe()` mounted once in `App.tsx`) and `AccountSettingsTab.tsx` (the Account tab; dev link shown as `account-dev-link` with Copy when the Worker runs the `log` transport; `refresh()` on mount when `since` is null). The Worker lives in `cloud/` (`src/index.ts` router + `handleRequest(request, deps)` seam, `auth.ts` pure handlers, `store.ts` `Store` with `d1Store`/`memoryStore`, `email.ts` `resendMailer`/`logMailer`, `pages.ts`, `crypto.ts`; D1 `mythscribe` bound as `DB`, migrations under `cloud/migrations/`, `worker-configuration.d.ts` generated by `npm run cloud:types` and ignored by eslint/prettier); only SHA-256 hashes of the poll secret, link token, and session token are stored; links are single use, the session is handed to `/auth/poll` exactly once, and an unknown attempt id and a wrong secret answer identically. Scripts: `cloud:dev` (needs `cloud/.dev.vars` with `EMAIL_TRANSPORT=log` and a `--local` migrations apply), `cloud:migrate` (remote), `cloud:deploy`, `cloud:types`; the vitest `cloud` project and `cloud/tsconfig.json` are in the gates. Operator state 2026-09-19: live. The `.env` token has Account → D1 → Edit, migration 0001 is applied remotely, the Worker is deployed at `api.mythscribe.app`, Resend has the domain verified and `RESEND_API_KEY` is set (a new secret takes a few seconds to reach the running Worker; a 503 `NOT_CONFIGURED` right after `secret put` is propagation, not a bug). Gap: Google/Apple sign-in is F-15.12.
- Cloud credits (F-15.3): every balance and charge is an integer in micro-USD (`MICROS_PER_USD` in `src/shared/cloudRates.ts`, which also owns `CLOUD_RATE_MULTIPLIER` (2x `MODEL_PRICING`), `cloudRateFor`, `cloudChargeMicros` (ceil, min 1, throws for an unpriced model), and the published `CLOUD_RATES` table with the default tiers; the Worker imports the same module, so there is one rate). Wire shapes in `cloudApi.ts`: `CreditPack`, `CreditSpendRow`, `CreditsResult` (`GET /credits`), `CheckoutBody`/`CheckoutResult` (`POST /billing/checkout`, the Worker builds the Lemon Squeezy URL with `checkout[custom][user_id]` and `checkout[email]`; the app only names a `variantId`), `isCheckoutUrl` (https on `*.lemonsqueezy.com`), error codes `BAD_SIGNATURE` 401 and `INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS` 402. Worker: migration `0002_credits.sql` (`credits` balance per user, `credit_events` signed ledger with `order_ref` UNIQUE), `store.ts` `getBalance`/`applyCreditEvent` (one D1 `batch` = one transaction; a UNIQUE violation answers `'duplicate'` and nothing moves; `memoryStore` mirrors it)/`spendByFeature`; `credits.ts` `handleCredits`, `handleCheckout`, `handleLemonSqueezyWebhook` (raw body, HMAC-SHA256 `X-Signature` compared timing-safe via `hmacSha256Hex` in `crypto.ts`; `order_created` with status `paid` credits `priceCents` of the configured pack, `order_refunded` debits it, idempotent on `<event>:<order id>`; unreadable, unknown-user, unknown-variant, and unrelated events are a logged 200 because Lemon Squeezy retries every non-2xx), and the meter F-15.4 calls: `hasCredit` (balance > 0, the pre-flight gate) and `meterRequest` (charges after the answer, unconditionally, so the last request may overdraw slightly). `authenticate()` in `auth.ts` is the shared bearer check. Vars: `LEMONSQUEEZY_PACKS` (JSON `[{ variantId, url, priceCents }]`, validated with `ConfiguredPacks`; malformed → `[]` and one `console.error`; unset until the products exist) and the secret `LEMONSQUEEZY_WEBHOOK_SECRET`; `cloud:types` now writes `cloud/worker-configuration.d.ts` explicitly. The operator recipe (test-mode store, packs, webhook URL and events, the curl replay against `wrangler dev`) is in `cloud/README.md`. App: `cloudAuthClient.credits/checkout`, `accountService.credits()/checkoutUrl()` (signed out → IO with the sign-in step; UNAUTHORIZED forgets the session like `refresh()` and pushes `account:changed`; unknown pack → VALIDATION), channels `account:getCredits` and `account:buyCredits` (the handler refuses any URL `isCheckoutUrl` rejects, then `openExternal`; `menu:openExternal` stays mythscribe.app-only), `accountStore` `credits`/`creditsBusy`/`creditsError` (cleared whenever the status is not signedIn), and `CreditsSection` in `AccountSettingsTab.tsx` (balance `account-credit-balance`, `Buy $5.00` buttons or "not on sale yet", Refresh, spend table via `featureLabel` now in `ai/usageFormat.ts`, the rates table). The e2e fake Cloud API answers both routes. Gaps: auto-top-up was dropped (a subscription, later); `order_created` arriving unpaid (a delayed payment method) is ignored and Lemon Squeezy sends no later paid event, so such an order is credited by hand if it ever happens.
- Cloud proxy (F-15.4): one route, `POST /ai/complete`, wire in `src/shared/cloudApi.ts`: `AiCompleteBody` (`feature: AiFeatureId`, the resolved `model`, `messages`, `maxTokens`, `json?`, `temperature?`, `stream`; caps `AI_COMPLETE_MAX_TOKENS` 4 000, `AI_COMPLETE_MAX_MESSAGES` 64, `AI_COMPLETE_MAX_CHARS` 200 000), `AiCompleteResult` (`text`, `model`, `usage`, `chargeMicros`, `balanceMicros`), and for `stream: true` a 200 `AI_STREAM_CONTENT_TYPE` (`application/x-ndjson`) body of `AiStreamEvent` lines (`delta`s, then exactly one `done` with the usage and the charge, or one `error` when the upstream failed after the headers went out). New codes `BAD_REQUEST` 400 (unreadable body, over a cap, or a model outside `CLOUD_RATES`) and `UPSTREAM` 502. Worker: `cloud/src/ai.ts` `handleAiComplete(request, deps: AiDeps)` (`AiDeps extends CreditsDeps` with `upstream: Upstream | null`, built from the secret `OPENAI_API_KEY`; null → 503 `NOT_CONFIGURED`) in the order authenticate → validate → `hasCredit` (402) → upstream → `meterRequest` (the model that answered when it has a rate, else the requested one) → answer; `cloud/src/openai.ts` is the upstream seam (`openAiUpstream(key, fetch?, baseUrl?)`: Chat Completions over plain `fetch`, no SDK in the Worker bundle, `stream_options.include_usage`, the `sseLines` reader, `UpstreamError` kinds `rate_limit → 429 RATE_LIMITED`, `auth`/`network`/`other → 502 UPSTREAM`, never a body in an error). A client that stops reading a stream aborts the upstream and is charged nothing (the tokens already generated are bounded by `maxTokens`, the operator's cost); a stream that ends without a usage chunk still gets one `done` (zero usage, the 1-micro minimum); a store failure while metering (after the provider answered) ends the stream with one `INTERNAL` error event, the cause in the log, nothing charged; writes after a client cancel are dropped rather than thrown. One `console.log` per answered request (request id, user id, feature, model, token counts, charge, status; never content), allowed for `cloud/src/**` by an eslint override. App: `src/main/ai/providers/cloud.ts` `buildCloudProvider({ baseUrl, fetch, token, onSessionEnded, resolveModel, credits })` is the `Provider` with `id: 'cloud'`: bearer from the live `token()` (null → `AiSignedOutError`), `feature` required on the request, `CLOUD_COMPLETE_TIMEOUT_MS` 15 s on the non-streamed call, `ndjsonLines` over the web `ReadableStream` (partial lines, cancel on early exit), and one mapping: `UNAUTHORIZED` → `onSessionEnded()` + `SIGNED_OUT`, `INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS` → `NO_CREDIT`, `RATE_LIMITED` → `RATE_LIMIT`, everything else → `PROVIDER` (`MythScribe Cloud reported a problem (<code>).`), fetch failure → `NETWORK`; `price = cloudPriceFor` (`src/shared/cloudRates.ts`, `priceFor`'s shape at the Cloud rate, never throws); `testConnection` = `credits(token)` (balance ≤ 0 → `NO_CREDIT`) answering the fast model. `Provider.price?` and `CompletionRequest.feature?` are the interface additions (`providers/types.ts`, with `AiSignedOutError` and `AiNoCreditError`); `runAiRequest` uses `provider.price ?? deps.price` for both the daily-cap estimate and the ledger row, so every cost line under a proposal is at the Cloud rate with no change to the feature stores. Where a project's requests go is `AiSettings.source` (`AiSource = ownKey | cloud`, `AI_SOURCE_LABEL`/`AI_SOURCE_MEANING` in `src/shared/aiSettings.ts`, defaulted to `ownKey` so stored rows parse): `AiProviderRegistry.get(source)` answers the one lazily built Cloud provider (4th constructor argument, wired in `index.ts` with the same `createCloudAuthClient` the account uses, `account.sessionToken()`/`account.sessionEnded()`, and `models.cloud[tier]`) or the key path as before; `handlers.ts` binds `providers.get` to `ai.get(sourceOf(db))` per project and `ai:testConnection` reads the open project's source (none → `ownKey`). `SIGNED_OUT` and `NO_CREDIT` are hard codes in `indexQueue.ts` and turn-off codes in `ghostTextController.ts`. AI tab: an "AI source" radiogroup (`ai-source-ownKey`/`ai-source-cloud`) writes the setting; Cloud hides the key form and shows `ai-cloud-account` (signed in as … / not signed in), the Models group edits `status.models[source]`, Test connection needs a key or a session by source, the data-sharing table's Provider column follows the source, and `aiStore.test()` awaits `flushAiSettings()` first (main reads the source from the saved row, so a click inside the debounce answered for the old source). e2e: the fake Cloud API answers `/ai/complete` (JSON or NDJSON) behind the bearer, records `cloudAiRequests`, and a streamed Plan-mode chat runs through it with the fake OpenAI seeing nothing and the cost line at 2x `priceFor`. Operator state: `OPENAI_API_KEY` not yet set on the live Worker (`npx wrangler secret put OPENAI_API_KEY` from `cloud/`, then `npm run cloud:deploy`); until then the route is 503. Gaps: no per-user rate limit on the proxy; a cancelled stream is unmetered. The balance in `done` reaches the app through `onBalance` (F-15.5).
- Cloud billing (AI-BILLING-SPEC slice B2, 2026-10-07; supersedes the balance and metering parts of the two bullets above on the Worker side): migration `cloud/migrations/0005_billing.sql` adds the append-only `ledger_entries` (types `topup | trial_grant | charge | refund | adjustment`, unique `idempotency_key`, charge details incl. cached tokens, provider cost, `markup_bps`; triggers refuse UPDATE/DELETE; seeded from `credit_events`, while `credits`/`credit_events` stay unread), `holds` (`active | settled | released`, prices locked per hold, unique per account + `Idempotency-Key`), `billing_config` (one JSON value per key over `DEFAULT_BILLING_CONFIG` in `cloud/src/config.ts`), `rate_limits`, `access_tokens`, and the sign-in code columns. Available balance = ledger sum − active unexpired holds (`Store.getBalance`); the balance check and the hold are one conditional `INSERT … SELECT … WHERE available >= amount` (`PLACE_HOLD`, `cloud/src/store.ts`); a Cron Trigger (`*/5`) runs `runScheduledSweep`. Pricing math is integer/BigInt in `src/shared/cloudBilling.ts` (shared with the app: `lockPrices`, `chargeMicros` = ceil(cost × (1 + markup)), `holdMicros`, `inputTokenUpperBound` = UTF-8 bytes + template), price table ids are OpenRouter's (`openai/gpt-5.4-mini` …, bare names accepted as aliases). Gateway: `openRouterUpstream` (`OPENROUTER_API_KEY`) else OpenAI direct with `openAiModelId`. New routes: `GET /pricing` (public `PricingResult`), `GET /usage` (paged ledger), `POST /auth/refresh` (15-min access tokens from the session = refresh token), `POST /auth/verify` (6-digit code); `/credits` adds `heldMicros`; `/ai/complete` adds `Idempotency-Key` and codes `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE` 413, `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` 422, `DUPLICATE_REQUEST` 409; the webhook adds `STALE_WEBHOOK`, partial refunds by `refunded_amount`, and the $30 app license (`LEMONSQUEEZY_APP_LICENSE`, same license row as the Supporter). Tests run the production SQL on `node:sqlite` with every migration applied (`cloud/src/testing/sqliteD1.ts`; the in-memory store is gone). Known gap until slice B3: the app still prices Cloud at `cloudRates.ts` (2x), sends no `Idempotency-Key`, and uses the session token as the bearer (still accepted).
- Starter pack and refunds (author 2026-10-08): migration `cloud/migrations/0006_starter_refunds.sql` adds `users.email_verified_at` (backfilled from `created_at`; `completeSignIn` sets it, `Store.markEmailVerified` for an unverified row), `starter_purchases` (PK `user_id`, UNIQUE `order_id`, never deleted), and index `ledger_entries_user_order`. `cloud/src/credits.ts`: `Variant.kind` `pack | starter | license`; the starter (`LEMONSQUEEZY_STARTER`) skips `min_pack_usd`, `starterRefusal` gates checkout and `/credits.starter`; the webhook credits a starter through `Store.creditStarter` (one batch: claim + conditional top-up → `applied | duplicate | refused`), and a refused one is refunded in full via `cloud/src/lemonSqueezy.ts` (`lemonSqueezyApi(LEMONSQUEEZY_API_KEY)`, injected as `deps.lemonSqueezy`, outcome `refunded | refused | unknown`). `handleRefund` (`POST /billing/refund`): `findOrder` → repeat answer from `findRefundHold` (`refund:<order>` key in `holds`, feature `refund`) → window → amount = floor-to-cents(min(ledger − held − `grantedMicros`, min(paid, top-up) − refunds)), where paid is `ledger_entries.paid_micros` (0006; the webhook records `total_usd − tax_usd` via `Store.creditOrder`/`creditStarter`; NULL = the credit; `OrderRow.creditedMicros`/`paidMicros`) → `placeRefundHold` (SQL `REFUND_LIMITS` re-checks both limits) → API → `settleRefund` (the webhook's `order_refunded:<order>:<cumulative cents>` key, `REFUND_ORDER` caps at the order's top-up) / `releaseHold` on 4xx / hold kept `REFUND_HOLD_MS` (24 h) on no answer. `refundOrder` (webhook) now caps at the order's top-up, scopes by account, ignores orders with no top-up, and settles a running refund hold for the order in the same batch only when the order's refunds reach `holds.refund_base_micros` (the order's refunds when the hold was placed, 0006) + the hold, so a replayed or smaller refund webhook leaves it running. `lemonSqueezyApi` times out at 12 s (`REFUND_TIMEOUT_MS`), under the app's 15 s `CLOUD_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. `/ai/complete` refusals carry `CloudApiError.offer` (`CreditOffer`: starter | packs + negative). Shared: `RefundableOrder`, `RefundBody`/`RefundResult`, `CreditsResult.starter|refunds` (defaulted for older Workers), code `NOT_ELIGIBLE` 409, `starterOfferText`/`noBalanceText` in `src/shared/hostedPricing.ts`. App: `CloudAuthClient.refund` (server copy passed through, `NOT_ELIGIBLE` always), `AccountService.refund` (`NOT_ELIGIBLE` → VALIDATION), channel `account:refund` (`read` access, works read-only), the cloud provider maps the offer into `AiNoCreditError`'s message; `BalanceSection` shows `account-starter` and the `account-refunds` list with a two-step Refund button; `accountStore.refund` reloads credits. Tests: `cloud/src/refund.test.ts`, `lemonSqueezy.test.ts`; e2e checks the offer and one refund.
- Hosted UX and client switch-over (AI-BILLING-SPEC slice B3b, 2026-10-07; supersedes the app-side pricing, wording, and bearer parts of the bullets above and below): **one pricing shape**, `PricingResult` (`GET /pricing`, `src/shared/cloudApi.ts`); B1's `CloudPricing` is gone, `CloudPricingCache`/`AiModelChoice.cloudPricing` hold `PricingResult`, `hostedAutoTable` (`aiRouting.ts`) turns its `routing.features` into the Auto table. `src/shared/cloudRates.ts` is deleted (`MICROS_PER_USD` now in `cloudBilling.ts`, which also owns `DEFAULT_BILLING_TERMS` spread by the Worker's config). `src/shared/hostedPricing.ts` is the app side: `bundledPricing()` (the Worker's defaults until `/pricing` answers), `hostedPriceFor` (exactly the Worker's `chargeMicros`), `hostedQuote` (uncached charge × safety factor, `needsConfirm` above the threshold), `multiplierLabel`, `hostedModelFor` (a tier left at `HOSTED_DEFAULT_MODELS` or `LEGACY_HOSTED_DEFAULT_MODELS` follows `routing.tiers`), `wordsCovered` (null while the constant is). Defaults approved 2026-10-07: `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash` / `-v4-pro` for `OPENROUTER_DEFAULT_MODELS`, `HOSTED_DEFAULT_MODELS`, `DEFAULT_HOSTED_MODELS` (OpenAI models stay on sale with multipliers), priced in `OPENROUTER_PRICING`. Tokens (A5, S6): `src/main/account/accessTokens.ts` — `CloudAccessTokens` (in-memory access token per session, minted by `cloudAuthClient.refresh`, renewed 60 s before expiry, concurrent calls share one refresh, NOT_FOUND → the session token for an older Worker) and `withAccessTokens(client, tokens)`, which `index.ts` wraps around the client so `AccountService` keeps passing the session token unchanged (one retry with a fresh token on a 401; sign-out still sends the session token, revoking all). The Cloud adapter takes `bearer`/`invalidateBearer`/`idempotencyKey` (`Idempotency-Key`, same on the retry) and maps the new codes (`AiTooLargeError`, `AiModelUnavailableError`; `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` stops the index queue and ghost text). Keychain-only session: `AiKeyStore.setKey('cloudSession')` refuses the plain fallback (`NO_KEYCHAIN_SESSION_MESSAGE`) and `requestLink` checks `canStoreProviderKey()` first; `MYTHSCRIBE_E2E_PLAINTEXT_KEYS` is honoured only when `!app.isPackaged`. Channels `account:getUsage` (`UsageResult`, cursor) and `account:getPricing` (refreshes when an hour old via the optional `cloudPricing` handler dep; null before any answer). Renderer: `accountStore` `pricing`, `usage`/`usageCursor`/`usageBusy`/`usageError`, `loadPricing`, `loadUsage(more)`, a window-focus refresh of the balance and table (at most every 10 s, one listener at a time, removed by `resetAccountStore`); `features/account/hostedPricing.ts` (`useHostedPricing`, `hostedPricingNow`, `useHostedSource`); `BalanceSection.tsx` (the Account tab's balance: `account-balance`, `account-words-left`, `account-balance-warning`, `Add $10.00` packs, `account-pack-example-<variant>`, `account-privacy`, `account-terms`, spend table without tokens, `MythScribe Cloud models` with `account-model-price-<id>`, `account-usage-history`); `BalanceNotice.tsx` (was `CreditNotice`; `balance-notice`, always on Cloud, `data-warning` when warning); copy in `creditMeter.ts`; `ai/RequestCost.tsx` renders `describeRequest(request, !hosted)` under every answer; `EditPassWorkspace` quotes hosted passes and confirms above the threshold (`edit-pass-quote`), `importStore.estimateFor` and the `library:estimate` handler quote hosted jobs. Gaps: the Worker still accepts the session token as a bearer (S6 not fully in force until it stops); single requests and background indexing are never quoted; a stored plain-text session from before is still read.
- Usage meter and caps (F-15.5): `src/shared/cloudUsage.ts` is the one owner of the period and the warnings, imported by the app and the Worker: `USAGE_PERIOD_DAYS` 30 (a rolling window; prepaid credits have no billing cycle), `LOW_BALANCE_MICROS` (1.00 USD), `RUN_OUT_WARNING_DAYS` 3, `periodSpentMicros`, `projectedDaysLeft` (whole days at the pace since the first charge in the period, 1 to 30 active days, so a new account is not averaged over thirty; null with no spend, 0 at a balance ≤ 0), and `creditWarning` (`empty` → `low` → `runOut`). Hard caps are what already existed, nothing new: the Worker's `hasCredit` refusal at a balance ≤ 0 and the app's daily USD cap in `runAiRequest`, which prices Cloud requests at the Cloud rate; there is no server-side author cap, no migration, no new route. Wire: `CreditsResult` gains `periodDays`, `periodSpend`, `periodFirstChargeAt` (zod defaults, so the app still reads a Worker deployed before them; `spend` stays lifetime). Worker: `store.spendByFeature(userId, since = 0)` and `store.firstChargeAt(userId, since)` (d1 and memory, covered by the `(user_id, created_at)` index), `handleCredits` windows on `deps.now() - USAGE_PERIOD_MS`. Live balance: the Cloud provider's `onBalance?` option fires from `complete` and the stream's `done` (never on a failure), `index.ts` emits the event `account:balanceChanged { balanceMicros }`, and `accountStore.subscribe()` (one unsubscribe for both account events) replaces `credits.balanceMicros`, or loads credits once when none are held and the account is signed in; `creditsAt` is the store's "now" for the projection (eslint's `react-hooks/purity` refuses `Date.now()` in render). UI: `features/account/creditMeter.ts` owns the copy (`creditWarningText`, `runOutText`); `UsageMeter` in the Account tab's Credits section (`account-period-spent`, `account-run-out`, `account-credit-warning`, the per-feature table on `periodSpend` captioned "Last 30 days", `account-all-time` for the lifetime total); `CreditNotice.tsx` is the status-bar button (`credit-notice`, rendered last in `StatusBar`), shown only when the project's `AiSettings.source` is `cloud`, the account is signed in, and a warning applies; it loads credits once on mount and opens Settings on the Account tab through `useShellDialogStore.show('settings', 'account')` (`settingsTab`, cleared on close; `SettingsDialog` `initialTab`, ignored when that tab is not visible). e2e: the fake Worker keeps a balance (`CLOUD_BALANCE_MICROS` 1 003 000, so one charged chat crosses 1.00 USD) and the notice appears with no Settings visit and no Refresh. Gaps: only the balance is live, `periodSpend` is as old as the last `/credits` load (the Account tab reloads on mount); the live Worker serves the period fields only after the next `npm run cloud:deploy`.
- AI source choice (F-15.11): the source itself is F-15.4's `AiSettings.source` and the AI-tab picker; this feature added the two other places it shows. `project:create` takes an optional `aiSource` (`AiSource`); the handler writes it into the new project's AI settings row right after the synchronous `manager.create` and before it answers, so the renderer's first `aiSettings:get` already reads it (no renderer-side write, no race with the store's load). `projectStore.create(name, format, directory?, aiSource?)` passes it through. `CreateProjectWizard` is four steps since F-5.18 (`STEPS`: name, format, source, dial; the header reads `Step n of 4`; Back follows `PREVIOUS`): step 3 lists `AiSource.options` with `AI_SOURCE_LABEL`/`AI_SOURCE_MEANING`, defaults to `ownKey`, and a hint (`wizard-source-hint`) says what the choice needs next (the key under Settings › AI, or sign-in and credits under Settings › Account; `signedInEmail` comes from `WelcomeScreen`, which reads the app-wide `accountStore`) and that it can be switched later. In `AiSettingsTab`, `ModelField` renders `CloudRateLine` (`ai-model-rate-<tier>`) only while the source is `cloud`: the saved model's rate from `cloudRateFor` (the one table in `cloudRates.ts`), or a warning for a model outside the table, which the proxy refuses (`UNKNOWN_MODEL`). Cloud gate (decided by the author 2026-10-07, own key until Cloud launches): `CLOUD_AI_AVAILABLE` in `src/shared/cloudApi.ts` (false until the Worker with `/ai/complete` is deployed and the store exists). While false: `CreateProjectWizard` and `AiSettingsTab` (both take an optional `cloudAvailable` prop defaulting to the flag, so unit tests can drive both states) render the Cloud option disabled with `CLOUD_COMING_SOON` (`wizard-cloud-coming-soon`, `ai-source-cloud-coming-soon`); a project still stored on `cloud` keeps it (never rewritten) and the AI tab shows `ai-cloud-unavailable` (`CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE_NOTICE`) instead of the account line, with Test connection disabled; `CreditNotice` renders nothing; `buildCloudProvider` (`available` option, default the flag) throws `AiCloudUnavailableError` (`CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE`, a hard code in `indexQueue.ts` and a turn-off code in `ghostTextController.ts`) from `complete`, `stream`, and `testConnection` before any fetch. Regardless of the flag, a Cloud `NOT_FOUND` maps to `CLOUD_UNAVAILABLE` ("isn’t reachable for AI yet") instead of `PROVIDER (NOT_FOUND)`. The e2e creates the smoke project on the own key and checks Cloud disabled in the wizard and the AI tab (signed in too); the streamed Cloud chat step and the Cloud rate lines are out of the e2e until the flag flips (the fake Worker's `/ai/complete` stays, `cloudAiRequests` is asserted empty).
- Local models (F-5.15; no migration): `AiSource` gains `local` and `AI_PROVIDER_IDS` gains `local` (the ledger's `provider` column is a Drizzle text enum, so no SQL change); `providerForSource` maps a source to its model map/ledger id (used by the AI tab, the dial's data-sharing table, and the import estimate). `src/shared/ai.ts` owns `LOCAL_DEFAULT_MODELS`, `defaultModelsFor`, `LocalAiBaseUrl` (http/https, ≤ 300), `LocalAiSettings`, `isLoopbackUrl`; `AiModels.local` and `AppState.localAi` are defaulted for older files; `AiStatus.local` carries the address. `buildOpenAiProvider` takes `baseURL`/`id`/`label` (a compatible server gets `max_tokens`; `mapOpenAiError(err, label)` names it; a 404 there says to pull the model). `registry.ts` `buildLocalProvider` (placeholder key, `price` always $0) is built by `AiProviderRegistry.get('local')` from the live `local()` settings and rebuilt when the address changes. `ai:setLocalEndpoint` stores the address without a trailing slash and runs `backfillSummaries(true)`. Renderer: `aiStore.setLocalEndpoint`, `AiSettingsTab` `LocalEndpointForm` (Server address, Save, `LOCAL_QUALITY_WARNING` as `ai-local-warning`, an off-machine alert), the privacy line by source, Reset to defaults per provider; the wizard's hint for the local source. E2E: the local source pointed at the fake provider answers Test connection and a Plan turn at $0.0000.
- Assisted first run (F-5.18): the wizard's fourth step lists `AI_DIAL_LEVELS` with `AI_DIAL_LABEL`/`AI_DIAL_MEANING`, an explainer of the background work (`wizard-dial-explainer`), and preselects `RECOMMENDED_DIAL` (`AI_DATA_SHARING.summary.minDial`, i.e. Ask) with a Recommended badge; Off is one click. `project:create` takes an optional `aiDial` written in the same settings write as `aiSource` (omitted keeps Off, so `project:open` and every older caller are unchanged). `App.tsx`'s `onCreate` opens the assistant panel (`layoutStore.toggle('assistant')`) when the chosen dial reaches `query`'s `minDial` and the panel is closed; the layout is app-wide, so this is the only layout write and opening an existing project never touches it. Query mode and the quick actions need nothing new: a fresh project has no conversations, a new one starts in `query`, and `QuickActions` always renders. App tests' `fillWizard` picks Off unless told otherwise, and the e2e picks Off so the rest of the smoke run starts at dial 0 as before. Since 2026-10-06 (all AI in the assistant): the row is the Actions menu (`AiActionsMenu`).
- Updates (F-15.7): `src/shared/updates.ts` owns `UPDATE_CHANNELS` (`stable | beta`, `updaterChannelFor` → `latest | beta`), `UpdateSettings` (`channel`, `autoCheck`, `installedNotes`, `lastSeenVersion`; stored in app-state.json under `updates`), the `UpdateStatus` union (`unsupported | idle | checking | upToDate | downloading | ready | error`), `UpdateState`, `releaseNotesText` (GitHub's HTML → plain text, capped at `RELEASE_NOTES_MAX`; notes are never rendered as HTML), and the check timing (10 s after start, then every 6 h). Main: `src/main/updates/updateService.ts` is the one owner, built like `AccountService` (injected `AppUpdaterLike`, `Schedule` from `src/main/schedule.ts`, `onChange` → `updates:changed`, disposed on `will-quit`); `autoUpdater.ts` is the only file that imports `electron-updater` (a named import: the package has no default export). `index.ts` passes `updater: null` with a reason for an unpackaged build, `NODE_ENV=test`, and a Linux build that is not an AppImage. It sets `channel` and then `allowDowngrade = false` (assigning a channel turns downgrades on), downloads in the background, never restarts by itself (`autoInstallOnAppQuit`), keeps `ready` until the install instead of checking again, stores the downloaded version's notes so the next run can show "What's new" (`unseenNotes` is derived, cleared by `markSeen`), and shows only the first line of an updater error. Channels `updates:getState|check|setChannel|setAutoCheck|markSeen|install`; `updates:install` refuses while a project is open, so the renderer's `installNow()` goes through `closeProjectWithConfirm` first (the installer starts before the process exits). Renderer: `src/renderer/features/updates/` has `updateStore.ts` (`subscribe()` mounted once in `App.tsx`), `UpdatesSettingsTab.tsx` (the app-scope Updates tab, also on the welcome screen; `ReleaseNotesView`), and `UpdateNotice.tsx` in the header (`update-notice`: update ready, or what's new once after an update). Help › Check for updates… opens the tab and checks. Packaging: `electron-builder.yml` publishes to GitHub as a draft with `generateUpdatesFilesForAllChannels`, mac `dmg` + `zip` with the hardened runtime; Windows signing values arrive only as `-c.win.azureSignOptions.*` flags from `.github/workflows/release.yml`, which creates the draft from `docs/release-notes/<tag>.md`, builds the three platforms, verifies the Authenticode signature and the notarization staple, and publishes the draft last (`--prerelease` for a `-beta.N` tag). Recipe: `docs/RELEASING.md`. Known gaps: the real download → restart → install path and the workflow have never run (no signing accounts yet); the e2e covers the unsupported-build tab and the stored channel.
- Diagnostics (F-15.8): opt-in, off on install, no install ID, no Sentry, no Electron `crashReporter` (minidumps hold process memory and cannot be scrubbed). `src/shared/diagnostics.ts` owns the counter enum (`DIAGNOSTIC_COUNTERS`: `app.launch`, `project.create|open`, `export.run`, `focus.enter`, `proposal.accept|reject`, `ai.request.<AiFeatureId>`; a name outside it cannot be stored or sent), `CrashReport` (`main | renderer | processGone`), the scrubbers (`scrubMessage`: quoted text, URLs, emails, paths → placeholders, 300 chars; `scrubStack`: app-relative frames under the given roots, everything else `<external>`, function names dropped, 20 frames), `DiagnosticsSettings` (`enabled`, `counts` per day, `queue` ≤ 10, `lastSentDay`; app-state.json under `diagnostics`), and `DiagnosticsState` (`pending` is the pretty-printed JSON that would be sent: `diagnostics.ts` must not import `cloudApi.ts`, which imports it for `DiagnosticsBody`). Main: `src/main/diagnostics/diagnosticsService.ts` is the one owner, built like `UpdateService` (injected `send`, `Schedule`, `onChange` → `diagnostics:changed`): nothing is recorded while off, switching either way clears counts and queue, `count()` emits no event, `flush()` (30 s after start, then every 6 h) sends only completed days plus the queue and keeps everything silently on failure, counts older than 14 days are pruned. `diagnosticsClient.ts` is the typed `POST /diagnostics` (no auth header, 15 s timeout, trims to `DIAGNOSTICS_BODY_MAX` oldest rows first; the sender is null under `NODE_ENV=test` unless `MYTHSCRIBE_CLOUD_API_URL` is set); `crashHandlers.ts` reports `uncaughtException`/`unhandledRejection` and then shows Electron's error box itself (a listener suppresses the default one). Count hooks are one line each: `app.launch` and `focus.enter` (the `enter-full-screen` listener) in `index.ts`; create/open, `export.run`, `proposal:settle`, and the ledger insert (so a cache hit counts, like F-5.14) in `handlers.ts`. Channels `diagnostics:getState|setEnabled|reportRendererError`. Renderer: `src/renderer/features/diagnostics/` (`diagnosticsStore.ts` subscribed once in `App.tsx`, `DiagnosticsSettingsTab.tsx` the app-scope Diagnostics tab with the "See exactly what would be sent" preview, `rendererErrors.ts` the `window` `error`/`unhandledrejection` listeners called from `main.tsx`, capped per session). Worker: `cloud/src/diagnostics.ts` (`handleDiagnostics`: unauthenticated, 32 KB cap, zod body, per-counter clamp, 204) upserts aggregates only into `diagnostic_counts` and `diagnostic_crashes` (grouped by `crashFingerprint` = SHA-256 of name + top 5 frames + version; migration `0003_diagnostics.sql`); no IP is stored and there is no code-side rate limit (zone rate limiting is the dashboard option). The operator reads reports with the two `wrangler d1 execute` queries in `cloud/README.md`.
- Developer tools (2026-10-07, `plans/plan-devtools.md`): an app-wide switch, `devTools` in app-state.json (off, read leniently), in Settings › Advanced (`AdvancedSettingsTab.tsx`, tab id `advanced`). `src/shared/devtools.ts` owns the vocabulary: `DevLogEntry` (`error|warn`, source `main|renderer|ipc|ai`), `DevAiRequest` (feature, provider, model, tier, prompt version, status `running|ok|cached|failed|cancelled|skipped`, `waitMs`/`firstTokenMs`/`totalMs`, tokens in/out/cached/reasoning, cap, cost, finish reason, answer length, error code, `note`), `GHOST_SKIP_REASONS` with labels, caps `DEV_LOG_MAX` 500 / `DEV_AI_MAX` 200 / `DEV_TEXT_MAX`, `DEVTOOLS_CHORD` (Ctrl+Shift+D), `redactSecrets`/`redactSettings`, and the pure `formatDiagnosticsReport`. Main `src/main/devtools/devToolsService.ts` is the one owner of the switch and both ring buffers (memory only; switching off drops them; every recording call is a no-op while off): `record`/`recordError`, `ipcFailure` (every error envelope: `createHandler`/`register` in `registry.ts` take an optional `FailureObserver`, `handlers.ts` passes it; the `[ipc]` console line, `IPC_LOG_PREFIX` in `errors.ts`, is skipped by the console hook), `hookConsole` (main's `console.error/warn`, installed once in `index.ts`), `observer` (the request path's `AiRequestDeps.observe`: `runAiRequest`/`runAiStream` call `start → prepared → sent → firstToken → done|failed`; the prompt and raw answer are held beside the row for `devtools:requestText`), `ghostSkip`, `ghostEmpty` (the `ai:ghostText` handler notes an answer post-processing emptied, with raw length, finish reason, output tokens against the cap, and reasoning tokens), `openChromium` (refused while off). Providers report `finishReason` and `usage.reasoningTokens` (`completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens`) for it. `handlers.ts` wraps `aiFailure` to log DISABLED, which never reaches the path. Channels `devtools:getState|setEnabled|snapshot|requestText|log|ghostSkip|clear|openChromium|report` (all `read`); events `devtools:changed|logAdded|requestChanged` (pushed only while on); `index.ts` rebuilds the native menu on change (`installApplicationMenu` now takes `devTools` and returns `{ dispose, rebuild }`). Menu: `menuFor({ devTools })` appends `DEVELOPER_MENU_ENTRIES` (`openDeveloperTools`, `openChromiumDevTools`) to Help; `MenuBar.tsx` and main both use it. Renderer `src/renderer/features/devtools/`: `devToolsStore.ts` (mirror of main's buffers plus local `open` and revealed `texts`; subscribed once in `App.tsx`), `DevToolsPanel.tsx` (a bottom drawer over the layout, tabs AI requests / Log, filters, clear, Copy diagnostics via `navigator.clipboard`, Chromium DevTools), `rendererDevLog.ts` (`listenForDevLog` from `main.tsx`: window errors, rejections, console wraps; `reportGhostSkip`, which `ghostTextController.ts` calls once per change of reason from `throttleReason` in `aiThrottle.ts` and its own checks). `DevToolsShortcut` in `App.tsx` binds the chord only while on. E2E: the step before Flexible nesting turns it on, opens the panel from Help, and fails a ghost-text probe on the fake server (`DEVTOOLS_FAIL_SENTINEL` → HTTP 500 → PROVIDER) through the local source.
- Supporter license (F-15.9, absorbs F-15.6): `src/shared/license.ts` is the one owner of the token format and the extras, imported by the Worker, main, and the renderer: `LICENSE_GRACE_DAYS` 14 (`LICENSE_GRACE_MS`), `LICENSE_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS` 24 h, `LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY_JWK` (Ed25519 public JWK; a documented placeholder that verifies nothing until the operator pastes the real one; `licensePublicKey(env)` takes `MYTHSCRIBE_LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY` first, for dev and e2e), `LicenseClaims` `{ v: 1, sub: userId, iat, exp }` (epoch ms), the base64url helpers, `encodeLicensePayload` / `formatLicenseToken` / `parseLicenseToken` (`base64url(JSON claims).base64url(signature)`; parsing never verifies), `ACCENTS` (`default | ember | sky | rose | gold | violet`, the three `--ms-accent*` values each), `SupporterSettings` (`token`, `refreshedAt`, `accent`; in `app-state.json` under `supporter`, plain JSON because the token is signed) and `SupporterStatus` (`licensed`, `since`, `validUntil`, `offline`, `product`, `accent`; the token never crosses IPC). Wire: `LicenseResult` (`GET /license` → `{ token | null, product: CreditPack | null }`) in `cloudApi.ts`; the Supporter product is bought through the existing `POST /billing/checkout` by its variant. Worker: migration `0004_supporter.sql` (`supporter_licenses` one row per user, `order_ref` UNIQUE, `granted_at`, `revoked_at`), `store.ts` `grantSupporter` (`'applied' | 'duplicate'`; a new order after a refund re-grants) / `revokeSupporter` / `findSupporter`, `license.ts` `handleLicense` (bearer; signs a fresh token per call with `importSigningKey`/`signEd25519` in `crypto.ts` (Web Crypto `Ed25519`); `token: null` without an active license; 503 `NOT_CONFIGURED` only for a licensed account with no key), `credits.ts` resolves a variant from packs ∪ supporter, so the webhook grants on a paid `order_created` and revokes on `order_refunded` for that variant and never books a credit event for it; the checkout message is `UNKNOWN_VARIANT` now. Vars: `LEMONSQUEEZY_SUPPORTER` (`{ variantId, url, priceCents }`) and the secret `LICENSE_SIGNING_KEY` (private JWK); `npm run cloud:license-keygen` (`cloud/scripts/licenseKeygen.mjs`) prints both halves; the operator recipe is in `cloud/README.md`. Main: `licenseVerifier.ts` `verifyLicenseToken(token, jwk, now)` (`node:crypto` `createPublicKey({ format: 'jwk' })` + `verify(null, …)`; expired, forged, malformed, or unusable key → null); `cloudAuthClient.license`; `AccountService` owns the license because it follows the account: `supporter()` (cache only; a token counts only when it verifies *and* its `sub` is the signed-in `userId`; `offline` when no refresh reached the Worker within the interval), `refreshLicense()`, `supporterCheckoutUrl()` (the variant from the last `/license` answer, kept in memory), `setAccent()` (VALIDATION for a non-default accent without the license), a quiet refresh on construction when signed in, after `signIn`, and every `LICENSE_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS` on the injected `Schedule` (a token that does not verify is refused and the cached one kept; an unreachable Worker leaves the cache), `forget()` (sign-out, 401) clears the token and pushes `account:supporterChanged` (the accent choice stays stored, reported as `default` until licensed again). In-flight answers are matched to the session by token (`sameSession`), not by object: `refresh()` replaces the state object, and a `/license` answer racing the Account tab's mount-time `refresh()` was dropped before that (the e2e caught it, failing on the badge about half the time). Channels `account:getSupporter|refreshSupporter|buySupporter|setAccent` in `handlers.ts` (buy refuses any URL `isCheckoutUrl` rejects, then `openExternal`), event `account:supporterChanged`. Renderer: `accountStore` `supporter`/`supporterBusy`/`supporterError` (loaded by `App` at start, signed out too; not cleared by the store on sign-out, main pushes that), `SupporterSection` in `AccountSettingsTab.tsx` (`account-supporter-badge`, `account-supporter-buy`, a Refresh named "Refresh license", the offline line), `AccentPicker.tsx` (`account-accent-<id>`, non-default swatches disabled without the license), `App` sets `document.documentElement.dataset.accent` (deleted for `default`), and `tokens.css` holds the `:root[data-accent=…]` blocks (hexes duplicated from `ACCENTS`; change both). e2e: the fake Cloud API signs with `e2e/fixtures/license-test-key.json` (throwaway; never the Worker's key) and the app is launched with that public half. Operator steps: keygen → `wrangler secret put LICENSE_SIGNING_KEY` → paste the public JWK into `license.ts` → the $39 product and `LEMONSQUEEZY_SUPPORTER` → `cloud:migrate` (0004) → `cloud:deploy`; until then `/license` answers `{ token: null, product: null }` (404 on the live Worker before the deploy) and the tab says the license is not on sale yet.
- Document zoom and interface size (F-7.10): two app-wide view settings, both in `appState.view` (`ViewSettings` in `src/shared/zoom.ts`: `editorZoom: ZoomFactor` default 1, `uiScale: UiScale` default `medium`). `zoom.ts` also owns `ZOOM_STEPS` (0.67–2, ten steps), `nextZoom` (snaps an off-table value to the nearest step first, stays put at the ends), `formatZoom` (`'125 %'`), `UI_SCALES`/`UI_SCALE_FACTORS` (`small` 0.9, `medium` 1, `large` 1.15), `UI_SCALE_LABELS`. Main owns the values: `view:get`, `view:zoomDocument { step }` (steps and persists, touches no window), `view:setUiScale { scale }` (persists, then `setZoomFactor` on every live window) in `handlers.ts`; `createWindow(uiScale)` in `index.ts` applies the factor on `ready-to-show` before `show()`. Renderer: `features/shell/viewStore.ts` (`load()` at App mount, `zoomDocument(step)` invokes and toasts `Document zoom 110 %`, `setUiScale` invokes silently, `useEditorZoom()`); `features/shell/zoom.ts` is the pure input side (`zoomStepFor(event)` for Ctrl+= / Ctrl+- / Ctrl+0 and `+`; `wheelZoomStepFor(event, now, lastAt)` for Ctrl+wheel, Ctrl only, `deltaY` sign, one step per `WHEEL_ZOOM_COALESCE_MS` 150 ms burst so a wheel notch or a trackpad pinch is one step); `ZoomShortcuts` in `App.tsx` (always mounted, capture phase; `keydown` and a non-passive `wheel` listener, both `preventDefault` + `stopPropagation`, so the editor never sees the chord and Chromium never zooms the window itself). The page scales through `editorStyle(settings, zoom)` in `features/editor/column.ts` (`maxWidth × zoom`, `fontSize × zoom`; line height is unitless and the spacings are em, so they follow), read by `DocumentEditor` and `StackedEditor`; in focus mode the `%` max-width override stays and only the font scales; the notes editor and the Settings preview are chrome and never zoom. Interface size scales everything (the page included; document zoom multiplies on top). Shortcuts `zoomIn`/`zoomOut`/`zoomReset` in `APP_SHORTCUTS` and the three View items (`when: 'always'`) carry the chords as native accelerators, the fallback when the page does not claim the key; `runMenuAction` routes them above the project gate. Settings › Appearance (`AppearanceSettingsTab.tsx`, app scope): the three-size radiogroup `appearance-ui-scale-<id>` and the document zoom row `appearance-document-zoom` with − / + / Reset. Fit at Large in 1280 × 720 (CSS viewport 1113 × 626) was measured with a scratch Playwright script and fixed where it failed: labelled buttons in the toolbar, tag bar, metadata pane, and summary block are `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap` (a label never breaks mid-word; rows wrap between buttons), the metadata pane's Brief and Summary rows are `flex-wrap` (the pane used to scroll sideways at Medium too), the toolbar's right cluster wraps, and the settings tab strip wraps (six tabs overflowed the 640 px dialog at every size). Known and left: the sidebar's create bar (`CreateNodeBar.tsx`) wraps its three labels at narrow widths; making them nowrap turns the bar into two rows even at the default window, so it needs a design call. e2e: Ctrl+= → font 17.6 px and column 770 px with `getZoomFactor()` still 1, Ctrl+wheel → one more step, Appearance → Large → `getZoomFactor()` 1.15 with the document untouched, Ctrl+0, and `view: { editorZoom: 1, uiScale: 'medium' }` in app-state.json.
- App license and trial (F-15.13, AI-BILLING-SPEC M1, slice B3a 2026-10-07): `src/shared/appAccess.ts` owns `APP_TRIAL_DAYS` 30, `TRIAL_ENDED_MESSAGE`, `AppTrial` (`startedAt`, `lastSeenAt`; `trial` in `app-state.json`, `.nullable().catch(null)`), `AppAccess` (`state: trial | licensed | expired`, `trialEndsAt` ISO, `daysLeft`), and the pure `trialEndsAt` (local midnight 30 calendar days after the start day), `touchTrial` (starts the clock, only moves `lastSeenAt` forward), `appAccessFor` (license wins; the clock is max(now, lastSeenAt), so a clock turned back gains nothing), `canWrite` (null = writable until main answers). Main: `src/main/account/appAccess.ts` `AppAccessService` (built in `index.ts` after the account; `licensed` = `account.supporter().licensed`, i.e. the F-15.9 token; caches the state, recomputes on its `Schedule` at most hourly and exactly at the trial's end, and on `refresh()`, which `onSupporterChange` and the `account:refreshSupporter` handler call; pushes `app:accessChanged` on change; `writable()`, `assertWritable()` → `AppError('VALIDATION', TRIAL_ENDED_MESSAGE, { code: 'TRIAL_ENDED' })`). The gate: `src/main/ipc/channelAccess.ts` `CHANNEL_ACCESS: Record<Channel, 'read' | 'write' | 'ai'>` (a new channel does not compile until classified; its test checks it equals `channels`); `registerHandlers` wraps `register` so every `write` channel asserts first; `ai` channels pass and the request path refuses instead (`providerFor` in `handlers.ts` throws `AiTrialEndedError`, code `TRIAL_ENDED`, before any text is built; the summary backfill and the voice job's `providerReady` check `access.writable()`), so AI failures stay data. `read` includes export, compile, provenance/tag/entity export, backups and restore, the account and purchase, app settings, and the project's view preferences (layout, session, focus, editor and AI settings, goals, backgrounds, reference pins, conversations). Channel `app:getAccess`, event `app:accessChanged`, `AiErrorCode` `TRIAL_ENDED` (in the index queue's hard codes and VibeWrite's turn-off codes). Renderer: `features/account/appAccessStore.ts` (`useAppAccessStore`, `useCanWrite`; loaded and subscribed once by `App`), `TrialBanner.tsx` under the header (`read-only-banner` role alert after the trial, `trial-banner` in the last `TRIAL_BANNER_DAYS` 7, `trial-buy` opens Settings › Account), `DocumentEditor` and `NotesEditor` add `useCanWrite()` to `editable`; the Account tab's license section (`LicenseSection`, region "MythScribe license", test ids still `account-supporter-*`, `account-trial-status`) replaces the Supporter section. Known gaps: other editable surfaces (sheets, metadata fields, tree rename) stay interactive and their saves fail with the trial message; the e2e has no read-only step (the smoke run holds a license), so the manual check is a scratch Playwright script on the built app: write `trial: { startedAt: now − 40 days, lastSeenAt: now }` into the userData `app-state.json`, launch, and expect `read-only-banner`, a `contenteditable=false` scene, `document:save` refused, `backups:now` ok. With the placeholder `LICENSE_PUBLIC_KEY_JWK` no build verifies a license, so every install turns read-only 30 days after its first launch of this version until the operator steps are done.
- Themes (F-7.8): `src/shared/themes.ts` owns the ids (`dark | light | high-contrast | sepia`), `BUILT_IN_THEMES` (label, `scheme`, `supporter`, the eight `THEME_TOKENS` hexes, preview accent), `CustomTheme` (`custom-<12 hex>`, name ≤ 40, `base`, all eight colours), `CUSTOM_THEMES_MAX` 8, and the pure `resolveTheme(settings, licensed)` / `availableThemes` / `nextTheme` / `themeNeedsLicense` / `themeBackground`. Gating (decided 2026-10-05): Dark, Light, High contrast free; Sepia and custom themes are Supporter extras; a locked or missing choice stays stored and paints Dark, like the accent. Persisted in `ViewSettings.theme` / `customThemes` (both `.catch` to Dark / `[]`, so a bad hand edit never resets the whole file). Channels `view:setTheme`, `view:saveCustomTheme` (creates or replaces, then selects it), `view:deleteCustomTheme` (current one falls back to its base; allowed unlicensed); handlers check `account.supporter().licensed` and call `setBackgroundColor` on every window (`ClosableWindow` grew it); `index.ts` creates the window with `themeBackground(view)`. Painting: `tokens.css` holds one `:root[data-theme=…]` block per built-in (dark is `:root`); the eight hexes are duplicated in `BUILT_IN_THEMES` and `themes.test.ts` compares them, so change both. `App.tsx` sets `data-theme` (absent for dark), `data-scheme`, `color-scheme`, and for a custom theme the eight variables inline on `<html>`. Light-scheme themes get darker Supporter accents (`:root[data-scheme='light'][data-accent=…]`); High contrast keeps its yellow over any accent. `viewStore`: `setTheme` (silent), `switchTheme` (View › Switch theme, toast `Theme: <label>`, cycles `nextTheme`), `saveCustomTheme`, `deleteCustomTheme`. UI: `shell/ThemePicker.tsx` (`appearance-theme-<id>` cards, `-new`/`-edit`/`-delete`, locked cards disabled) and `shell/ThemeEditor.tsx` (`theme-editor`) at the top of Settings › Appearance, with the `AccentPicker` moved there from the Account tab (test ids unchanged). Known gaps: the shell paints the theme after `view:get` answers, so a non-dark theme can show one dark frame at launch (the window background already matches); an accent error lands in `supporterError`, shown only on the Account tab.
- Page edges (F-7.11): the third app-wide view setting, `ViewSettings.pageEdges` (default `true`; an app-state.json from before it parses as on). Main persists it through `view:setPageEdges { on }` (answers the `ViewSettings`) and does nothing else with it; the renderer applies it. `viewStore`: `setPageEdges(on)` silent (Settings › Appearance checkbox `appearance-page-edges`), `togglePageEdges()` toasts `Page edges shown/hidden` (View › Page edges, `when: 'always'`, no chord, routed above the project gate in `runMenuAction`), `usePageEdges()`. Drawing: `editor/column.ts` `columnClass(sheet)` = `COLUMN` plus `SHEET` (`.ms-sheet` in `app.css`: `--ms-sheet` background, `--ms-line` hairline, soft shadow, 2 px radius) and `deskClass(sheet)` = `bg-desk px-4 py-6` for the scroll container; tokens `--ms-desk` (= `--ms-bg`, so Off is pixel-identical to before) and `--ms-sheet` (one step lighter, same hue; a theme sets both, sheet lighter). The column's own `px-6` is the margin, so toggling never moves the text. Single document: the `EditorContent` wrapper is the sheet, its scroll container the desk; `sheet = pageEdges && !focus` (focus mode keeps F-6.4's surface). Stacked view: the `<section>` of each `Region` is now the column (moved off the `h2` and the toolbar-less `EditorContent`, which renders `py-6` only) and becomes the sheet with `my-6`; the stack's scroll container is the desk (`bg-desk px-4`); separators stay on the desk between sheets; `EmptyFolder`, notes, and the Settings preview are untouched. The desk's side gap is 16 px on purpose: it comes out of the pane width, and the e2e's F-3.6 check (a 900 px setting yields a column wider than 700 px) has only ~742 px of pane at that point. e2e: the sheet class on the column, View › Page edges hides it with a toast and writes `pageEdges: false`, the Appearance checkbox restores it.
- Settings dialog (F-7.5): `src/renderer/features/shell/settingsDialogTabs.ts` is the tab registry (`SETTINGS_DIALOG_TABS`, Editor and AI (F-5.1) with `scope: 'project'`, Appearance (F-7.10), Account (F-15.2), Updates (F-15.7), and Diagnostics (F-15.8) with `scope: 'app'`; the tab strip wraps since F-7.10; `settingsTabsFor(format)` keeps only the app-wide tabs when no project is open, which is when the welcome screen's header button, Ctrl+,, and Tools › Settings open it; never a placeholder) and `SettingsDialog.tsx` the modal (role `dialog`, ARIA tablist like `SidebarTabs`, Escape/X/backdrop close, initial focus on the active tab, no Tab trap, same as `DialogHost`). It is not routed through `dialogStore` (whose modals are confirm/prompt-shaped). `SettingsButton` in `App.tsx` owns the open state, the Ctrl/Cmd+, `keydown` listener, and the dialog mount; it renders only while a project is open, so the shortcut cannot fire on the welcome screen. The active tab is local state, reset on open; a caller may name the tab to open on (`show('settings', tab)`, F-15.5). Fixed size since 2026-10-07 (decided by the author): `h-[min(640px,85vh)] w-[min(640px,90vw)]` on every tab (`data-testid="settings-dialog"`), only the tab panel scrolls; the e2e compares the bounding box on the Editor and AI tabs.
- Layout (F-7.2): `src/shared/layout.ts` owns the panel limits (`LAYOUT_LIMITS`), the zod `Layout` (`sidebar`/`notes` with `open` and `size` as fractions of the window width), `defaultLayout()`, and the clamps (`clampPanel`, `clampForEditorMin`, which keeps the editor at 30 % by clamping the dragged panel). App state stores it (`layout` field, defaulted so old files load); `layout:get`/`layout:set` read and write it. `src/renderer/features/shell/layoutStore.ts` is the renderer owner (loaded at app start, optimistic with one debounced write, registered with the pending-save registry; `resetLayoutStore()` in tests); `ResizeHandle.tsx` is the one drag handle (`role="separator"`, aria values in percent, px deltas that callers turn into fractions); widths render as `vw` so panels follow window resizes with no listener. The sidebar toggle lives in the top bar; the notes toggle in the editor toolbars. Since F-5.4, F-9.6, and the tags column (2026-10-06) there are five `LAYOUT_PANELS` (`sidebar`, `notes`, `tags` 15–35 % closed at 0.2, `assistant` 20–50 %, `references` 15–35 %, closed at 0.22 by default); `StoredLayout` defaults the later ones so an older app-state file loads. Five panels at their floors no longer leave the editor its 30 %, so `normalizeLayout(layout, keep)` first shrinks in the order assistant, references, tags, notes, sidebar and then closes in `CLOSE_ORDER` (tags, references, notes, assistant, sidebar), never `keep` (the panel being opened). The e2e reopens the tags column with `showTags()` where a step needs it. Dock (layout 3c): `src/shared/dock.ts` owns `DOCK_PANELS` (the five side panels plus `editor`), `defaultDock()`, `isValidDock`/`normalizeDock` (the contract refuses an invalid `layout.dock.columns`; `StoredLayout` repairs or defaults it, so old files keep their widths), and the pure moves `movePanel` (`beside` = new column, `stack` = into a column; never stacks the editor; returns the input itself for a no-op), `stepPanel` (menu Move left/right; skips columns whose panels are closed), and `dropTargetAt` (`DOCK_EDGE_PX` edge strips open a column, halves stack; the editor only takes beside drops). `layout.ts` counts the editor minimum per column: `columnWidth` (first open panel's size), `columnLimits` (tightest bounds of the open panels), `withColumnSize` (resizing sets every stacked panel), `clampForEditorMin` and `normalizeLayout` work on columns. The store adds `movePanel`/`stepPanel`/`resetLayout` and `isColumnShown`. Renderer: `features/shell/Dock.tsx` (`DockColumn` = width, border, handle on the edge facing the editor, labelled after its first open panel; `DockSlot` = drop zone, capture-phase listeners active only while `dockDragStore` holds a dragged panel, indicator `dock-drop-indicator`; `DockPanelControls` = grip `Move <Panel>` plus menu `<Panel> panel options` on the shared `ContextMenu`); panels render content only (their headers carry the controls) and `App.tsx` `ProjectScreen` maps the shown columns, one keyed list in and out of focus mode so the editor never remounts. Panel button names now overlap (`Move Assistant`), so e2e queries for the header toggles use `exact: true`. The references toggle (`ReferencesToggleButton`) sits in the top bar beside the assistant toggle; View › References (`toggleReferences`) leaves focus mode first, then toggles.
