- Resume where you left off (F-1.7): `src/shared/session.ts` owns `ProjectSession` (every field defaulted; `parseStoredSession` is lenient, cutting an over-long `positions` list; `withPosition` keeps the most recent `SESSION_POSITIONS_MAX` = 200 first), stored under the settings key `session` (`getProjectSession`/`setProjectSession` in `src/main/project/settingsStore.ts`; channels `session:get`/`session:set`). `src/renderer/features/project/sessionStore.ts` holds only what no other store owns (`positions`, `sceneDetailsOpen`, the one-shot `caretFocusId`) and snapshots the rest from its owners when it writes (tree `selectedId`/`collapsed`/`tagFilter`, entity `selectedId`, layout `sidebar.tab`, outline `folderView`, focus `active`). `App.tsx` calls `load(Promise.all([tree, tags, entities]))` on project open: it restores only ids that still exist, then subscribes to those stores (slice comparisons, so word counts are not moves) and registers its flush with the pending-save registry; `clear()` runs first on close so leaving focus mode and emptying the tree are not recorded. `DocumentEditor` (single-document view only) records the caret on `onSelectionUpdate`, restores it with `setTextSelection` once ready, and takes the focus (`focus(null, { scrollIntoView: false })`) when `takeCaretFocus(id)` says so; `editor/scrollMemory.ts` `useScrollMemory(ref, id, ready)` restores and records the scroller's `scrollTop` for the document and, keyed by folder id, for `StackedEditor` once every region has loaded. `NotesPanel`'s Scene details state moved into the session store. The e2e checks it across the F-7.9 relaunch (same scene, caret, folded folder, sidebar tab).
- `src/renderer/features/project/pendingSaves.ts` — the pending-save registry: `registerPendingSave(flush)` returns an unsubscribe; `flushPendingSaves()` runs every flusher and rethrows the first failure. The project store awaits it before create/open/close and in `closeWindow()`. F-3.2 autosave registers here.
- Window close and quit: with a project open, main prevents the window `close`, emits `window:close-requested`, the renderer flushes and invokes `window:close`, and main closes the project and windows. `manager.close()` runs on `will-quit` (not `before-quit`) so the database outlives the flush. A failed flush leaves the window open with a toast and invokes `window:close-cancelled`, which resets main's `quitRequested` so an abandoned Cmd+Q does not make the next plain window close quit the app (macOS; manual check only, the e2e does not drive Cmd+Q).
- Crash safety (F-8.3; no migration): WAL in `src/main/db/connection.ts`; close/quit flush above; `src/renderer/features/project/saveOnBlur.ts` `installSaveOnBlur()` (mounted in `App.tsx`) runs `flushPendingSaves` on window `blur` and on `visibilitychange` to hidden, toasting a failure. Recovery journal: `src/shared/recovery.ts` (`RECOVERY_KINDS` `document | notes`, `RECOVERY_STASH_MS` 250, `RecoveryItem`, `RecoveryRestored`); `src/main/project/recoveryJournal.ts` keeps `<project>/recovery/<kind>-<id>.json` (atomic through `writeTextAtomic`, no fsync — an app crash keeps the page cache; ids outside `[A-Za-z0-9-]` refused; malformed, misnamed, and `.tmp` files deleted on read). Channels `recovery:stash|clear|list|restore|discard`; `list`/`restore` drop entries whose node is gone or whose content equals the stored one, and `restore` saves documents through `saveFromEditor` in `handlers.ts` (the same path as `document:save`: word log, summary/mention queue). `createAutosaveStore` takes an optional `journal { stash, clear }`: a per-record trailing stash timer starts on the first edit, a save with nothing newer pending clears the entry and `reload` clears dropped drafts, both only for ids stashed this session (a save that beat the throttle sends no IPC); journal failures are swallowed (the real save reports disk trouble). Only the document and notes stores journal; scene metadata and entity drafts do not. Each stash sends the whole document JSON. `features/project/recovery.ts` `offerRecovery()` runs after `init` (project already current) and `open`, never `create`: Recover → flush, `recovery:restore`, `reload` the open editors, merge word counts, refresh goals; Discard… asks again (`danger`), and "Keep for later" leaves the journal for the next open. A dead renderer makes main close the project (`render-process-gone`), so the usual path is the prompt on reopen.
- Automatic backups (F-8.4; no migration, no dependency): `src/shared/backups.ts` owns `BACKUP_INTERVALS` (15/30/60/120 min), `BACKUP_KEEP_CHOICES` (5/10/20/50), `BackupSettings` (app-state `backups`, all defaulted: on, 30 min, on close, keep 10, `folder` null = default), `BackupSettingsPatch` (`folder` accepts only `null`: a path comes only from main's `backups:chooseFolder` dialog), `BackupEntry`, `BackupState`, the name helpers (`<Name> YYYY-MM-DD HHmmss.zip` in `<Name> (<id8>)/`; a renamed project keeps its subfolder by the id suffix). `src/main/backups/zip.ts` is our own stored/deflated zip writer/reader on `node:zlib` (no zip64, no encryption; size and CRC checked, inflate capped). `backupArchive.ts`: `createBackupArchive` (a `VACUUM INTO` snapshot as `project.db` plus the folder minus `-wal`/`-shm`, `recovery/`, `*.tmp`, and a backup folder nested inside the project; written to `.tmp` and renamed), `listBackups`/`pruneBackups` (only names matching the pattern), `extractBackup` (relative names only, 2 GB cap, SQLite header check, never into an existing folder). `backupService.ts` `BackupService` (built in `index.ts`; default folder `Documents/MythScribe Backups`, or `<userData>/backups` under `MYTHSCRIBE_USER_DATA`): change detection by `SELECT total_changes()` against a baseline taken on open (its `onChange` listener is registered after the main one in `handlers.ts`) and after each backup; the timer runs through the `Schedule` seam; `manager.onBeforeClose` (new; listener errors are logged and the close proceeds) runs the on-close backup. Everything is synchronous on main (fine at novel sizes; large image assets pause main briefly). Automatic failures are kept as `lastError`, pushed on `backups:changed`, and toasted once by `features/backups/backupStore.ts`. Channels `backups:get|setSettings|chooseFolder|now|reveal|restore`; `restore { file? }` takes only a listed backup of the open project (else NOT_FOUND) and extracts beside it, or with no file asks for a zip and a parent folder; the copy is `<Name> (restored YYYY-MM-DD HHmm).mythscribe` (` 2`, ` 3` on collision), opened through `manager.open` (migrations run), removed again if it fails to open. A restored copy keeps the project id, so its backups share the original's subfolder. Renderer: `BackupsSettingsTab.tsx` (app scope, after AI), `projectStore.restoreBackup(file?)` (flush first, no recovery offer: the journal is never in a backup).
- Drafts (F-8.5; migration 0014): text-only drafts. The tree, titles, notes, scene metadata, tags, and entities are shared; only the text of manuscript documents (`manuscriptDocuments` from `voice/profile.ts`) differs. Node ids never change, so everything keyed by node id keeps working. The active draft's text is the live `node.content` (no other read path changed); an inactive draft keeps `draft_text` rows (`draft_id`/`node_id` cascade, `content` null = empty, `word_count`). The one rule (`textOf`): a draft reads a document as its row, or as the live text when it has none, so a scene added after a draft was left reads the same there until edited there. The active id is the settings KV `ACTIVE_DRAFT_KEY` (`activeDraft`). `src/shared/drafts.ts` owns `DRAFT_NAME_MAX` (60), `FIRST_DRAFT_NAME`, `DraftName` (trimmed), `DraftInfo`/`DraftList`/`DiffSegment` (`same|add|del`)/`DraftDocDiff`/`DraftComparison`/`DraftChange { list, changed }`; `src/shared/textDiff.ts` (own code, no dependency) owns `diffWords(a, b)` (word+whitespace tokens, merged runs, `DIFF_MAX_EDITS` 2000) and `diffWordCount`. `src/main/draft/draftStore.ts`, one transaction each: `ensureDrafts` (creates "Draft 1", active, on first use; repairs a dangling active id), `listDrafts`, `switchDraft` (live text into the left draft's rows, the target's rows into live through `saveDocument`, target rows deleted), `duplicateDraft` (textOf the source; does not switch), `renameDraft`/`deleteDraft` (VALIDATION for a taken name case-insensitively, the active draft, the last draft), `compareDrafts` (plain text via `docToText`, tree order, ancestor `path`), `revertDocuments(fromId, nodeIds?)` (into the active draft only). Channels `drafts:list|switch|duplicate|rename|delete|compare|revert`; the switch and revert handlers call `documentsWritten` (summary/mention re-queue) and never `recordWriting` (not words written). Renderer: `features/drafts/draftStore.ts` (list, `activeId`, `busy`; every mutation flushes pending saves first, a failed flush aborts it; errors toast; `nextDraftName`, `draftNameProblem`), `DraftsDialog.tsx` (shell dialog `drafts`, Tools › Drafts…: list with Active marker and counts, Switch, Compare with current, Duplicate…, Rename…, Delete… with a danger confirm, disabled for the active one), `DraftCompare.tsx` (From/To pickers, per-scene inline diff in the `.rewrite-diff` styles with long unchanged runs collapsed by `diffContext.ts`, Revert this scene / Revert all with a confirm, offered only when one side is active), `DraftStatus.tsx` (`status-draft` in the status bar once there are two drafts; a click opens the dialog). Switch and revert refresh through `editor/rewrittenDocuments.ts` `refreshRewrittenDocuments(changed, { countsAsWriting: false })`, shared with crash recovery (which passes the default `true`); `false` uses `treeStore.rebaseWordCount`, which moves the session baseline with the count so the status bar's session delta does not jump. The list loads on project open and clears on close (`App.tsx`). Known gaps: formatting-only differences read as unchanged (compare and revert's change detection by plain text vs. stored JSON differ: revert copies any JSON difference); a manuscript document cannot leave the manuscript (moves stay within a section, deletes cascade its rows), so `draft_text` rows never go stale.
- Snapshots (F-8.6; migration 0015): text-only frozen copies of one document or of every document in the project (`projectDocuments` in `voice/profile.ts`: front, manuscript, end, in tree order; it shares `documentsInTreeOrder` with `manuscriptDocuments`). The text taken is the live `node.content` (the active draft's); `draft_name` records the active draft without creating one (`activeDraftName` in `draftStore.ts`, null before drafts exist). Tables `snapshot` (`kind` manual|milestone|auto, `scope` document|project, `node_id` cascade for a document snapshot, `note`, `created`) and `snapshot_text` (`snapshot_id`/`node_id` cascade, `content` null = empty, `word_count`); a deleted document drops out of every snapshot, a document snapshot goes with it. `src/shared/snapshots.ts` owns the limits (name 80, note 2000, `SNAPSHOT_AUTO_KEEP` 20), the input/output schemas, `defaultSnapshotName`, and `autoSnapshotName`. `src/main/snapshot/snapshotStore.ts`, one transaction each: `listSnapshots` (newest first, rowid tie-break; docCount/wordCount summed from `snapshot_text`, `nodeTitle` joined), `takeSnapshot` (document scope needs a document: NOT_FOUND/VALIDATION), `updateSnapshot` (`milestone: true` flags; `false` turns a milestone into manual, an auto stays auto), `deleteSnapshot`, `compareSnapshot(id, againstId?)` (docs either snapshot holds, a side without a copy reads current text; reuses `diffDocument` from `draftStore.ts`), `restoreSnapshot(id, nodeIds?)` (docs whose live JSON differs from the snapshot's row are first kept as an `auto` snapshot "Before restoring …" (document scope when one, else project), written through `writeDocumentText` in `documentStore.ts` (shared with draft switch/revert), then autos beyond 20 are pruned oldest first; nothing differs, no auto). Channels `snapshots:list|take|update|delete|compare|restore`; the restore handler calls `documentsWritten` for the changed ids and never `recordWriting`. Renderer: `features/snapshots/snapshotStore.ts` (list newest first, `busy`; take/update/remove/restore each flush pending saves first, a failed flush aborts; errors toast; restore refreshes through `refreshRewrittenDocuments(changed, { countsAsWriting: false })`; loads on project open, clears on close in `App.tsx`), `SnapshotsDialog.tsx` (shell dialog `snapshots`, Tools › Snapshots… after Drafts…: Take snapshot form with This document, the open document (tree selection of kind `document`, none while an entity page is open) or Whole project, name prefilled with `defaultSnapshotName`, note, Milestone; list filtered All / This document (document snapshots of the open one) / Milestones; rows with Milestone and Auto badges, scope, date, words, note; Compare with current, Restore… and Delete… behind danger confirms, Edit… inline, sending only changed fields so an unflagged auto stays auto), `SnapshotCompare.tsx` (snapshot picker and Against: Current text or another snapshot; per-document `drafts/DocDiff.tsx`, shared with `DraftCompare`; Restore this document / Restore all… only against the current text; asks again after a restore).
- `src/main/lifecycle.ts` — `installSingleInstance` requests Electron's single-instance lock and focuses the existing window on a second launch; `index.ts` quits when the lock is refused.
- `src/main/project/legacy.ts` — `isLegacyDatabase` opens a database read-only and detects the v0 layout (`documents` table, no `node` table); `locateProject` in `projectStore.ts` uses it so a v0 file is refused with a clear message instead of being migrated by accident.
- v0 import (F-1.6; no migration): `project:open` runs `locateProject` first; a `legacy` location asks `dialogs.confirmLegacyConversion(source, legacyBackupPath(...))` (a native message box, Convert/Cancel) and then `convertLegacyProject` before `manager.open`. `legacyImport.ts` reads the v0 database read-only (missing tables and later-v0 columns read as empty), builds the v1 project in a hidden sibling `.<name>.converting-<id>` with `createProject(..., { skeleton: false })` (sections only), and only then renames the original to `<Name> (v0 backup YYYY-MM-DD[ n]).mythscribe` (with `-wal`/`-shm` for a single file) and the new folder to the original path; a failed second rename restores the original. What comes across: the tree (`legacyTree.ts` `planLegacyTree`: section by v0 `section`, null to the manuscript, top-level `doc_type = 'note'` rows into an end-matter "Notes" folder, orphans and cycles to the top level, `novel` level to a plain folder, siblings by position then created, location/POV/timeline to `scene_meta`), content and notes (`legacySlate.ts` `slateToTiptap`: paragraph, heading 1–3, blockquote, sceneBreak, align; bold/italic/underline/strike/code; inline tag marks, sizes, colours dropped; unreadable values become plain paragraphs), `editor_*` settings field by field (`legacyEditorSettings`), tags (`legacyTagRecords` → `importTagBank`; `plot-thread` → `plotThread`, bad colours to the category default, parents by name) and their document links (`addDocumentTag`, non-taggable targets skipped), and `reference_docs` as blank-template entities (`slateToText`; a duplicate name is skipped). Left in the backup: scene summaries, background images, v0 tag templates, AI presets, per-document formatting presets. `manager.open` and `openProject` still refuse a v0 location (the startup reopen never converts silently). Test fixture: `legacyFixture.ts` (`writeV0Project`, v0's own schema) and the checked-in `e2e/fixtures/v0-project.mythscribe` made from it.
- `src/preload/index.ts` — exposes `window.mythscribe` (`invoke`, `on`), restricted to contract channels. Sandbox and context isolation are on.
- `src/renderer/lib/ipc.ts` — `ipc().invoke(channel, input)` typed client that validates outputs; `setIpcClient` for tests.
- `src/renderer/features/manuscript/` — `treeStore.ts` is the one owner of tree state: normalized `byId`/`childrenOf`/`rootIds`, derived `sectionOf` and `wordCountRollup` (rebuilt once per `load()` from `tree:list`), plus `selectedId` and in-memory `collapsed`. F-2.2–2.4 mutate it by updating the changed records, never by reloading the world. `ManuscriptTree.tsx` is an ARIA tree (roving tabindex, arrow keys); sections are labelled via `sectionLabel` and are not selectable; front/end-matter documents carry `data-matter` and the gold `text-matter` color. Per-level colors are tokens (`--ms-level-*`, `--ms-matter`); each theme (F-7.8) sets its own. `App.tsx` loads/clears the store on project open/close and renders the fixed-width sidebar + main pane (F-7.2 adds resizing).
- Tree mutations: main validates structure inside a transaction in `src/main/tree/treeStore.ts` (`createNode`, `renameNode`, `duplicateNode`, `deleteNode`, `moveNode`; section roots are refused with `VALIDATION`, sibling positions stay contiguous: gap-close the old siblings, then shift the new ones, then land the row) and returns the row(s); the renderer store merges the returned records with pure, tested helpers (`insertIntoIndex`, `duplicateIntoIndex`, `removeFromIndex`, `moveInIndex`, `planRemoval` for the post-delete selection fallback) instead of reloading; `expandAncestors` opens the path to a new node. The structural rule (what level fits under which parent) has one owner, `canPlaceLevel` in `src/shared/labels.ts`, used by main and by `resolveDropTarget`/`resolveMenuCreateTarget` in `placement.ts`. Flexible nesting (the author, 2026-10-07): upward only, a level sits at its own level or any higher one (scene under the root, a part, or a chapter; chapter under the root or a part; part under the root). Hierarchy consumers must not assume scene → chapter → part: `compiledBlocks` heads a scene whose parent is the root or a part with its title at chapter level (no chapter numbering exists; headings are node titles), export `title` blocks carry `inPart` so EPUB's contents keep a root-level chapter out of the part above it, a single scene exported alone prints no heading, `chapterGroups` lists chapter-level scenes, and the import commit keeps nodes the outline does not show (generic, or a loose scene/chapter) in front of their container's outline children when they were in front. `tree:move`'s `afterId` is tri-state: `undefined` appends, `null` inserts first, a string inserts after that sibling. Delete confirms through `dialogs.confirm` in `ManuscriptTree.tsx`. Drag and drop is native HTML5 (no dependency); the transient drag state and the indicator live in `ManuscriptTree.tsx`, not the store. In e2e, `locator.dragTo()` on an expanded folder row needs an explicit `sourcePosition` inside the header row, or it grabs the centre of the whole subtree. `placement.ts` is the one place that turns "selection + level" into `{ parentId, afterId }` (bottom bar, Insert menu/shortcuts, and the empty-folder invitation use `resolveCreateTarget`, which appends along the bottom of the outline and stops where the last leveled child cannot hold the level; the right-click menu uses `resolveMenuCreateTarget`, which creates right inside a clicked root, part, or chapter, via `createLevel(level, id, { direct: true })`). `contextMenuItems.ts` returns plain `{ id, label }` data so F-7.1 can render the same items natively; front/end matter rows get the section's template items (`template:<id>`, parsed back with `templateIdOf`, never cast) after the generic pair (F-2.6). `src/shared/matterTemplates.ts` is the one owner of the 14 templates (Tiptap JSON inside the editor schema); `tree:create` takes an optional `template`, main fills title, content, `matterType`, and `wordCount` and refuses a template outside its section; the renderer's `createFromTemplate` selects the new document without inline rename and adds its words to every ancestor rollup. Default titles come from `defaultNodeTitle` in `src/shared/labels.ts`.
- `src/renderer/features/editor/` (F-3.1) — `extensions.ts` is the one owner of the document schema: `buildExtensions({ sceneBreak, onSave })` returns StarterKit trimmed to the spec (no lists, links, code blocks, horizontal rules, or trailing node; the trailing node made a freshly applied heading read inactive after select-all), `TextAlign` on headings and paragraphs, and the custom `SceneBreak` atom block (`<div data-scene-break>` with the text as an extension option, not stored in the node, so restyling applies to old breaks; `insertSceneBreak` opens a paragraph after it). `documentStore.ts` owns the loaded document (`id`, `content`, `dirty`; generation guard) and autosave (F-3.2): `edit(json)` records the latest editor state and restarts a 1 s debounce; `flush()` writes the pending job through `document:save`, waits for any write already on the wire (writes are serialized, so close/quit cannot outrun a save that already left), keeps the job for retry on failure unless a newer edit replaced it, and merges the returned word count into the tree via `useTreeStore.setWordCount` (delta walk up the ancestors, no reload); `saveNow()` is Ctrl+S (the `SaveShortcut` extension, `Mod-s`); `load(id)` flushes the outgoing document without waiting and registers the flusher with the pending-save registry once, `clear()` unregisters. Pending jobs (a map keyed by document id, so a failed save for one document survives edits to another), the timer, and the in-flight promise are module state; tests must call `resetDocumentStore()` in `beforeEach`. `src/shared/wordCount.ts` is the one word counter (main caches it on `node.word_count`).
- Stacked editing (F-3.8, F-2.5): `documentStore.ts` holds every loaded document in `docs: Record<id, { content, dirty }>` with `load(id)`/`unload(id)`/`edit(id, json)`, per-id debounce timers and load tokens, and one serialized write queue (`drain`). `DocumentEditor.tsx` is the region: it owns the load/unload effect, the Tiptap instance, an optional own toolbar, and an `onFocus(editor)` callback; `EditorPane.tsx` is the single-document wrapper. `StackedEditor.tsx` renders `descendantDocuments(index, folderId)` (pure, in `treeStore.ts`) as `<section aria-label={title}>` regions with one shared `Toolbar` targeting the last-focused region (tracked as `{ id, editor }` and dropped when the region leaves the stack); the separator is decided per folder from `sectionOf` (scene-break text for manuscript, `<hr>` for matter). The empty-folder invitation creates through `createLevel`/`createGeneric` with `{ keepSelection: true }` so the stack stays. `App.tsx` `MainPane` renders `StackedEditor` for folders and `EditorPane` for documents. The book-like column (F-3.4) and formatting (F-3.6) have one owner, `column.ts`: `COLUMN` is the utility string every editing surface uses and `editorStyle(settings)` sets the `--ms-editor-*` variables (max width, font size, line height, paragraph spacing, first-line indent; token fallbacks in `tokens.css`, consumed by `.ms-editor` rules in `app.css`) on the pane; this is the one sanctioned inline style in the editor. Settings: `src/main/project/settingsStore.ts` reads/upserts the `editor` row (falls back to `defaultEditorSettings(format)` on a missing or invalid row); `src/renderer/features/editor/settingsStore.ts` is the renderer owner (`load` with the tree on project open, `update(patch)` optimistic with one 150 ms debounced `editorSettings:set`, revert and toast on failure, registered with the pending-save registry, `clear` on close; `useEditorSettings(format)` falls back to the format default; tests call `resetEditorSettingsStore()`). `EditorSettingsTab.tsx` hosts the controls plus a live preview (a static sample styled with `editorStyle`, `.ms-editor.ms-preview` drops the editor's min-height) inside the Settings dialog (F-7.5). Changing the scene-break text rebuilds the editor instance (the text is an extension option); `documentStore.edit()` keeps `docs[id].content` at the latest JSON so the rebuild starts from unsaved typing. The empty state (F-3.5) is the `empty-state` block in `App.tsx`. Word counts (F-3.3): `StatusBar.tsx` is presentational (`status-words`, `status-delta` test ids; formatters in `wordFormat.ts`); `DocumentStatusBar` inside `DocumentEditor.tsx` counts the editor live with `useLiveWordCount` (a `useSyncExternalStore` on Tiptap's `update` event, snapshot cached by document identity so caret moves never recount; saved count while loading), the stack shows the folder's `wordCountRollup` with no delta (folder rollups move with structure); the session delta comes from `sessionBaseline` in the tree store (a copy of the rollup taken in `load()`, cleared in `clear()`; `sessionDelta(state, id)`), so nodes created this session count as all new. `Toolbar.tsx` snapshots its `editor` prop inside `useEditorState`'s selector because Tiptap 3 does not refresh the selector when the editor prop changes. `EditorPane.tsx` loads by id and mounts a fresh editor instance per document, keyed by id and load state and constructed with the content, so loading never enters the undo history and undo cannot cross documents; while loading the editor is read-only and the toolbar gets `editor={null}`. `Toolbar.tsx` reads active state through `useEditorState` and exposes it as `aria-pressed`. `src/shared/tiptap.ts` holds the recursive zod `TiptapNode` schema and `EMPTY_DOC` for main, renderer, and later importers. jsdom lacks what ProseMirror needs to mount, so `src/renderer/test/setup.ts` polyfills `Range.getClientRects`/`getBoundingClientRect` and `document.elementFromPoint` once for every renderer test.
- Compiled preview (F-3.12): no migration, no AI. `src/shared/compile.ts` owns zod `CompiledSceneMeta` (`SceneMeta` picked to `{ location, pov, timeline }`, trimmed; `compiledSceneMeta()` answers null when all are empty, so the brief never crosses IPC), `CompiledTag { id, name, color }`, `CompiledEntry { id, kind, level, depth, title, meta, tags, content }`, `CompiledManuscript { entries }`. Main `src/main/document/compileStore.ts` `compileManuscript(db)`: `listNodes` once, a preorder walk of the manuscript root's descendants by position, `documentJson` (unreadable = null), `parseStoredSceneMeta`; tags from `listAllLinkedTags` in `documentTagStore.ts` (one query, `{ id, name, color }` per node; `listAllDocumentTags`, the F-5.7 ranker's read, now derives from it). Channel `manuscript:compile`. Renderer `src/renderer/features/compile/`: `compiledBlocks.ts` (pure reading-order rules: heading / meta / break / text; a generic folder prints nothing) and `CompileDialog.tsx` (shell dialog `compile`, View › `openCompile` `Compiled preview`, built on `StatsFrame`; flushes the document and scene-meta stores, then asks once; loading, error `role="alert"`, empty `Nothing to compile yet.`; text is `<article class="ms-editor ms-preview">` with `editorStyle(settings)`, each document rendered by `Node.fromJSON(manuscriptSchema(sceneBreak))` + `DOMSerializer` into `replaceChildren`, no innerHTML, a document that fails to parse renders nothing). `manuscriptSchema` in `extensions.ts` is `getSchema(buildExtensions(...))` with an inline-tag node id, schema only, so `inlineTag` and `aiOrigin` parse and no plugin runs. Test ids `compile-preview`, `compile-heading` (`data-level`), `compile-scene-meta`, `compile-scene-break`, `compile-text`. Known gaps: not live while open; inline tag colours are read when it renders.
- Manuscript export (F-12.1): no migration, no AI, no new dependency. `src/shared/bookExport.ts` owns `ExportFormat` (`pdf | docx | epub | md`, `EXPORT_EXTENSIONS`, `EXPORT_FORMAT_LABELS`), `ExportScope` (`manuscript` | `chapters { ids }` | `document { id }`), `ExportFormatting` (font serif/sans, size 9–16, spacing 1/1.5/2, letter/A4, `chapterNewPage`, `indentParagraphs`, `sceneBreak`), `ExportOptions`, `ExportProgress` (`collect | render | write`, done/total), `ExportResult`. `compiledBlocks` moved to `src/shared/compiledBlocks.ts` so the preview (F-3.12) and the export share the reading-order rules; `compileStore.ts` grew `compileSection(db, section, { only })` (a chosen node keeps its ancestors and descendants; `compileManuscript` wraps it). Main `src/main/export/`: `collect.ts` `collectBook` (matter units = the top-level children of front/end, text only, empty dropped; NOT_FOUND / VALIDATION for a missing or folder document; `Nothing to export.`), `model.ts` (Tiptap → `BookUnit`s of title/paragraph/heading/quote/sceneBreak blocks; `startsPage` is the one page-break rule; aiOrigin and the import origin are dropped, inline tags print their name), `xml.ts` (escaping, XML-1.0-invalid characters stripped), pure renderers `markdown.ts`, `html.ts` (print page + EPUB XHTML), `docx.ts` (hand-written OOXML), `epub.ts` (EPUB 3, `mimetype` first and stored), `pdf.ts` (hidden sandboxed window with JS off over a temp file, removed in `finally`), `run.ts` `exportBook` (renderPdf injected for tests); `fs.ts` `writeBufferAtomic`. Handler `export:run` picks `<project>.<ext>` beside the project folder via `chooseExportPath`, then collects (so `Nothing to export.` comes after the save dialog). Renderer `src/renderer/features/export/`: `exportStore.ts` (choices in memory per session, `chapterGroups`, `buildExportOptions`, flush then run with a `requestId`-filtered progress subscription; cleared on project close), `ExportDialog.tsx` on `StatsFrame`, which now takes an `action` (the primary button beside a secondary Close). `useOpenDocument` moved to `features/manuscript/` (snapshots and export share it). Since Compile v2 CV2 (2026-10-07) `model.ts`, `html.ts`, and the `BookUnit` writers are gone: the dialog's options become a compile format (`exportDialogFormat`) and print through the CV2 writers (next bullets), and PDF is laid out by Paged.js with script on in a locked-down window. Since CV3 (2026-10-07) the dialog, `exportStore.ts`, `exportDialogFormat`, `exportBook`, `export:run`, and every `bookExport.ts` export but `ExportScope` and `ExportProgress` are gone too: the compile window replaced them (File › Export… opens it).
- Compile v2 model (F-12.4, CV1): no migration. `src/shared/compileFormat.ts` owns the format: `CompileOutput` (`pdf docx epub rtf odt html txt md`, labels, extensions, `FINAL_OUTPUTS` pdf/epub), `BookFont` (`BOOK_FONT_INFO` family names; files ship in CV2), `PageSetup` (`PAGE_SIZE_INFO` in inches, `pageDimensions`), `Typography`, `SectionLayout` per `SectionLevel` (`part chapter chapterScene scene`), `Separator`, `HeadersFooters` (recto/verso slots, `facing`, `hideOnOpeners`), `MatterSettings`, `ContentSettings`, `Replacement` (regex refined), `MetadataSettings`, `CompileFormat` (version 1), `BUILTIN_COMPILE_FORMATS` (ids `builtin:*`), library helpers (`findCompileFormat`, `sortFormats`, `formatNameTaken`, `copyName`, `duplicateFormat`, lenient `CompileFormatLibrary`), and the per-project `CompileProjectState` (settings key `compile.state`: formatId, output, scope, `excluded` ids; `setIncluded`). `src/shared/bookDetails.ts`: `BookDetails` (settings key `bookDetails`, every field defaulted, cover in `assets/covers/`), `bookSurname`, `bookTitle`, `isbnFor`. `src/shared/compileModel.ts`: `CompileSource` (front/manuscript/end `CompileNode`s = `CompiledEntry` + synopsis + notes), `compileBook(input) → CompiledBook { output, format, metadata, items, toc, words }` with `BookItem`s (`page` generated, `matter`, `section` with resolved `heading`/`layout`/`break`/`inPart`/`runningHead`, `separator`, `text` of `ContentBlock`s, `synopsis`, `note`), plus `selectEntries`, `contentBlocks`, `applyOpenings`, `compileReplacements`, `sectionHeading`, number formatting, `furnitureSegments`/`fillFurniture`/`furnitureValues`, `approximateWords`. Main: `compileStore.ts` `compileSource(db)` (one tree and tag read; `compileSection` lost its `only` option to `selectEntries`), `settingsStore.ts` `get/setBookDetails`, `get/setCompileState`, `appStateStore.ts` `compileFormats`, `export/formatLibrary.ts` (`createFormat`, `saveFormat`, `deleteFormat`: built-ins read-only, names unique ignoring case, `COMPILE_FORMATS_MAX`), handlers for the twelve channels (cover via `chooseEntityImage` + `addImageAsset('covers')`). The compile window (CV3) is the only caller in the renderer. Known gaps: replacements run per text run (a match split across marks is missed).
- Compile v2 writers (F-12.4, CV2): no migration; one new dev dependency (`pagedjs` 0.4.3, MIT, its polyfill text bundled into main via `?raw`). Shared: `bookFonts.ts` (the bundled faces per `BookFont` under `resources/fonts/<dir>/` with `OFL.txt`; Fontsource 5.3.0 Latin + Latin Extended WOFF2 by `unicode-range`, Liberation Serif 2.1.5 TTF; `fontFaceCss(fonts, urlFor)`, `fontStack`, `officeFontName`, `formatFonts`), `compilePages.ts` (`pageRuns(book)`: the one page-start rule for every paged writer and the preview — first item and every item with a break; front division bare; openers bare-first under `hideOnOpeners`; the body's first run restarts numbering and, with sides, goes recto; `itemBreak`, `hasSides`), `compileHtml.ts` (items → HTML for `print`/`web`/`epub` flavours, `bookCss` per format, `printDocument`, `webDocument`, `PAGED_FURNITURE_HANDLER`: after layout marks bare pages and writes the folio into `--ms-folio` and contents links, because Paged.js's `counter-reset: page` does not carry), `xmlEscape.ts` (moved from main). Main `src/main/export/`: `printPage.ts` (print HTML + `file://` fonts + CSP + Paged.js held back + handler), `pdf.ts` (`renderPdf(book)`: temp file, offscreen unthrottled sandboxed window in partition `mythscribe-print` that cancels non-file requests, `PagedPolyfill.preview()` with a 10-minute timeout, `printToPDF` with CSS page size; `bookFontsDir()` = `resources/fonts` in dev, `process.resourcesPath/fonts` packaged via electron-builder `extraResources`), `docx.ts` (a Word section per page run, deduplicated header/footer parts with PAGE fields, `titlePg` openers, `oddPage` rectos, `pgNumType` restart, mirror/gutter/evenAndOdd/hyphenation settings, level styles with outline levels, TOC field over bookmarks, comments, framed drop caps), `epub.ts` (files split at pages/matter/chapter-level or page-breaking sections; OPF with ISBN URN or UUID, subtitle, creator, series, subjects, cover-image; nav with nested toc and landmarks), `odt.ts` (named styles, mirrored page layout, Standard/Opener/Front masters, chapter field, annotations, drop caps), `rtf.ts` (direct formatting, `\sect` per run, annotations), `text.ts`, `markdown.ts`, `inlines.ts` (drop-cap split, small-caps lead, flat paragraphs), `collect.ts` (`compileProject`, `checkCompileScope`, `hasContent`), `run.ts` (`compileToFile`, `renderOutput`, `readCover`), `testBook.ts` (writer fixtures + `xmlError` via jsdom). Channel `compile:run { format, output, scope, requestId } → CompileRunResult | null` (read; file named after the book title beside the project folder; `export:progress`; counted as `export.run`). The 108-page sample lays out in ~2 s offscreen (105 s in a plain hidden window: rAF throttling). Known gaps: PDF hyphenation needs Chromium dictionaries Electron lacks on Windows/Linux; DOCX TOC has no page numbers until Word updates it; epubcheck not run (no Java).
- Compile window (F-12.4, CV3): renderer `src/renderer/features/compile/`. `compileWindowStore.ts` is the one owner of compiling: the project's compile state (format, output, quick pick, `excluded` ticks; `ensureState` once per project from `App.tsx`, every change written through `compileState:set` in order), My formats (`compileFormat:*`), the shown format `draft` (built-ins read-only; a library format's edits wait for Save/Revert and survive closing the window), the `compile:source` the preview reads (`open` flushes the document, scene metadata, and notes stores first), and the run (`compile:run`, `export:progress`; it awaits the pending state writes because main reads the stored ticks). `bookDetailsStore.ts` owns Book details (`bookDetails:*`). `CompileWindow.tsx` (shell dialog `compileWindow`: format list, Duplicate/Rename/Save/Revert/Delete, eight tabs from `FormatTabs.tsx`/`ContentsTab.tsx` built on `fields.tsx`, Compile for, Book details…), `BookDetailsDialog.tsx` (also shell dialog `bookDetails`; draft helpers in `bookDetailsDraft.ts`), `compileContents.ts` (quick-pick `chapterGroups`/`resolveScope`, `includeRows`, `previewStarts`/`previewBook` (about `PREVIEW_WORDS` from a run start), `formatProblem`, `replacementError`, `COMPILE_TABS`), `CompilePreview.tsx` + `pagedFrame.ts` (the print page with `fontFaceCss(formatFonts, bookFontUrl)` in a srcdoc iframe, Paged.js loaded as an app file via `?url` because the inherited CSP forbids inline script, `PagedConfig.auto=false`, `preview()`, then `furnishPagedPages` from `compileHtml.ts`, which `PAGED_FURNITURE_HANDLER` now carries as source; reflowable outputs show `webDocument`). Fonts reach the renderer on the asset scheme: `bookFontUrl` (`mythscribe-asset://book-fonts/<dir>/<file>`), main's `bookFontPathFor` (only files `bookFontFaces` lists) under `bookFontsDir()`, the scheme now `corsEnabled` and the answer `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`, `font-src mythscribe-asset:` in the renderer CSP. The binder's context menu adds a `menuitemcheckbox` "Include in compile" (`INCLUDE_ITEM_ID`, `MenuItem.checked`). Menu: `compileManuscript` (Compile…), `exportManuscript` (Export…, alias), `openBookDetails`. Known gaps: the preview's folios count from the window's first page; mirrored pages show one under another, not as spreads.
- Find and replace in a document (F-3.10): renderer only, no channel, no migration; the `MenuItemId` enum gained `findInDocument`/`replaceInDocument` (Edit › Find…, Replace…) and `APP_SHORTCUTS` `find` (Ctrl+F)/`replace` (Ctrl+H). `src/shared/findInDocument.ts` owns `FindOptions { query, matchCase, wholeWord, regex }`, `compileFind` (literal = F-10.2's `findInRun` exactly; regex = JS `RegExp` with `dgu` (+`i` unless Match case) over quote-straightened pattern and text, zero-length matches skipped, Whole word through `isWholeWordAt`, now exported from `replace.ts`; `Invalid pattern`/`Too long` errors) and `expandReplacement` (regex only: `$&`, `$1`–`$99`, `$<name>`, `$$`, captures sliced from the original text by the `d` indices so curly quotes survive). `features/editor/findReplace.ts` is the `FindReplace` extension (manuscript editors only, beside `NameCheck`): plugin state `{ options, matches, current, error, decorations }` (`findStateOf`), matches per run (adjacent text nodes of one textblock; a hard break or tag token ends a run, as in F-10.2), recomputed on every doc change with the current match kept at the mapped position; classes `find-match`/`find-match-current` (app.css, tints of `--ms-warning`). Commands `setFind(options|null, anchor?)` (current = first match at or after the anchor, scrolls, never moves the selection), `findStep(±1)` (wraps, selects the match), `replaceMatch` (caret after the replacement, next match current), `replaceAllMatches` (one transaction, last to first, one undo step); replacements are ordinary transactions, so autosave and counts follow. `findStore.ts` (`open`, `showReplace`, `query`, `replacement`, the three toggles, `focusTick`; `openFind(replace)` prefills a single-line selection, regex-escaped in regex mode; reset on project close) and `FindBar.tsx`, a right-aligned strip docked above the editor's toolbar in a column wrapper around `EditorPane`/`StackedEditor` in `App.tsx` (a floating panel covered the toolbar's AI buttons), so it hides no control, never spans the notes panel, and works in focus mode. It drives the *active* editor (`useActiveEditorStore`): the single document, or the stack region last focused; it clears the find on the editor it leaves and on close. Enter/Shift+Enter next/previous, Enter in Replace = Replace, Escape closes and refocuses the editor. `FindShortcuts` in `App.tsx` (capture phase, as `SearchShortcut`) and the menu both call `openFindInDocument` in `menuActions.ts`, which toasts `Open a document first.` with no active editor. Known gaps: notes editors have no find; no cap on highlighted matches (a regex `.` marks every character); a pathological regex can stall the renderer.
- Spellcheck (F-3.11): Chromium's spellcheck is on by Electron's default (no `spellcheck` key in `webPreferences`). `src/shared/dictionary.ts` owns `DICTIONARY_KEY` (`dictionary` settings row), `DictionaryWord` (trimmed, no whitespace, ≤ 64), `ProjectDictionary { words }`, the pure `addWord`/`removeWord` (case-sensitive, unique, sorted), and `MAX_SPELL_SUGGESTIONS` (5). `getProjectDictionary`/`setProjectDictionary` live in `settingsStore.ts` (no migration). `src/main/spellcheck/sessionDictionary.ts`: `createSessionDictionary(session, onError).sync(words)` makes the session's custom word list equal `words` (list, remove the extra, add the missing; calls serialized on one chain, later sync wins); it runs with the project's words in `manager.onChange` (`[]` on close), after every add/remove, and with `[]` at startup, because Electron keeps custom words in the profile, shared by every project (on macOS and Windows it also writes them to the OS dictionary while the project is open). Since F-3.14 the handlers sync `words ∪ story name words` through `syncSpelling` (next bullet), and `ProjectDictionary` also carries `notNames`. Channels `dictionary:get|add|remove` and `spellcheck:replace` (`webContents.replaceMisspelling` on the focused window); `src/main/spellcheck/contextMenu.ts` `spellMenuPayload` turns main's `context-menu` params into the `spellcheck:menu` event (editable target with a misspelled word only). Renderer: `features/editor/dictionaryStore.ts` (loaded and cleared with the project in `App.tsx`), `SpellcheckMenu.tsx` (mounted after `DialogHost` while a project is open; a capture-phase `contextmenu` listener remembers the position and the focused editable, the shared `ContextMenu` shows suggestions and `Add to project dictionary`, and the focus goes back to the editable before `spellcheck:replace`, which Chromium needs), and the `Project dictionary` section of `EditorSettingsTab.tsx`. The inline-tag token menu prevents default, so main never fires for a token. Known gaps: no language picker, no on/off switch, no spelling menu on the welcome screen; the e2e covers only the Settings path (Hunspell dictionaries download from the network), the live menu was checked by a scratch Playwright script on 2026-10-02.
- Story names in spellcheck (F-3.14): local only, no AI, no dial. `src/shared/storyNames.ts` owns `wordsIn` (words with ranges, possessive ending left out), `storyNameWords` (the words of entity and tag names as spelled, 2–64 letters, unique, sorted), `buildNameIndex { entityNames, nameTagNames, accepted }`, `editDistance` (bounded, a swap of neighbours counts one), and `nearName` (names of ≥ 4 letters; one letter away, two from 6 letters on; entity spelling wins over the tag's, a lower-cased tag word is capitalized to match the typed word). `dictionary.ts` adds `notNames` (lower-cased, in the same `dictionary` settings row, default `[]`), `addNotName`, and `spellcheckWords`. Main: `syncSpelling()` in `handlers.ts` syncs the session dictionary to the stored words plus the name words of every entity and tag, read on each call and never stored, so a renamed or deleted name leaves nothing behind; it runs on project change, `dictionary:add|remove`, every tag and entity create/update/delete/link/import, a template load, and an F-5.16 job that created an entity. A new write path for entities or tags must call it. Channel `dictionary:notName { word }`. The preload bridge has one non-channel method, `isWordMisspelled(word)` (`webFrame.isWordMisspelled`, synchronous; false until Hunspell has loaded; it does not consult the custom words, which is why known names are excluded by the index, not by it); the renderer reads it through `lib/spellcheck.ts` (`setSpellChecker` for tests). Renderer: `features/editor/nameCheck.ts` is the `NameCheck` extension (manuscript editors only): a whole-document rescan on every doc change, every caret move (the word the caret stands at the end of is still being typed and is skipped), and on a `refresh` meta transaction (dispatched when the entity, tag, or dictionary store changes identity; no autosave, no undo step), one shared index with a per-word memo, a decoration `.name-near-miss` with `spellcheck="false"` (drops Chromium's red squiggle, so main's `spellcheck:menu` never fires for it) for a word that is near a name and that the spellchecker calls misspelled; tags in the character, setting, and world-building categories are names, other tag names are only accepted words. Its `contextmenu` handler opens `useNameMenuStore`, which `SpellcheckMenu.tsx` renders (known spelling, `Not a name` → `dictionaryStore.dismissName`). Known gaps: no near-name underline until the Hunspell dictionary has downloaded (and none for a language without one); a word split by a mark change is skipped; dismissed words have no list in Settings; an entity's tag outlives the entity, so its lower-cased words stay accepted. The e2e asserts the session's word list (`spellcheckerWords()`), not the underline; the underline and menu were checked by a scratch Playwright script on 2026-10-02.
- Brand: the originals are `art/Full-Logo.png` (wordmark, transparent; navy "Myth" so light surfaces only) and `art/Small-Logo.png` (icon tile). Derived, committed assets: `build/icon.png` (512px, electron-builder generates the `.ico`/`.icns` from it at package time), `resources/icon.png` (the same tile, imported in `src/main/index.ts` with `?asset` as the Windows/Linux window icon), `resources/logo-full.png` (cropped wordmark), and `src/renderer/assets/logo-mark.png` (256px tile rendered by `Logo.tsx` as an `<img>`). Regenerate all four from `art/` if the originals change; do not hand-edit the derived files.
- `src/renderer/features/shell/dialogs/` — the dialog service: `dialogs.confirm`, `dialogs.prompt`, `toast.*`, rendered by `<DialogHost/>`.
- `src/renderer/features/project/projectStore.ts` — Zustand store for the open project; pattern for all renderer stores.
- `src/renderer/features/project/formats.ts` — the format catalogue (label, summary, structure) for Novel / Epic / Web novel; one owner for format copy, which F-1.3 seeding and F-1.5 labels must keep in sync.
- `src/renderer/styles/tokens.css` — every color/font/radius as `--ms-*` variables, mapped to Tailwind utilities in `app.css` via `@theme inline`. Components use utilities like `bg-surface`, `text-fg-muted`, `border-line`, `bg-accent`.
- Tests beside code as `*.test.ts(x)`; vitest runs `main` (node) and `renderer` (jsdom) projects. E2E in `e2e/` drives the built app; native dialogs are stubbed from the main process via `app.evaluate` (see the save-dialog stub in `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`) or bypassed by passing explicit paths through the bridge.


- Manuscript import (F-12.2): `src/shared/import.ts` is the one owner of the draft (`ImportFormat` `docx | md | txt`, `IMPORT_EXTENSIONS`/`importFormatOf`, `ImportPlacement` `manuscript | front | end` on chapters only, `ImportScene { id, title, excluded, paragraphs: TiptapNodeT[] }` → `ImportChapter` → `ImportPart` → `ImportDraft { source, parts, nextId }`, `IMPORT_TITLE_MAX` = `NODE_TITLE_MAX` (the contract cannot be imported there), `baseName`, `sceneFirstLine`, `sceneWords`, `sceneDocument`, `draftSummary` (new nodes only), and the combined outline (2026-10-06): optional `existing` on parts, chapters, and scenes (the node's own id; scenes carry their stored content as `paragraphs`), `absorbed` on scenes (existing scene ids merged into it), `ImportDraft.existing { parts, chapters, scenes }` (what main put in), and `existingChanges(draft)` → `{ deletedScenes, mergedScenes, deletedChapters, deletedParts }`, the one rule both the dialog's warning line and the commit's deletes use). Provenance: every imported paragraph carries `attrs.origin = 'imported'` (`PARAGRAPH_ORIGIN_ATTR`/`IMPORTED_ORIGIN` in `provenance.ts`); the editor keeps it through `OriginParagraph` in `extensions.ts` (StarterKit `paragraph: false` + `@tiptap/extension-paragraph`, rendered as `data-origin`, only the importer's value parses back). Main `src/main/import/`: `blocks.ts` (the reader model `heading | paragraph | blank | break`, `paragraphNode` sets the origin and merges runs, `isBreakGlyph` for `***`/`* * *`/`---`/`⁂`…, `blankRunsToBreaks` ≥ 2 blanks → one break), `docx.ts` (the only file importing `mammoth` 1.12.3; `convertToHtml` with `ignoreEmptyParagraphs: false` and images dropped) → `html.ts` `htmlToBlocks` (a tokenizer over mammoth's tag set, `em`/`strong` → marks, `br` → `hardBreak`, entities decoded; no DOM), `text.ts` (`readMarkdown` joins lines CommonMark-style and parses `*x*`/`**x**`; `readPlainText` joins only when `looksHardWrapped` (90th-percentile line ≤ 100 chars and a blank line exists), otherwise one line = one paragraph), `structure.ts` `buildDraft(blocks, { name, format, novelFormat })` (h1+h2 → part+chapter, one level → chapter; `Part/Book/Act N`, `Chapter N`, `12`, `IV.`, `Prologue/Epilogue/Interlude` lines ≤ 80 chars promote; breaks split scenes titled `Scene N`; `promoteTitleLines` turns a title-like line (`isTitleLine`: no ending punctuation, ≤ 8 words / 60 chars, all caps, Title Case, or ≤ 3 words) after a break or a part heading, with prose after it, into a chapter of that name (the file's first line only when the file has no chapter markers); no part heading → one part titled after the file; `guessPlacements` sends leading `title|copyright|dedication|…` chapters to front and trailing `acknowledg|about the author|…` to end unless every chapter matches; leading untitled content under 300 words → a `Front matter` chapter; ids `p1c2s3`), `read.ts` `readManuscript(path)` (VALIDATION for an unsupported extension, an unreadable file, no text), `existing.ts` `withExisting(db, draft)` (the project's part → chapter → scene nodes ahead of the imported parts, with `existing` ids; generic nodes are not shown) and `storedDocument(row)`; `commit.ts` `importDraft(db, format, draft)` → `{ rows, words, tagCandidates, rewritten, deleted, changedExisting }` (one transaction; without `draft.existing` new parts are appended after the manuscript root's children and nothing existing is touched; with it, the outline is laid out in the draft's order: existing nodes moved/renamed only when they differ, an existing scene's content rewritten only when its paragraphs differ from the stored ones or it absorbed others, the ids `existingChanges` names deleted, generic children of a deleted container moved to the end of the manuscript, every listed id re-checked against the db first (VALIDATION `The project changed since the import was opened.`), an existing chapter in matter refused; chapters, scenes with `content` + cached `wordCount`; a front/end chapter is one generic document under its section with scenes joined by `sceneBreak`; excluded subtrees and new parts left without manuscript chapters skipped; nothing to do → VALIDATION `Nothing selected to import.`). `createProject(…, { skeleton: false, fill })` runs `fill(db)` after seeding and removes the folder if it throws. `dialogs.chooseManuscriptFile()`; channels `import:open { path? }` → draft | null (with a project open the combined outline; without one a novel-labelled draft for import to start), `import:commit { draft }` → `{ nodes, words, tree, rewritten }` (the whole tree after the commit), and `import:createProject { draft, name, format, directory? }` → ProjectInfo | null (`chooseProjectSavePath` like `project:create`, `manager.create` with `skeleton: false` and the import as `fill`, then the shared `afterImport`: tag-candidate proposals, `documentsWritten` for rewritten scenes, `continuity:changed` for deleted ones, mentions, summaries) (handler tests in `handlers.test.ts`; the fixture `src/main/import/fixtures/sample.docx` was made once by a scratch jszip script). Renderer `src/renderer/features/import/`: `draftEdits.ts` pure edits (`renameNode`, `setPlacement` (refused for an existing chapter), `moveNode`, `nestChapter`, `moveScene`, `shiftNode` (Move up/down: siblings first, then across into the neighbouring container), `moveTo(id, targetId, before|after|into)` (drag and drop), `removeNodes`, `mergeScene`, `mergeScenes`/`mergeChapters` (reading order, the first keeps id and title; `absorb` records existing scenes), `mergeWithNext`, `splitScene` → `<title> (split)` with an id from `nextId`, `sceneIds`/`chapterIds`/`idsOfKind`; an impossible edit returns the same draft object), `importStore.ts` (`target: project | new` from `useProjectStore.current` at `open()`, which flushes pending saves first into a project; `projectName`/`projectFormat`; `selected` + `anchor` (Shift ranges within one level); `history` for `undo()` (100 steps, cleared when the AI pass applies); `edit()` prunes the selection and the pane on every change; `commit()` into a project rebuilds the tree index from `tree` (keeps collapsed state, selects the first created document), `refreshRewrittenDocuments` for rewritten ones, toasts `Imported N scenes (W words)`; into a new project `useProjectStore.createFromImport`, a cancelled save dialog keeps the review; `resetImportStore()`), `ImportDialog.tsx` (mounted in `App.tsx` while a draft is held; testids `import-dialog|question|summary|existing|selection|merge-selected|delete-selected|node[data-import-id][data-import-kind][data-import-new][data-drop]|select|rename|new-badge|placement|merge-next|delete|split|undo|commit|cancel|project-name|project-format`; HTML5 drag and drop on the rows; backdrop click does not close because the edits live nowhere else; Escape/Cancel discard; Ctrl+Z undoes outside text fields). File › Import manuscript… (`importManuscript`, `when: 'project'`) and the welcome screen's Import manuscript… button. e2e: import to start with a plain-text file (title lines become chapters, no skeleton), then a Markdown file into Smoke Novel through the stubbed open dialog → the combined outline (New rows) → merge/undo and delete/undo → rename → Import → the new part after the project's parts, `p[data-origin="imported"]`, italics kept.
- AI structure detection for import (F-12.3): `src/shared/importStructure.ts` is the one owner of the pass's shape: `IMPORT_CHUNK_WORDS` 2,500, `STRUCTURE_TOKENS_PER_WORD` 1.35, `STRUCTURE_CHUNK_OVERHEAD_TOKENS` 250, `STRUCTURE_OUT_TOKENS_PER_CHUNK` 200 (`estimateStructureCost(words, model)` → `{ chunks, tokensIn, tokensOut, costUsd, priced }` via `priceFor`; the dialog computes it in the renderer from the non-excluded words and the fast model of the project's source, recomputed on every edit while the offer is up), `STRUCTURE_PARAGRAPH_HEAD`/`TAIL` 300/150 (`shortenParagraph`: boundaries live at paragraph edges, so the middle of a long paragraph is not sent), `STRUCTURE_REASON_MAX` 120, `STRUCTURE_TAGS_MAX` 6, `STRUCTURE_TITLE_MAX` 60, `flattenDraft(draft)` (reading order, excluded nodes and empty scenes skipped; global `index`, `local`, `sceneStart`/`chapterStart`), `chunkParagraphs` (greedy by words, a paragraph is never split), the zod `StructureBreak { before, kind: chapter | scene, reason }`, `StructureScene { start, title | null, tags }`, `StructureSuggestions`, `ImportDetectProgress`, `ImportDetectResult` (success or `aiFailure` data), `PendingTagProposal`. `src/shared/import.ts` grew `ImportAiMarks { break, title, reason }` as `ai?` on scenes and chapters, `tags` (bank names, default `[]`) on scenes, and `isDefaultSceneTitle` (the literal `Scene <n>`, optional ` (split)`s: the only titles the AI may replace; an author's `Round 2` stays). Feature id `importStructure` (`AI_FEATURE_IDS`, budgets 6,000 in / 400 out, `AI_DATA_SHARING` at Suggest, a row on the site's docs page). **Not the F-5.13 queue**: `index_job` rows are keyed by node and resume after a crash; the draft has no nodes and nothing may be written before Import, so `src/main/ai/importStructure.ts` `detectImportStructure(db, deps, { draft, requestId, signal, onProgress })` is its own serial loop: `assertFeatureAllowed` → flatten, chunk → per chunk `runAiRequest` (`fast`, `json`, `contextHash` over the chunk text + sorted bank names, `requestId: <id>:c<n>`) → lenient item parse then clamps (outer shape strict → `AiFallbackError` PROVIDER) → drop breaks at 0, outside the chunk, or `scene` breaks already at a `sceneStart` (a `chapter` break at a scene start that is not a `chapterStart` is kept) → tags mapped by `toTagName` against the bank, unknown dropped → `createProposal` (`nodeId: null`, `content` = the raw answer) → `onProgress` → unref'd `JOB_MIN_INTERVAL_MS` wait; the parent `signal` aborts the chunk in flight and throws `AiCancelledError`; any throw settles the chunk proposals already created `rejected` (the failure branch carries no ids). Prompt `prompts/importStructure.v1.ts` (`IMPORT_STRUCTURE_RULES` first, then `Tag bank: …`, then `Paragraphs:` with `— chapter starts here: "…" —` / `— scene starts here: "…" —` markers and `[<global index>] <text>` lines; the eval fixture has a `fixture` and a `maxed` case, 4,178 of the 6,000 input tokens at a full chunk, scored live by `scoreStructure`). Handlers: `import:detectStructure` (parent `registerInflight(requestId)` so `ai:cancel { requestId }` stops the loop, `import:detectProgress` to every window, `releaseInflight` in `finally`, AI errors → `aiFailure` data), `import:commit` writes one pending `importStructure` proposal per created manuscript scene with tags (`ImportResult.tagCandidates`; `promptVersion` the catalogued current one, `model` the source's fast model or `'import'`), `proposal:pendingTags` (`listPendingProposals(db, feature, nodeId)`, newest; answers the row even with an empty list so the tag bar can settle a stale one; null = none pending). Renderer: `draftEdits.ts` `applyStructure(draft, suggestions) → { draft, added, titled }` (breaks highest-first: split the scene at `local`, a `chapter` break also splits the chapter after the previous scene, new nodes titled by `splitTitle` and marked `ai.break`; then a fresh flatten sets titles on `isDefaultSceneTitle` scenes (`ai.title`) and `tags`), `mergeChapter`, `rejectSuggestion` (scene → `mergeScene`, chapter → `mergeChapter`). `importStore.ts` `detect: DetectState | null` (`offer | running | done | failed | skipped`; `offerFor` sets it at `open()` only when `isFeatureAllowed(settings, 'importStructure')` and the draft has words), `startDetect` (under `useAiActivityStore().track`, the `import:detectProgress` subscription, applies the result, keeps `proposalIds`), `cancelDetect` (`ai:cancel`), `skipDetect`, `rejectSuggestion` (bumps `rejected`); edits are refused while running; `commit()` settles the chunk proposals `accepted` (or `acceptedPart` after a reject), dropping the draft settles them `rejected`, a CANCELLED reply returns to `offer`. `ImportDialog.tsx` `DetectPanel` (testids `import-detect`, `import-detect-skip`, `import-detect-estimate`, `import-detect-progress`, `import-detect-cancel`, `import-detect-cost`, `import-detect-error`), per-row `import-ai-badge` (reason in `title`) and `import-reject`. `TagsPanel.tsx` asks `proposal:pendingTags` once per document (`askedPending` ref; after the bank and the node's links are loaded) and seeds the F-4.7 `done` state with `fromImport: true` (`<model> · from import` instead of the cost line); unknown and linked names are dropped, an empty set settles the row `rejected` silently, and a live Recommend result for the node is never overwritten. e2e: the F-12.2 step raises the dial to Suggest, clicks Detect (fake server keys on `IMPORT_STRUCTURE_SENTINEL`: one break, one title, one tag), checks the badge, the summary, the cost line, imports 4 scenes, and accepts the pending chip on the first scene.

## Known gotchas

- e2e crash step (F-8.3): Playwright treats a crashed Electron page as dead for good (`page.reload` aborts; after a main-side reload every locator still says "Target crashed"). The step swaps in a fresh window instead: a one-shot `before-quit` guard, destroy the window, `app.emit('activate')` so main's `createWindow` builds a new one, and reassign `page`. Steps after it must use the new `page`.
- e2e race seen 2026-10-01 (F-9.6), twice in four runs: after the F-9.4 "jump to scene" row click, `editor.click()` + `End` could land while the jump was still settling, the name stayed selected, and the Backspaces ate the wrong text (`"… Th waited."`). The step now retries click + End until the selection is collapsed (`toPass`). Use the same guard after any `openMention`/`openPassage` jump before typing.
- e2e wizard, 2026-10-01 (F-10.2): the first step failed four times in one afternoon with the wizard already on step 2 (a key pressed on the real keyboard landed in the autofocused name field, so Next passed validation). The step now empties the field before testing the refusal. Later stray keys can still land elsewhere (`"Smoke Novelqic"`); rerun on an idle keyboard.
- `importStructure.test.ts` "splits a long draft into chunks…" failed once in a full run on 2026-10-01 (`expected 201 to be greater than or equal to 990`: the chunk spacing was not waited for) and passed on the rerun; likely another file's fake timers leaking across the shared worker. Root cause found 2026-10-08: the wall clock, not a leak (see the clock gotcha below).
- Unit-suite leak flake (2026-09-17 to 2026-10-01: the three `openScene` tests in `assistantStore.test.ts`, `EntityEditor.test.tsx` "lists the scenes the tag reaches…", stray `unexpected layout:set` and "That passage is no longer in the scene" toasts in `VoiceSection`/`backgroundStore`/`assistantStore` tests; red about one full run in three): hunted and fixed 2026-10-01. It was never one leak. The workers share modules across files (`isolate: false`), and three things crossed files: (1) `AssistantPanel.test.tsx` replaced the store's `openScene` action with a spy through `setState`, and no `reset…Store()` restores an action, so every later file in that worker called the spy (now restored in `afterEach`); (2) `EntityEditor.test.tsx` used `vi.mock` on `openPassage`, which does not reach an `EntityEditor` module another file already imported, so the real `openMention` ran, the mock saw no call, and a 3 s `editorFor` wait stayed open and toasted in the next file that registered `sc-1` (the mock is gone; `editorFor` now lives in `activeEditorStore.ts` and `resetActiveEditorStore()` gives up every open wait); (3) files that change the layout without `resetLayoutStore()` in `afterEach` left the 150 ms `layout:set` debounce pending. `src/renderer/test/setup.ts` now resets the layout store, the active editor store, and the toasts after every renderer test. Rules that follow: never `vi.mock` a renderer module (assert the real effect, or inject), never leave a replaced store action in place, and to hunt a new order-dependent failure log `process.pid` + `expect.getState().testPath` from the setup file, then replay that worker's file list with `maxWorkers: 1` and a fixed `sequence.sequencer`, and bisect by pairs (the 2026-10-01 hunt took one failing run to find each culprit). Full isolation is the other fix and was measured the same day: the whole suite goes from about 25 s to 64 s, so the shared workers stay. Still open from that hunt: `AuthorRulesSection.test.tsx` "adds a phrase…" and `EditorSettingsTab.test.tsx` "updates each numeric setting…" each failed once in ten back-to-back runs under an instrumented setup (a real-timer debounce firing mid-burst on a loaded machine, so one write became several); not seen in the ten clean runs after the fix. Seen again 2026-10-06: every test in `backgroundStore.test.ts` failed together in 2 of about 10 full runs (once with the e2e running alongside, once alone), green alone and on reruns.
- The e2e smoke test flaked once per full run on 2026-09-19 (during F-15.2), each time on a step far from the new one and green on a plain `npx playwright test` rerun: the F-14.10 rewrite step ("expected the paragraph to open with text") and the F-6.5 control-bar hover (`data-visible` stayed `false` after `page.mouse.move`). Both are timing under WSLg, not product bugs (on 2026-09-21, F-15.7, the control-bar hover failed two runs in a row, once in each direction, before a green third: the pointer moves race the fullscreen resize); rerun once before digging. Update 2026-09-19 (F-15.3): both steps failed again in three consecutive runs in one session, plus the wizard focus-theft gotcha above (`Smoke Novel?`), each time on a different step and never on the new one; the machine was focus-unstable after hours of e2e runs, and a `wsl --shutdown` is the fix before blaming the product. If the same step fails twice in a row on a fresh WSL, it is a real bug. Seen again in the 2026-09-22 audit: the F-6.5 hover, then the F-14.7 flagged ghost text (a 5 s wait on the idle timer plus two fake-server calls), while seven search agents were grepping the repo; green at once on the quiet machine. Do not run the e2e beside heavy background work. Update 2026-10-02 (F-3.14): 4 of 12 runs failed, at three steps that share one symptom, the caret or a DOM selection not being where the step put it after `editor.click()` + `Control+End`: the F-14.10 selection ("expected the paragraph to open with text", with the typewriter lines typed at the top of Scene 1; or the selection reading empty at ~3178) and the F-14.6 disclosure count (41 instead of 40 at ~2664, the ghost text accepted away from the document end). The name check was suspected and ruled out: the smoke text has no near miss, so its decoration set is `DecorationSet.empty` throughout and it never touches the DOM. Hunted 2026-10-08: see the ProseMirror focus-timer gotcha below.

- The e2e "flake" (2026-09-11/12: the wizard's name field gaining stray letters such as `Smoke Novelo`, `Smoke NovelaR`, `Smoke Novelul`, or shrinking after Back) was keyboard focus, not load: on WSLg the shown Electron window steals the desktop focus, so anything typed on the machine while the test runs lands in the test's inputs. Since 2026-09-12 main calls `win.showInactive()` when `NODE_ENV === 'test'` (the harness sets it), and the test passed back to back afterwards. If the wizard step ever fails again, keep `test-results/**/trace.zip`, check the recorded value for foreign characters, and do not add Playwright retries to hide it. `pgrep -f electron` matches the calling shell's own command line, so check `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` before concluding an instance is stale.
- Electron cannot open an X11 window on the dev machine (2026-09-17, during F-5.7): every launch, `npm run test:e2e`, `npm run dev`, and a three-line BrowserWindow app alike, dies with SIGTRAP right after the zygotes start, while `electron --version` works, `xset q` answers, and the `/tmp/.X11-unix/X0` and WSLg Wayland sockets exist. `--ozone-platform=wayland` keeps the process alive and Playwright reaches the DOM, but nothing paints, so every click times out on "visible, enabled and stable"; `ELECTRON_OZONE_PLATFORM_HINT=wayland`, `--disable-gpu`, and `--ozone-platform=x11` do not help. It is the WSLg session, not the repo (no dependency changed since the last green e2e on 2026-09-15). Fix (confirmed 2026-09-17): `wsl --shutdown` from Windows and reopen the terminal, then rerun the e2e; it went green at once. Do not add machine-specific launch switches to the spec.
- Test hygiene (2026-09-12): stores with a debounced write (editor settings, layout, the autosave instances) must be reset in `afterEach` as well as `beforeEach`, because a timer scheduled by a file's last test otherwise fires into the next file's IPC fake (reused workers, no per-file isolation) and shows up there as a stray toast such as `unexpected editorSettings:set`. Four files were missing it; if a similar stray toast appears, look for the file that scheduled the write, not the file that failed.
- `src/main/ai/importStructure.test.ts:326` (the chunk-spacing assertion, `expected 449 to be >= 990`) failed once in the full unit suite on 2026-10-03 (F-14.12) and passed alone and on the rerun: wall-clock timing under the parallel workers, not a leak. Fixed 2026-10-08 (wall clock; see the clock gotcha).
- The wall clock steps backwards on this machine (2026-10-08: journald logged "Time jumped backwards" 49 times in 34 h of WSL uptime; once `Date.now() - started` read -715 ms across one test). Never measure elapsed time, or tell an older file from a newer one, by `Date.now()` or mtimes, in tests or in product logic that gates a timer. Drive the timer instead (fake timers; `vi.waitFor` advances a fake clock itself, so yield on `setImmediate`), use `performance.now()` for intervals, and detect a freshly written file by `<path>#<inode>` (a write is a temp file renamed over its name). Fixed on that basis: `importStructure.test.ts` (chunk spacing now asserted on a faked `setTimeout`), the F-8.4 "Back up now" e2e step (mtime did not increase), and a product bug in `ghostTextController.ts`: the idle tick compared `Date.now()` with the last edit, read a pause shorter than its timer had waited, sent nothing, and left no timer, so the suggestion never came until the next keystroke. It now reads a monotonic `clock` (`performance.now()`) and an early tick reschedules itself for the rest of the wait.
- ProseMirror focus timer (the e2e caret flakes, hunted 2026-10-08): on gaining focus prosemirror-view (`handlers.focus`) schedules a 20 ms timer that writes its own selection back into the DOM unless it has already read the DOM's, so an `End`/`Control+End`, or a DOM selection set by `evaluate`, that lands inside those 20 ms is undone and the caret falls back to the click point (typed text mid-paragraph: the F-14.6 count 41 for 40, the typewriter lines at the top, the F-14.10 selection reading empty). In `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` never follow `editor.click()` with keys or a DOM selection directly: use `clickIntoEditor(locator)` (click, focused, then a page `setTimeout(20)`, which fires after ProseMirror's) or `caretToEnd(locator)` (that plus `Control+End`, checked with `caretAtEnd`). The F-6.5 hover reads the viewport through `fullscreenViewport()` (the renderer size once it matches main's content bounds; under Xvfb with no window manager fullscreen never resizes the window, so the sizes agree at once).
- Renderer test fakes must answer every debounced write the test triggers (2026-10-08, `TagsPanel.test.tsx` "the column follows the layout store…" failed in a full run): a layout toggle writes `layout:set` 150 ms later, a fake that throws `unexpected layout:set` makes the store revert the toggle, and a test slower than the debounce then sees its own click undone. `TagsPanel.test.tsx` and `NotesPanel.test.tsx` answer it now (`return input`), as `TagsTab.test.tsx` already did.
- The e2e ghost-text step (2026-09-15, seen twice on F-14.3): `editor.click()` lands the caret mid-paragraph and a bare `End` then goes to the end of the soft-wrapped visual line, so typed text and the ghost request land inside the paragraph and the second suggestion never shows. Use `Control+End` (the document end) before typing at the end of the scene; the F-14.7 and F-5.3 steps do now.
- Dev-machine note: projects created before F-1.3 (2026-09-10, e.g. `~/test.sqlite.mythscribe`) have no seeded sections, so `tree:list` returns nothing for them. They are throwaway; delete them rather than adding a repair path. F-2.1 must still render sanely for an empty tree.
